&\leq C(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}+||u^{k}||_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}}+||\eta^{n}||_{H^{3}})\left(\frac{1}{2}\|D_{t}v_{d}^{m}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{m}||_{H^{1}}^{2}\right)\\
&\quad+C\left(1+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}^{2}\right)(\|v_{d}^{m}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\ast}}^{2})+C[\partial_{t}F^{7}]_{\ell}^{2}\\
&\quad+C\left(1+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}^{2}\right)(\|F^{1}\|_{L^{q_{-}}}^{2}+\|F^{4}\|_{W^{1-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2}+\|F^{5}\|_{W^{1-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2})+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}\\
&\quad+\|D_{t}v_{l}^{k}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}}+\|v_{l}^{k}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{n}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{m}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\eta}^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|v_{l}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}.\end{aligned}

Note that the sequence
\{\|D_{t}v_{d}^{m}(0)\|_{H^{0}}\}
are uniformly bounded and
D_{t}v_{d}^{m}(0)\rightharpoonup D_{t}^{2}u(0)
strongly in
H^{0}
, as proved in Theorem
4.3
. Integrating both sides of equation (
4.43
) from
0
to
T
, we then employ Gronwall’s inequality, Hölder’s inequality, the smallness of
\mathfrak{K}(\eta)
and the initial data
\partial_{t}u(0)
to derive that

(4.44)

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}(\|D_{t}v_{d}^{m}\|_{0}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{m}\|_{1}^{2})+\|D_{t}v_{d}^{m}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2}+\|D_{t}v_{d}^{m}\|_{L^{2}H^{0}(\Sigma_{s})}^{2}+\|[D_{t}v_{d}^{m}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}\|_{L^{2}([0,T])}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\big\{(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3/2+\varepsilon_{-}/2}}+||u^{k}||_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{5}{2}}}+||\eta^{n}||_{L^{\infty}H^{3}})T\big\}\bigg(\sum_{j=0}^{2}\|D_{t}^{j}u(0)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\xi(0)\|_{H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})(0)\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\ast}}^{2}+(1+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3/2+\varepsilon_{-}/2}}^{2})\|v_{d}^{m}\|_{L_{t}^{2}\mathcal{H}^{1}}^{2}+\mathfrak{F}^{n}+\mathscr{S}^{n,k}\bigg),

where
\mathfrak{F}^{n}
is defined by replacing
\eta
with
\eta^{n}
in
\mathfrak{F}
, and

\displaystyle\mathscr{S}^{n,k}=

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\eta^{k}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{5/2}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{2}}^{2}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\eta}^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}\|v_{l}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|D_{t}v_{l}^{k}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{2}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{5}{2}}}+T\|v_{l}^{k}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{n}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{5}{2}}}+\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{k}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}.

Using the identity

v_{d}^{m}(t)=v_{d}^{m}(0)+\int_{0}^{t}\bigl(D_{t}v_{d}^{m}+R^{n}v_{d}^{m}\bigr)\,ds

together with Theorem
4.3
, we choose
T>0
sufficiently
small (specifically,
T\lesssim\min(\delta,\frac{1}{2})
) so that

C\Bigl(1+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}^{2}\Bigr)\|v_{d}^{m}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}^{2}\;\lesssim\;T\,\|\partial_{t}v_{d}^{m}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}^{2}
,
and hence can be absorbed into the left-hand side. Here we have also used the estimate
for the difference between
D_{t}v_{d}
and
\partial_{t}v_{d}
, which will be
established in Lemma
4.6
.
As a consequence, (
4.44
) can be rewritten in the form

(4.45)

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\bigl(\|\partial_{t}v_{d}^{m}\|_{0}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{m}\|_{1}^{2}\bigr)+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}^{m}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}^{m}\|_{L^{2}H^{0}(\Sigma_{s})}^{2}+\|[\partial_{t}v_{d}^{m}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}\|_{L^{2}([0,T])}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\;\exp\!\Bigl\{\bigl(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3/2+\varepsilon_{-}/2}}+\|u^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{5/2}}+\|\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3}}\bigr)T\Bigr\}\Bigg(\mathcal{E}(0)+\|(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})(0)\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{*}}^{2}+\mathfrak{F}^{n}+\mathscr{S}^{n,k}\Bigg).

This estimate provides a uniform energy–dissipation bound for

(\partial_{t}v_{d}^{m},\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{m})
on the time interval
[0,T]
.

Step 4 – Energy-Dissipation bound for
(v_{d}^{m},\xi_{d}^{m})

The regularity of these lower-order terms follows immediately from integrating their temporal derivatives. Using (
4.45
), we obtain the following estimate for
(v_{d}^{m},\xi_{d}^{m})

(4.46)

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}(\|v_{d}^{m}\|_{0}^{2}+\|\xi_{d}^{m}\|_{1}^{2})+\|v_{d}^{m}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}+\|v_{d}^{m}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{0}(\Sigma_{s})}^{2}+\|[v_{d}^{m}\cdot\mathcal{N}]_{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T])}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\{(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3/2+\varepsilon_{-}/2}}+||u_{1}^{k}||_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{5}{2}}}+||\eta^{n}||_{L^{\infty}H^{3}})T\}\Bigg(\mathcal{E}(0)+\|(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})(0)\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{*}}^{2}+\mathfrak{F}^{n}+\mathscr{S}^{n,k}\Bigg).

Step 5 – Passing to the Limit

We now use the energy estimates (
4.46
) and (
4.45
) to pass to the limit as
m\to\infty
, with
n
and
k
fixed, and
v_{l},\xi_{l}
are given.

According to Proposition
2.4
and energy estimates, the sequences
\{v_{d}^{m}\}
and
\{\partial_{t}v_{d}^{m}\}
are uniformly bounded both in
L^{2}H^{1}\cap L^{2}H^{0}(\Sigma_{s})\cap L^{\infty}H^{0}
.
\{\xi_{d}^{m}\}
and
\{\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{m}\}
are uniformly bounded in
L^{\infty}H^{1}
,
\{\partial_{t}\xi^{m}\}
,
\{[v_{d}^{m}\cdot\mathcal{N}]_{\ell}\}
and
\{[\partial_{t}v_{d}^{m}\cdot\mathcal{N}]_{\ell}\}
are uniformly bounded in
L^{2}([0,T])
. Up to the extraction of a subsequence, it holds that

(4.47)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} v_{d}^{m}\rightharpoonup v_{d}\ \text{weakly-}\ \text{in}\ L^{2}H^{1}\cap L^{2}H^{0}(\Sigma_{s}),\quad\partial_{t}v_{d}^{m}\rightharpoonup\partial_{t}v_{d}\ \text{weakly in}\ L^{2}H^{1}\cap L^{2}H^{0}(\Sigma_{s}),\\
v_{d}^{m}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\ast}}{{\rightharpoonup}}v_{d}\text{weakly-}\ast\ \text{in}\ L^{\infty}H^{0},\quad\partial_{t}v_{d}^{m}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\ast}}{{\rightharpoonup}}\partial_{t}v_{d}\ \text{weakly-}\ast\ \text{in}\ L^{\infty}H^{0},\\
\xi_{d}^{m}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\ast}}{{\rightharpoonup}}\xi_{d}\ \text{weakly-}\ast\ \text{in}\ L^{\infty}H^{1},\quad\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{m}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\ast}}{{\rightharpoonup}}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\ \text{weakly-}\ast\ \text{in}\ L^{\infty}H^{1},\\
[v_{d}^{m}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}\rightharpoonup[v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}\ \text{weakly-}\ \text{in}\ L^{2},\ [\partial_{t}v_{d}^{m}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}\rightharpoonup[\partial_{t}v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}\ \text{weakly in}\ L^{2}.\end{aligned}

By lower semicontinuity, the energy estimates (
4.45
) and (
4.46
) imply that

(4.48)

\displaystyle\|v_{d}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{0}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{0}}^{2}+\|v_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2}+\|v_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{0}(\Sigma_{s})}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{0}(\Sigma_{s})}^{2}+\|[v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|[\partial_{t}v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\xi_{d}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}

is bounded by the terms in (
4.46
) from second line to the last up to a universal constant.

Step 6 – Improved Bound for
\partial_{t}\xi_{d}

In this step, we fix
n,k
and the prescribed functions
\xi_{l},v_{l}
, and apply the functional calculus developed in
[
10
, Section 8]
to the modified gravity-capillary operator
\mathcal{K}_{k}
induced by the
(\cdot)_{1,\Sigma_{k}}
inner product defined by equation (
2.8
).

To be specific,
\mathcal{K}_{k}
is defined as follows:

\mathcal{K}_{k}\varphi:=g\varphi-\sigma\partial_{1}\Big(\frac{\partial_{1}\varphi}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big)-\sigma\partial_{1}\big(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})\partial_{1}\varphi\big).

Since
\eta^{k}
is smooth, we use the same argument as in
[
10
]
to develop the same results as in
[
10
]
for
\mathcal{K}_{k}
and
D_{j}^{s}
induced by
\mathcal{K}_{k}
.

Let’s first derive some preliminary results. Let the auxiliary function
\psi
satisfy

(4.49)

-\Delta\psi=0\quad\text{in}\ \Omega,\quad\partial_{\nu}\psi=(D_{j}^{s}(\partial_{t}\xi_{d}-a_{0}(t)\zeta_{0}))/|\mathcal{N}_{0}|\quad\text{on}\ \Sigma,\quad\partial_{\nu}\psi=0\quad\text{on}\ \Sigma_{s},

where
s=1-2\tilde{\alpha}\in[0,1)
for some
\tilde{\alpha}\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
to be determined,

\zeta_{0}
is the equilibrium state of the free surface, and
a_{0}(t)
is a function depending only on
t
such that:

(4.50)

\displaystyle{}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}-a_{0}(t)\zeta_{0}=0.

Using kinematic boundary condition (the fourth equation in system (
1.28
)), we have

(4.51)

\displaystyle{}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}=

\displaystyle\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}^{2}\xi_{l}^{k})+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(\int_{0}^{t}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d})+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}I^{n,k}_{2}+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(R^{n}v_{l}^{k})\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}.

Inserting (
4.51
) into (
4.50
) yields the explicit expression for
a_{0}(t)

(4.52)

\displaystyle{}\begin{aligned} a_{0}(t)=&\frac{\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}^{2}\xi_{l}^{k})+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(\int_{0}^{t}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d})+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}I^{n,k}_{2}+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(R^{n}v_{l}^{k})\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}}{\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\zeta_{0}}.\end{aligned}

Using (
4.52
), we have the following estimate using Sobolev embedding, Cauchy’s inequality and trace theorem

(4.53)

\displaystyle{}|a_{0}(t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\|v_{d}\|_{H^{1}}+t\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+t\|u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{H^{1}}.

Taking the temporal derivative of both sides of (
4.52
), we obtain the expression for
a_{0}^{\prime}(t)
:

(4.54)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} a_{0}^{\prime}(t)=&\frac{\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}v_{d}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{n}+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}}{\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\zeta_{0}}+\frac{\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}D_{t}(R^{n}v_{l}^{k})\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}}{\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\zeta_{0}},\end{aligned}

which leads to the following estimate:

(4.55)

\displaystyle{}\begin{aligned} |a^{\prime}(t)|\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|v_{d}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{H^{1}}+\|u^{k}\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|v_{d}^{k}\|_{H^{1}}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|D_{t}v_{l}^{k}\|_{H^{1}}.\end{aligned}

For the test function
\psi
defined in (
4.49
),
[
10
, Proposition 9.1]
provides the estimates

(4.56)

\|\psi\|_{H^{1}}\lesssim\|(\partial_{t}\xi_{d}-a_{0}(t)\zeta_{0})\|_{H^{s-1/2}},\ \|\psi\|_{H^{2}}\lesssim\|D_{j}^{s}(\partial_{t}\xi_{d}-a_{0}(t)\zeta_{0})\|_{H^{1/2}},\ \|\partial_{t}\psi\|_{H^{1}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}\xi_{d}-a_{0}(t)\zeta_{0})\|_{H^{s-1/2}}.

Inserting (
4.53
) and (
4.55
) into (
4.56
), we have

(4.57)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\psi\|_{H^{1}}\lesssim&\|(\partial_{t}\xi_{d})\|_{H^{s-1/2}}+\|v_{d}\|_{H^{1}}+t\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+t\|u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\\
&\quad+\|u(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{H^{1}},\\
\|\psi\|_{H^{2}}\lesssim&\|D_{j}^{s}(\partial_{t}\xi_{d})\|_{H^{1/2}}\|v_{d}\|_{H^{1}}+t\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+t\|u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\\
&\quad+\|u(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{H^{1}},\\
\|\partial_{t}\psi\|_{H^{1}}\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}\xi_{d})\|_{H^{s-1/2}}+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|v_{d}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{H^{1}}+\|u^{k}\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{H^{1}}\\
&+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|v_{d}^{k}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|D_{t}v_{l}^{k}\|_{H^{1}}.\end{aligned}

We now work to enhance the regularity of
\partial_{t}\xi_{d}
. Integrating both sides of (
4.18
) from
0
to
T
and using the convergence results from Step 5, we obtain

(4.58)

\displaystyle\left<\partial_{t}^{2}v_{d},w\right>_{\ast}+\int_{0}^{T}\Big[((\partial_{t}v_{d},w))+(\partial_{t}v_{d},R^{n}w)+((v_{d},R^{n}w))+(\partial_{t}\xi_{d},w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n})_{1,\Sigma_{k}}+[D_{t}v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n},w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}\Big]

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}[\partial_{t}F^{7},w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}+\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}v_{d},w\partial_{t}J^{n})_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\int_{0}^{T}\beta(\partial_{t}v_{d}\cdot\tau,(w\cdot\tau)\partial_{t}J^{n})

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\left[\partial_{t}F^{1}\cdot wJ^{n}+\partial_{t}J^{n}KF^{1}\cdot w\right]+\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}(R^{n}v_{l}),w)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}+\int_{0}^{T}((\partial_{t}(R^{n}v_{l}),w))+(((R^{n}v_{l}),R^{n}w))

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}((R^{n}v_{l}),R^{n}w)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}+\int_{0}^{T}((R^{n}v_{l}),w\partial_{t}J^{n}(J^{n})^{-1})_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}-\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Sigma_{s}}\beta(v_{d}\cdot\tau)(w\cdot\tau)\partial_{t}J

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}F^{4}\cdot w-\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Sigma_{s}}\left[\partial_{t}F^{5}w+\partial_{t}J^{n}K^{n}F^{5}w\right]\cdot\tau J^{n}

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\mu}{2}(\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{n}}v_{d}:\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}w+\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}v_{d}:\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{n}}w+\partial_{t}JK\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}v_{d}:\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}w)J^{n}-\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}J^{n}K^{n}\partial_{t}v_{d},w)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}_{T}}

\displaystyle+\big(\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{zz}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta^{k}),(w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n})\big)_{L^{2}}+[\mathcal{R}_{zz}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta^{k},w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell},

where

\langle\partial_{t}^{2}v_{d},w\rangle_{*}=(\partial_{t}v_{d},w)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}(T)-(\partial_{t}v_{d},w)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}(0)-\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}v_{d},\partial_{t}w)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}-\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}v_{d},\partial_{t}J^{n}w)_{L^{2}(\Omega)}
for any
w\in\mathcal{W}_{\sigma}
such that
\partial_{t}w\in\mathcal{H}^{0}
.

Set the test function to be
w=M^{n}\nabla\psi\in\mathcal{W}_{\sigma}
in (
4.58
) where
\psi
is defined by (
4.49
). Using the fact that
\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}M^{n}\psi=0
, we obtain that

(4.59)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}\xi_{d},D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\xi_{d})_{1,\Sigma_{k}}=I+II+III+IV+V,

where
I=\left<\partial_{t}^{2}v_{d},M^{n}\nabla\psi\right>_{*}
, and

\displaystyle II=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\left[((\partial_{t}v_{d},M^{n}\nabla\psi))+(\partial_{t}v_{d},R^{n}M^{n}\nabla\psi)+((v_{d},R^{n}M^{n}\nabla\psi))+[\partial_{t}v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n},M^{n}\nabla\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}\right]

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}\left[[\partial_{t}F^{7},M^{n}\nabla\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}+(\partial_{t}v_{d},M^{n}\nabla\psi\partial_{t}J^{n})_{L^{2}(\Omega)}+\beta(\partial_{t}v_{d}\cdot\tau,(M^{n}\nabla\psi\cdot\tau)\partial_{t}J^{n})\right],

\displaystyle III=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\left[\partial_{t}F^{1}\cdot M^{n}\nabla\psi+\partial_{t}J^{n}K^{n}F^{1}\cdot M^{n}\nabla\psi\right]J^{n}-\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}F^{4}\cdot M^{n}\nabla\psi

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Sigma_{s}}\left[\partial_{t}F^{5}M^{n}\nabla\psi+\partial_{t}J^{n}K^{n}F^{5}M^{n}\nabla\psi\right]\cdot\tau J^{n},

\displaystyle IV=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\mu}{2}(\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{n}}v_{d}:\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}M^{n}\nabla\psi+\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}v_{d}:\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{n}}M^{n}\nabla\psi+\partial_{t}J^{n}K^{n}\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}v_{d}:\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}M^{n}\nabla\psi)J^{n}

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}v_{d},R^{n}M^{n}\nabla\psi)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}-\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Sigma_{s}}\beta(v_{d}\cdot\tau)(M^{n}\nabla\psi\cdot\tau)\partial_{t}J^{n}-\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}J^{n}K^{n}\partial_{t}v_{d},M^{n}\nabla\psi)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}_{T}}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}\xi_{d},a_{0}(t)D_{j}^{s}\zeta_{0})_{1,\Sigma_{k}},

and

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} V=&\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}(R^{n}v_{l}),M^{n}\nabla\psi)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}+\int_{0}^{T}((R^{n}v_{l}),R^{n}M^{n}\nabla\psi)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}+\int_{0}^{T}(R^{n}v_{l},M^{n}\nabla\psi\partial_{t}J^{n}(J^{n})^{-1})_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}\\
&\quad+\int_{0}^{T}((\partial_{t}(R^{n}v_{l}),M^{n}\nabla\psi))+(((R^{n}v_{l}),R^{n}M^{n}\nabla\psi))\\
&\quad+\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{zz}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta^{k}),M^{n}\nabla\psi)_{L^{2}}+\int_{0}^{T}[\mathcal{R}_{zz}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta^{k},M^{n}\nabla\psi]_{\ell}.\end{aligned}

The left hand side of (
4.59
) can be rewritten as follows

(\partial_{t}\xi_{d},D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\xi_{d})_{1,\Sigma}=\|D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1-\frac{s}{2}}_{\mathcal{K}}}^{2}
.
Hence, it suffices to estimate each term from
I
to
V
.

To estimate
I
, we rewrite
I
by definition and estimate:

\displaystyle\left<\partial_{t}^{2}v_{d},M^{n}\nabla\psi\right>_{*}

\displaystyle=(\partial_{t}v_{d},M^{n}\nabla\psi)_{L^{2}(\Omega)}(T)-(\partial_{t}v_{d},M^{n}\nabla\psi)_{L^{2}(\Omega)}(0)-\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}v_{d},\partial_{t}(M^{n}\nabla\psi))_{L^{2}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\lesssim||\partial_{t}v_{d}||_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}||\psi||_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}||\partial_{t}v_{d}||_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}||\partial_{t}\psi||_{L^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim||\partial_{t}v_{d}||_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\Big(||(\partial_{t}\xi_{d})||_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{s-\frac{1}{2}}}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}||\partial_{t}^{2}\xi_{d}||_{L^{2}H^{s-\frac{1}{2}}}\Big)+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\Big(\|\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}\xi_{d})\|_{H^{s-1/2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|v_{d}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{H^{1}}+\|u^{k}\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{H^{1}}

(4.60)

\displaystyle\quad+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|v_{d}^{k}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|D_{t}v_{l}^{k}\|_{H^{1}}\Big).

Using the kinematic boundary condition and trace theorem, we have:

\displaystyle||\partial_{t}^{2}\xi_{d}||_{H^{s-\frac{1}{2}}}\lesssim

\displaystyle||(\partial_{t}v_{d})\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}||_{H^{s-\frac{1}{2}}}+\|(v_{d})\cdot\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{n}\|_{H^{s-\frac{1}{2}}}+||\partial_{t}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{l}^{k}||_{H^{s-\frac{1}{2}}}+||u_{1}^{k}\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{d}||_{H^{s-\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\|D_{t}(R^{n}v_{l}^{k})\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}\|_{H^{s-\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||\partial_{t}v_{d}||_{H^{1}}+\|v_{d}\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+||\partial_{t}u^{k}||_{W^{2,q_{-}}}||\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}||_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}

(4.61)

\displaystyle+||u_{1}^{k}||_{W^{2,q_{-}}}||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}||_{H^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}.

Plugging this inequality into (
4.60
) and using the fact that
s=1-2\tilde{\alpha}<1
, we have

(4.62)

\displaystyle\left<\partial_{t}^{2}v_{d},M^{n}\nabla\psi\right>_{*}

\displaystyle\lesssim||\partial_{t}v_{d}||_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}||_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}+||\partial_{t}v_{d}||_{H^{1}}+||\partial_{t}u^{k}||_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}||\partial_{t}\xi^{k}||_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}

\displaystyle+||u_{1}^{k}||_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}||_{L^{2}H^{s+\frac{1}{2}}}+\|v_{d}\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\Big(\|v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\Big(\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)\|_{H^{1}}\Big)+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}u\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{2}}\Big(\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|D_{t}v_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}\Big).

We now turn to II and estimate each term directly:

(4.63)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}((\partial_{t}v_{d},M^{n}\nabla\psi))\lesssim||\partial_{t}v_{d}||_{L^{2}H^{1}}||\psi||_{L^{2}H^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\ ||\partial_{t}v_{d}||_{L^{2}H^{1}}||D_{j}^{s}(\partial_{t}\xi_{d})||_{L^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}\Big(\|v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\Big),

(4.64)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}v_{d},R^{n}M^{n}\nabla\psi)\lesssim T||\partial_{t}v_{d}||_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}||\psi||_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\ T||\partial_{t}v_{d}||_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}||_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}\Big(\|v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\Big),

and

(4.65)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\int_{0}^{T}((v_{d},R^{n}M^{n}\nabla\psi))\lesssim\ T^{\frac{1}{2}}||v_{d}||_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}||D_{j}^{s}(\partial_{t}\xi_{d})||_{L^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\|v_{d}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\big(\|v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\\
&\qquad+\|u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\big),\\
&\int_{0}^{T}[\partial_{t}v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n},M^{n}\nabla\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}\lesssim\ ||[\partial_{t}v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}||_{L_{t}^{2}}||D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}||_{L^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}+}}+\|[\partial_{t}v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}\big(\|v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\\
&\qquad+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\big),\\
&\int_{0}^{T}[\partial_{t}F^{7},M^{n}\nabla\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}\lesssim\ ||[\partial_{t}F^{7}]||_{\ell}||D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}||_{L^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}+}}+\|[\partial_{t}F^{7}]_{\ell}\|_{L^{2}}\big(\|v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\\
&\qquad+\|u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\big),\\
&\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}v_{d},M^{n}\nabla\psi\partial_{t}J^{n})_{\mathcal{H}^{0}(\Sigma)}\lesssim\ T^{\frac{1}{2}}||\partial_{t}v_{d}||_{L^{\infty}L^{2}}||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}||_{L^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}\big(\|v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\\
&\qquad+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\big),\end{aligned}

as well as

(4.66)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\beta(\partial_{t}v_{d}\cdot\tau,(M\nabla\psi\cdot\tau)\partial_{t}J^{n})_{\mathcal{H}^{0}(\Sigma_{s})}=

\displaystyle\ 0,

where we used the fact that
\partial_{\nu}\psi=0
on
\Sigma_{s}
and (
4.55
).

For III, we note that it can be rewritten as:

(4.67)

\displaystyle III=\partial_{t}\mathcal{F}(M^{n}\nabla\psi)-\mathcal{F}(\partial_{t}(M^{n}\nabla\psi))+\mathcal{F}(R^{n}M^{n}\nabla\psi).

According to (
4.19
), for forcing terms
F^{j}
with
j=1,3,4,5
, we may apply the similar estimates as in the energy bound for
\partial_{t}v_{d}^{m}
in Step 3. In this way, we obtain

(4.68)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\mathcal{F}(M^{n}\nabla\psi)-\mathcal{F}(\partial_{t}(M^{n}\nabla\psi))+\mathcal{F}(R^{n}M^{n}\nabla\psi)

\displaystyle\lesssim\big[\|\partial_{t}(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\ast}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}(\|F^{1}\|_{L^{q_{-}}}+\|F^{4}\|_{W^{1-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}+\|F^{5}\|_{W^{1-1/q_{-},q_{-}}})\big]\|\psi\|_{H^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big[\|\partial_{t}(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\ast}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}(\|F^{1}\|_{L^{q_{-}}}+\|F^{4}\|_{W^{1-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}+\|F^{5}\|_{W^{1-1/q_{-},q_{-}}})\big]\|D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1/2}_{\mathcal{K}}}

\displaystyle+\big[\|\partial_{t}(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\ast}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}(\|F^{1}\|_{L^{q_{-}}}+\|F^{4}\|_{W^{1-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}+\|F^{5}\|_{W^{1-1/q_{-},q_{-}}})\big]

\displaystyle\times\big(\|v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{H^{1}}\big).

We then estimate each term in IV. For terms in the first line of IV, we bound them by

(4.69)

\displaystyle-\int_{\Omega}\frac{\mu}{2}(\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{n}}v_{d}:\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(M^{n}\nabla\psi)+\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}v_{d}:\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{n}}(M^{n}\nabla\psi)+\partial_{t}J^{n}K^{n}\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}v_{d}:\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(M^{n}\nabla\psi))J^{n}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{n}\|_{W^{1,\infty}}\|v_{d}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}\|\psi\|_{H^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{3/2+\varepsilon_{-}/2}}\|v_{d}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}\|D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1/2}_{\mathcal{K}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{3/2+\varepsilon_{-}/2}}\|v_{d}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1}}\big(\|v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{H^{1}}\big).

For the remaining terms in IV, we bound them as

(4.70)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &-(\partial_{t}v_{d},R^{n}M^{n}\nabla\psi)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}+\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}v_{d}\cdot M^{n}\nabla\psi\partial_{t}J^{n}+\int_{\Sigma_{s}}\beta(v_{d}\cdot\tau)(M^{n}\nabla\psi\cdot\tau)\partial_{t}J^{n}+(\partial_{t}\xi_{d},a_{0}(t)D_{j}^{s}\zeta_{0})_{1,\Sigma_{n}}\\
&\lesssim\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}(\|R^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}+\|\partial_{t}J^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)})\|\psi\|_{H^{1}}+\|v_{d}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s})}\|\partial_{t}J^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma_{s})}\|\psi\|_{H^{2}}\\
&\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{3/2+\varepsilon_{-}/2}}\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{s-1/2}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{3/2+\varepsilon_{-}/2}}\|v_{d}\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s})}\|D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{1/2}_{\mathcal{K}}}\\
&\quad+\big(\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|v_{d}\|_{H^{1}}\big)\Big(\|v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\\
&\quad\quad+\|u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{H^{1}}\Big).\end{aligned}

Finally, for the term
V
, using the similar computation as in Step 4, we obtain

(4.71)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} V\lesssim&\|\psi\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{2}}\Big(\|\partial_{t}^{2}{\eta}^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}}}\|v_{i}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}{\eta}^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|D_{t}v_{l}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}}\\
&\quad+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|D_{t}v_{l}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}{\eta}^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|v_{l}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\Big)\\
&\quad+\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\psi\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}\xi^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\psi\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}\\
\lesssim&\mathscr{S}^{n,k}\cdot\Big(\|v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\\
&\quad+\|u(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{H^{1}}\Big)+\big(\|v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\\
&\quad+\|u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{1}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{H^{1}}\big).\end{aligned}

Finally, using (
4.60
)–(
4.71
), together with Cauchy’s inequality, and letting
j\rightarrow+\infty
, we obtain the following enhanced estimate for
\partial_{t}\xi_{d}
:

(4.72)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{3/2-\tilde{\alpha}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\big\{(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3/2+\varepsilon_{-}/2}}+||u^{k}||_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{5}{2}}}+||\eta^{n}||_{L^{\infty}H^{3}})T\big\}\bigg(\mathcal{E}(0)

\displaystyle+\|(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})(0)\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\ast}}^{2}+\mathfrak{F}^{n}+\|u_{1}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-2\tilde{\alpha}}}+\mathscr{S}^{n,k}\bigg).

Notice that the right-hand side of (
4.72
) contains the term
||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}||_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-2\tilde{\alpha}}}
, which cannot be controlled by the existing bound
\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\in L^{2}H^{1}
from the energy estimate (
4.48
). Thus, we utilize a bootstrapping argument to improve the
\partial_{t}\xi_{d}
estimates.

Setting
\tilde{\alpha}=\frac{1}{4}
which implies
\frac{3}{2}-2\tilde{\alpha}=1
, we have
||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}||_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-2\tilde{\alpha}}}=||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}||_{H^{1}}
is controllable. Based on (
4.72
), we obtain the improved bound for
\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\in L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\tilde{\alpha}}=\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\in L^{2}H^{\frac{5}{4}}
.
Resetting
\tilde{\alpha}=\frac{1}{8}
, the term
||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}||_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-2\tilde{\alpha}}}=||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}||_{H^{\frac{5}{4}}}
is bounded by the result of the previous step. Again based on (
4.72
), we obtain the improved bound for
\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\in L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\tilde{\alpha}}=\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\in L^{2}H^{\frac{11}{8}}
.
Iterating this procedure allows us to bootstrap the regularity of
\partial_{t}\xi_{d}
. Let
\tilde{\alpha}_{i}
denote the value of
\tilde{\alpha}
chosen at the
i
-th step. We have
\tilde{\alpha}_{i+1}=\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{i}
. It is then straightforward to verify that
\tilde{\alpha}_{i}\rightarrow 0
. Therefore, there exists an integer
N
such that
\tilde{\alpha}_{i}\leq\alpha
for
i>N
. At that stage, the term including
\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-2\tilde{\alpha}}}
can be absorbed by the LHS, and we obtain the following estimate:

(4.73)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{3/2-{\alpha}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\big\{(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3/2+\varepsilon_{-}/2}}+||u^{k}||_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{5}{2}}}+||\eta^{n}||_{L^{\infty}H^{3}})T\big\}\bigg(\mathcal{E}(0)

\displaystyle+\|(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})(0)\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\ast}}^{2}+\mathfrak{F}^{n}+\mathscr{S}^{n,k}\bigg)

Step 7 – Pressure Estimates and Strong Solution

In this step, we fix
n,k
and the prescribed functions
\xi_{l},v_{l}
.
Using the convergence in (
4.47
) and the estimate (
4.73
), we pass to the limit in (
4.15
) for almost every
t\in[0,T]
so that

(4.74)

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}v_{d},w)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}+((v_{d},w))+(\xi_{d},w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n})_{1,\Sigma_{0}}+\left(\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d},\partial_{1}(w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n})\right)_{L^{2}}+[v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n},w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}

\displaystyle=\int_{\Omega}F^{1}\cdot wJ^{n}+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}F^{4}\cdot w-\int_{\Sigma_{s}}F^{5}(w\cdot\tau)J^{n}-[F^{7},w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}+((R^{n}D_{t}v_{l}^{k}),w)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}

\displaystyle+((R^{n}v_{l}^{k},w))-(I^{n,k}_{1},\partial_{1}(w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}))_{L^{2}(-\ell.\ell)}-\left(\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})\partial_{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k},\partial_{1}(w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n})\right)_{L^{2}},

coupled with the kinematic boundary condition (
1.30
)
for any
w\in\mathcal{W}(t)
.
Then applying Theorem
3.5
, we recover the pressure
q_{d}
.
Moreover,
(v_{d},q_{d},\xi_{d})
is the strong solution to equation (
1.28
) satisfies the following bound

(4.75)

\displaystyle\|v_{d}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|q_{d}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\xi_{d}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}v_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{0}}^{2}+\|\xi_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2}+\|v_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2}+\|\xi_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{3/2-\alpha}}^{2}+\|[v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}]_{\ell}\|_{L^{2}_{t}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|F^{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}^{2}+\|F^{4}\|_{L^{2}W^{1-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2}+\|F^{5}\|_{L^{2}W^{1-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2}+\|[F^{7}]_{\ell}\|_{L_{t}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}^{2}\|v_{l}^{k}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{2}W^{2,q_{+}}}+T\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}.

Applying the Sobolev embedding
H^{3/2-\alpha}\hookrightarrow W^{2-1/q_{-},q_{-}}
, we substitute (
4.73
) into (
4.75
) to obtain

(4.76)

\displaystyle\|v_{d}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|q_{d}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\xi_{d}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\big\{(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3/2+\varepsilon_{-}/2}}+||u^{k}||_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{5}{2}}}+||\eta^{n}||_{L^{\infty}H^{3}})T\big\}\bigg(\mathcal{E}(0)+\|(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})(0)\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\ast}}^{2}+\mathfrak{F}+\mathscr{S}^{n,k}\bigg)

Therefore, the bound (
4.76
) implies that
(v_{d},q_{d},\xi_{d})
enjoys higher regularity than that required for weak solutions. This allows us to derive a uniform bound for the solution to (
1.28
) that is independent of
k
which we establish in the next step.

Step 8 – Uniform
k
Bound

In this step, we fix
n
and the prescribed functions
\xi_{l},v_{l}
.

Suppose that the strong solution of (
1.28
) is denoted by
(v_{d}^{k},q_{d}^{k},\xi_{d}^{k})
. We now aim to show that this solution converges to the solution of the following system

(4.77)

{}\begin{cases}\partial_{t}v_{d}+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(v_{d},q_{d})+\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}\nabla_{\mathcal{A}}(R^{n}v_{l})+\partial_{t}(R^{n}v_{l})=F^{1}(u,p,\eta)~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}v_{d}=0~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(q_{d},v_{d})\mathcal{N}^{n}+\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(R^{n}v_{l})\mathcal{N}^{n}=g\xi_{d}\mathcal{N}^{n}-\sigma\partial_{1}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2}}}\partial_{1}\xi_{d})\mathcal{N}^{n}+\partial_{1}I_{1}^{n}\mathcal{N}^{n}\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sigma\partial_{1}(\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)(\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{l})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta)\mathcal{N}^{n}+\sigma\partial_{1}(\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d})\mathcal{N}^{n}\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+F^{4}(u,p,\eta)~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
(S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(q_{d},v_{d})\nu+\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(R^{n}v_{l})\nu-\beta v_{d})\cdot\tau=F^{5}(u,\eta,p)~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
v_{d}\cdot\nu=0~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
\partial_{t}\xi_{d}=v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}+(R^{n}v_{l})\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}+\int_{0}^{t}(\partial_{t}u_{1}\cdot\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{l}+u_{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d})+I_{2}^{n}~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
\sigma(\mp\frac{\partial_{1}\xi_{d}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\pm\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{d})(\pm\ell)\pm\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)(\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{l})\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\eta\pm I_{1}^{n})\\
\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad=\kappa(v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n})(\pm\ell)-{F}^{7},\end{cases}

as
k\to\infty
, where

I_{1}^{n}=\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta(0))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n})~~~~\operatorname{and}~~~~~~~I_{2}^{n}=u(0)\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n}
.
To prove this convergence result, we need to first establish a uniform bound for
(v_{d}^{k},q_{d}^{k},\xi_{d}^{k})
. Observing that the bounds for
(v_{d}^{k},\xi_{d}^{k},q_{d}^{k})
depend on
(||u^{k}||_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{5}{2}}}
,
||\eta^{k}||_{L^{\infty}H^{3}})
, which may blow up as
k\rightarrow\infty
, we modify the estimates for several terms in Steps 3, 4 and 6 to remove the dependency.

For the energy-dissipation estimate, we only modify the bounds for (
4.23
), (
4.24
) and (
4.25
), while keeping the estimates for all other terms unchanged. For (
4.23
), we estimate

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{m},\partial_{t}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{l}^{k})_{1,\Sigma_{k}}\lesssim

\displaystyle||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{m}||_{W^{1,\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{-}}}}||\partial_{t}u_{1}^{k}||_{W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)}||\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}||_{W^{1,+\infty}}+||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{m}||_{W^{1,\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{-}}}}||\partial_{t}u_{1}^{k}||_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}||\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}||_{W^{2,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}

(4.78)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{m}||_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}||\partial_{t}u^{k}||_{W^{2,q_{-}}}||\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}||_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}+||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{m}||_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}||\partial_{t}u^{k}||_{H^{1+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}}||\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}||_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}.

Similarly for (
4.24
), we have

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k},u_{1}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k})_{1,\Sigma_{k}}

\displaystyle=\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}g\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}+\sigma\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{1}^{2}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})u_{1}^{k}\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{d}^{k}\partial_{1}^{2}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}g\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}\partial_{1}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}+\sigma\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}\partial_{1}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})\partial_{1}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{d}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\mathcal{R}_{zz}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})u_{1}^{k}\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{d}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}(\partial_{1}^{2}\zeta_{0}+\partial_{1}^{2}\eta^{k})

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}g\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}\partial_{1}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}+\sigma\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}\partial_{1}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})\partial_{1}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{d}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}

\displaystyle\lesssim||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}||^{2}_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}(||\eta^{k}||_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+||u^{k}||_{W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)})

(4.79)

\displaystyle\lesssim||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}||^{2}_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}(||\eta^{k}||_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+||u^{k}||_{W^{2,q_{-}}}),

and similarly

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k},D_{t}(R^{n}v_{l}^{k})\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n})_{1,\Sigma_{k}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|D_{t}v_{l}^{k}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|D_{t}v_{l}^{k}\|_{1+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{2}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{n}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}).

For the uniform
\frac{3}{2}-\alpha
estimate of free surface, we apply the functional calculus for gravity-capillary operator
\mathcal{K}
(Not the modified operator). Most of the computations are identical to those in Step 6 except for the estimate of the term including
\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})
. We estimate this term as follows

(4.80)

\displaystyle{}\begin{aligned} &\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{1}D_{j}^{s}(\partial_{t}\xi_{d}-a_{0}(t)\rho_{0})\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}\lesssim\|\partial_{1}D_{j}^{s}(\partial_{t}\xi_{d}-a_{0}(t)\rho_{0})\|_{H^{-\frac{s}{2}}}\|\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}+}}\|\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}\|_{H^{\frac{s}{2}}}\\
\lesssim&\big(\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}+\|v_{d}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}\big)\\
&\times\|\eta^{k}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}.\end{aligned}

We now substitute estimates (
4.78
) and (
4.79
) into the bounds obtained in Step 3, and apply (
4.80
) in the

\frac{3}{2}-\alpha
estimate.
Combing these estimates, we obtain the following uniform bound for
(v_{d}^{k},\xi_{d}^{k},q_{d}^{k})
:

(4.81)

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{1}\bigg(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\bigl(\|\partial_{t}^{i}v_{d}^{m}\|_{0}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{i}\xi_{d}^{m}\|_{1}^{2}\bigr)+\|\partial_{t}^{i}v_{d}^{m}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2}+\|[\partial_{t}^{i}v_{d}^{m}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}\|_{L_{t}^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{i}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\|v_{d}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|q_{d}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\xi_{d}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\{(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}+||\eta^{n}||_{L^{\infty}H^{3}})T\}\bigg(\mathcal{E}(0)+\|(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})+\mathfrak{F}^{n}(0)\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\ast}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathfrak{K}(u^{k},p^{k},\eta^{k})\mathfrak{K}_{-}(v_{l}^{k},\xi_{l}^{k})+(T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{n}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{5}{2}}}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\eta}^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2})(\mathfrak{K}(u^{k},p^{k},\eta^{k})+\mathfrak{K}_{-}(v_{l}^{k},\xi_{l}^{k}))\bigg),

where
\mathfrak{K}_{-}
is defined by (
4.87
) in the next step.
Using the convergence result established in (
C.2
), we obtain the following bound from (
4.81
).

(4.82)

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{1}\bigg(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\bigl(\|\partial_{t}^{i}v_{d}^{m}\|_{0}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{i}\xi_{d}^{m}\|_{1}^{2}\bigr)+\|\partial_{t}^{i}v_{d}^{m}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2}+\|[\partial_{t}^{i}v_{d}^{m}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}\|^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{i}\xi_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\|v_{d}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|q_{d}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\xi_{d}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\exp\{(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}+||\eta^{n}||_{L^{\infty}H^{3}})T\}\bigg(\mathcal{E}(0)+\|(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})(0)\|_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\ast}}^{2}+\mathfrak{F}^{n}

\displaystyle+\mathfrak{K}(u,p,\eta)\mathfrak{K}_{-}(v_{l},\xi_{l})+(T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{n}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{5}{2}}}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\eta}^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2})(\mathfrak{K}(u,p,\eta)+\mathfrak{K}_{-}(v_{l},\xi_{l}))\bigg).

We now have a uniform bound for
(v^{k}_{d},q^{k}_{d},\xi^{k}_{d})
and
(\partial_{t}v_{d}^{k},\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k})
independent of
k
. This uniform boundedness implies that, up to the extraction of a subsequence,

(v_{d}^{k},\xi_{d}^{k},q_{d}^{k})\rightharpoonup(v_{d},\xi_{d},q_{d})~~\operatorname{in}~~(L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}\times L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}\times L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}),

(v_{d}^{k},\xi_{d}^{k},q_{d}^{k})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\ast}}{{\rightharpoonup}}(v_{d},\xi_{d},q_{d})~~\operatorname{in}~~(L^{\infty}H^{1}\times L^{\infty}H^{1}\times L^{\infty}L^{2}),

(\partial_{t}v_{d}^{k},\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k})\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\ast}}{{\rightharpoonup}}(\partial_{t}v_{d},\partial_{t}\xi_{d})\ \text{in}\ L^{\infty}L^{2}\times L^{\infty}H^{1}),\ (\partial_{t}v_{d}^{k},\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k})\rightharpoonup(\partial_{t}v_{d},\partial_{t}\xi_{d})~~\operatorname{in}~~((L_{t}^{2}H^{1}\times L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}).

From the weak convergence above, together with the strong convergence of
\eta^{k}
to
\eta
, we conclude that
(v_{d},\xi_{d})
is the weak solution to the following equation

(4.83)

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}v_{d},w)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}+((v_{d},w))+(\xi_{d},w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n})_{1,\Sigma_{0}}+(\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d},\partial_{1}(w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}))_{L^{2}}+[v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n},w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}

\displaystyle=\int_{\Omega}F^{1}\cdot wJ^{n}-\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}F^{4}\cdot w-\int_{\Sigma_{s}}F^{5}(w\cdot\tau)J^{n}-[F^{7},w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}+(\partial_{t}(R^{n}v_{l}),w)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}+((R^{n}v_{l},w))

\displaystyle-(I_{1}^{n},\partial_{1}(w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}))_{L^{2}(-\ell.\ell)}+(\partial_{1}\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{l}\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\eta,(w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}))_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\left[\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{l}\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\eta,w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}\right]_{\ell}.

For the kinematic boundary condition, we note that the kinematic boundary condition for smooth-value problem is expressed as

(4.84)

\displaystyle{}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}=v_{d}^{k}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}+R^{n}v_{l}^{k}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}+\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}u_{1}^{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{l}^{k}+\int_{0}^{t}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}+I^{n,k}_{2}.

For the right hand side of the equation above, we have the following estimate

\displaystyle||v_{d}^{k}\cdot\mathcal{N}+R^{n}v_{l}^{k}+\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}u_{1}^{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{l}^{k}+\int_{0}^{t}u_{1}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}+I_{2}^{n,k}||_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}}\lesssim||\partial_{t}u||_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}||\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k}||_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}

\displaystyle+||v_{d}^{n}||_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+||u^{k}||_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}||\partial_{t}\xi_{d}^{k}||_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}+||I_{2}^{n,k}||_{H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|v_{l}^{k}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}.

Therefore, both the left- and right-hand sides of the kinematic boundary condition for the smooth, given-data problem are uniformly bounded in
L^{\infty}H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}
. Using this uniform boundedness and the compact embedding theorem, we infer that equation (
4.84
) converges to:

\partial_{t}\xi_{d}=v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}+R^{n}v_{l}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}+\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}u_{1}\cdot\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{l}+\int_{0}^{t}u_{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d}+I^{n}_{2}

strongly in
L^{\infty}L^{2}
as
k\rightarrow\infty
. Therefore,
(\xi_{d},v_{d})
is the weak solution to (
4.77
). Moreover, since
(v_{d},\xi_{d},q_{d})\in(L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}\times L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}\times L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}})
, this weak solution is a strong solution to (
4.77
) and is bounded by the right-hand side of (
4.82
).

Step 9 – Contraction Map

In this step, we fix
n
and prove that system (
4.77
) induces a linear map from
(v_{l},\xi_{l})
to
(v,\xi)
.

Setting
v_{d}=D_{t}v,q_{d}=\partial_{t}q,\xi_{d}=\partial_{t}\xi
, and combining this with the initial data constructed in Appendix
D
, we can recover
(v,q,\xi)
.

To construct the contraction map, we introduce the following definitions:

(4.85)

\displaystyle\mathscr{D}_{-}(u,\eta)

\displaystyle:=(\|u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2})+\sum_{j=0}^{2}\Big(\|\partial_{t}^{j}u\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{j}u\|_{L^{2}L^{2}(\Sigma_{s})}^{2}\Big)+\|\partial_{t}^{3}\eta\|_{L^{2}H^{1/2-\alpha}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=0}^{2}\Big(\|\partial_{t}^{j}\eta\|_{L^{2}H^{3/2-\alpha}}^{2}+\|[\partial_{t}^{j}u\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}}\Big)+\Big(\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2}\Big),

(4.86)

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}_{-}(u,\eta)

\displaystyle:=\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{1}\|\partial_{t}^{i}u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1+\varepsilon_{-}/2}}^{2}+\sum_{k=0}^{2}\|\partial_{t}^{k}u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{0}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{1}\|\partial_{t}^{i}\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}^{2}+\sum_{j=0}^{2}(\|\partial_{t}^{j}\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}+\|[\partial_{t}^{j}u\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}]_{\ell}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}}^{2}),

(4.87)

\mathfrak{K}_{-}(u,\eta):=\mathscr{E}_{-}(u,\eta)+\mathscr{D}_{-}(u,\eta).

together with the functional space

(4.88)

\displaystyle\mathscr{H}:=\{(v,\xi)\in L_{t}^{\infty}\mathcal{W}_{\sigma}\times L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}|\mathfrak{K}_{-}(v,\xi)<+\infty,~v(0,x)=v_{0}^{n},~\partial_{t}v(0,x)=\partial_{t}v_{0}^{n},~\xi(0,x)=\xi_{0}^{n},\partial_{t}\xi(0,x)=\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n}\}

where
v_{0}^{n},\partial_{t}v_{0}^{n},\xi_{0}^{n},\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n}
are defined as in Appendix
D
.

We first prove that the space
\mathscr{H}
is complete. Since
\mathfrak{K}_{-}(u,\eta)
induces a complete Sobolev space,it suffices to show that the limit satisfies the fixed boundary condition.

When
(v,\xi)\in\mathscr{H}
, it holds that

\|\partial_{t}v\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}\xi\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}v\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\xi\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}<\mathfrak{K}_{-}(v,\xi)<\infty

which implies that

(v,\xi,\partial_{t}v,\partial_{t}\xi)\in H_{t}^{1}W^{2,q_{-}}\times H_{t}^{1}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}\times H_{t}^{1}H^{1}\times H_{t}^{1}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}
.
Therefore, any Cauchy sequence
\{(v^{r},\xi^{r})\}
in
\mathscr{H}
implies that
\{(v^{r},\xi^{r},\partial_{t}v^{r},\partial_{t}\xi^{r})\}
is a Cauchy sequence in

H_{t}^{1}W^{2,q_{-}}\times H_{t}^{1}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}\times H_{t}^{1}H^{1}\times H_{t}^{1}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}
.
This implies that the limit of the Cauchy sequence satisfies the initial condition required in the definition of the space
\mathscr{H}
. Therefore,
\mathscr{H}
is a complete space.

We denote the metric induced by norm
\mathfrak{K}_{-}(u,\eta)
as
d(u,\eta)
. Then the estimate (
4.82
) implies that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{K}_{-}(v,\xi)\lesssim

\displaystyle\exp\{(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}}+\|\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3}})T\}\big(\mathscr{E}(u(0),p(0),\eta(0))

\displaystyle+\mathfrak{F}^{n}+(\delta+(T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{n}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{5}{2}}}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\eta}^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}))\mathfrak{K}_{-}(v_{l},\xi_{l})\big),

where for simplicity,
\mathfrak{F}
denotes the norms of the forcing terms.

Moreover, given two different tuples
(v_{l}^{1},\xi_{l}^{1})
and
(v_{l}^{2},\xi_{l}^{2})
. We subtract the respective evaluations of equation (
4.77
) to obtain a new system for the difference functions
(v^{1}-v^{2},\xi^{1}-\xi^{2},q^{1}-q^{2})
:

\begin{cases}\partial_{t}(v_{d}^{1}-v_{d}^{2})+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(v_{d}^{1}-v_{d}^{2},q_{d}^{1}-q_{d}^{2})+\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}\nabla_{\mathcal{A}}(R^{n}(v_{l}^{1}-v_{l}^{2}))+\partial_{t}(R^{n}(v_{l}^{1}-v_{l}^{2}))=0~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(v_{d}^{1}-v_{d}^{2})=0~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(q_{d}^{1}-q_{d}^{2},v_{d}^{1}-v_{d}^{2})\mathcal{N}^{n}+\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(R^{n}(v_{l}^{1}-v_{l}^{2}))\mathcal{N}^{n}=g(\xi_{d}^{1}-\xi_{d}^{2})\mathcal{N}^{n}-\sigma\partial_{1}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2}}}\partial_{1}(\xi_{d}^{1}-\xi_{d}^{2}))\mathcal{N}^{n}\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sigma\partial_{1}(\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)(\partial_{1}\partial_{t}(\xi_{l}^{1}-\xi_{l}^{2}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta)\mathcal{N}^{n}\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sigma\partial_{1}(\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)\partial_{1}\partial_{t}(\xi_{d}^{1}-\xi_{d}^{2}))\mathcal{N}^{n}~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
(S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(q_{d}^{1}-q_{d}^{2},v_{d}^{1}-v_{d}^{2})\nu+\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(R^{n}(v_{l}^{1}-v_{l}^{2}))\nu-\beta(v_{d}^{1}-v_{d}^{2})\cdot\tau=0~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
(v_{d}^{1}-v_{d}^{2})\cdot\nu=0~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
\partial_{t}(\xi_{d}^{1}-\xi_{d}^{2})=(v_{d}^{1}-v_{d}^{2})\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}+(R^{n}(v_{l}^{1}-v_{l}^{2}))\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}+\int_{0}^{t}(\partial_{t}u_{1}\cdot\partial_{t}\partial_{1}(\xi_{l}^{1}-\xi_{l}^{2})+u_{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}(\xi_{d}^{1}-\xi_{d}^{2}))~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
\sigma(\mp\frac{\partial_{1}(\xi_{d}^{1}-\xi_{d}^{2})}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\pm\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)\partial_{t}\partial_{1}(\xi_{d}^{1}-\xi_{d}^{2}))(\pm\ell)ds\pm\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)(\partial_{t}\partial_{1}(\xi_{l}^{1}-\xi_{l}^{2}))\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\eta)\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad=\kappa((v_{d}^{1}-v_{d}^{2})\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n})(\pm\ell).\end{cases}

Then, applying energy and elliptic estimates similar to those in the previous steps, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathfrak{K}_{-}\bigl(v^{1}-v^{2},\xi^{1}-\xi^{2}\bigr)\;\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta\exp\{(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}}+\|\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3}})T\}\,\bigg(\delta+(T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{n}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{5}{2}}}

\displaystyle+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\eta}^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2})\cdot\mathfrak{K}_{-}\bigl(v_{l}^{1}-v_{l}^{2},\xi_{l}^{1}-\xi_{l}^{2}\bigr)\bigg),

for the same
\delta\in(0,1)
.

Together with the estimates obtained above, this inequality implies that the
Picard iteration induced by (
1.28
) defines a contraction
mapping in the functional space
\mathscr{H}
. Consequently, for each fixed
n
, the iteration admits a unique fixed
point
(v^{n},\xi^{n},q^{n})
, which solves the system (
1.33
)

Moreover, the solution satisfies the estimate

\displaystyle\mathfrak{K}_{-}(v^{n},\xi^{n})+\|q^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}q^{n}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}q^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{0}}

\displaystyle\;\lesssim

\displaystyle\exp\!\Bigl\{\bigl(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}+\|\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3}}\bigr)T\Bigr\}\bigg\{\mathcal{E}(u_{0},p_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle+\|(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})(0)\|^{2}_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\ast}}+\mathfrak{F}^{n}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{n}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{5}{2}}}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\eta}^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2})(\mathfrak{K}(u,p,\eta))\bigg\}.

Step 10 – Uniform
n
Bound

In this step, we derive a uniform bound for the solution to (
1.22
).

First, for any prescribed
n
, we apply the Theorem
3.7
to equation (
1.33
) to show that

\displaystyle\|v^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|q^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\xi^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{Z}+\exp\!\Bigl\{\bigl(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}+\|\eta^{n}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3}}\bigr)T\Bigr\}\bigg\{\mathcal{E}(u_{0},p_{0},\xi_{0})

\displaystyle+\|(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})(0)\|^{2}_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\ast}}+\mathfrak{F}^{n}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{n}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{5}{2}}}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\eta}^{n}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2})(\mathfrak{K}(u,p,\eta))\bigg\}.

With this
q_{+}
estimate, we define a function
w
as follows:

\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}}w=J^{n}(-2\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{n}}\partial_{t}v^{n}-\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}\partial_{t}^{2}v^{n})-\langle J^{n}(-2\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{n}}\partial_{t}v^{n}-\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}\partial_{t}^{2}v^{n})\rangle,

where
\langle f\rangle:=\frac{1}{|\Omega|}\int_{\Omega}f
.
With the
q_{+}
estimate, the function
w
is well-defined and enjoys good regularity. The details are given in
[
10
, Proposition 10.1]
.

After differentiating (
1.33
) with respect to time, we take
\partial_{t}^{2}v^{n}-w
as the test function. Combining the results of
[
10
, Section 10.2]
, the
\frac{3}{2}-\alpha
estimate, and the elliptic estimates derived in Step 8, we obtain the following bound:

\displaystyle\mathfrak{K}(v^{n},\xi^{n},q^{n})\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+T)\exp\big\{T(\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}+\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})\big\}

\displaystyle\times\bigg\{\mathcal{E}(u_{0},p_{0},\xi_{0})+\|(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})(0)\|^{2}_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\ast}}+\mathfrak{F}^{n}+\mathcal{Z}+\mathfrak{K}(u,p,\eta)\bigg\}.

This uniform bound implies the existence of a time
T>0
, independent of
n
, such that
(v^{n},\xi^{n},q^{n})
exists on the interval
(0,T)
for every
n
. Moreover, combining this estimate with the convergence result (
1.25
), we obtain the following uniform bound

(4.89)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \mathfrak{K}(v^{n},\xi^{n},q^{n})\lesssim&(1+T)\exp\big\{T(\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}+\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})\big\}\\
&\quad\quad\times\bigg\{\mathcal{E}(u_{0},p_{0},\xi_{0})+\|(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})(0)\|^{2}_{(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\ast}}+\mathfrak{F}+\mathcal{Z}+\mathfrak{K}(u,p,\eta)\bigg\}.\end{aligned}

With this uniform bound, which is independent of
n
, we may let
n\to\infty
.
Using the same argument as Step 9 to establish convergence,
the sequence
(v^{n},\xi^{n},q^{n})
converges to a solution of
(
1.17
). This completes the proof.
∎

Now, it remains to prove Lemma
4.6
showing the difference between
D_{t}v_{d}
and
\partial_{t}v_{d}
.

Lemma 4.6
.

D_{t}=\partial_{t}-R
where
R
is defined in Proposition
2.5
. It holds that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}v_{d}-D_{t}v_{d}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}^{2}\lesssim||\partial_{t}\eta||_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}^{2}||v_{d}||_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}^{2},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}v_{d}-D_{t}v_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}-D_{t}v_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{0}(\Sigma_{s})}^{2}\lesssim||\partial_{t}\eta||^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|v_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2},

\displaystyle\|\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}}\partial_{t}v_{d}-\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}}D_{t}v_{d}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{2}L^{2}}\lesssim T||\partial_{t}\eta||^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|v_{d}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}.

Proof.

For the first inequality, we have the following computation using the fact that
R\sim\partial_{t}\nabla\bar{\eta}
:

\displaystyle||Rv_{d}||^{2}_{W^{2,q_{-}}}\lesssim

\displaystyle||\nabla^{2}Rv_{d}||^{2}_{L^{q_{-}}}+||\nabla R\nabla v_{d}||_{L^{q_{-}}}^{2}+||R\nabla^{2}v_{d}||_{L^{q_{-}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||\nabla^{2}R||^{2}_{L^{q_{-}}}||v_{d}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+||\nabla R||^{2}_{L^{\frac{1}{1-{\varepsilon_{-}}}}}||\nabla v_{d}||_{L^{\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}^{2}+||R||^{2}_{L^{\infty}}||\nabla^{2}v_{d}||_{L^{q_{-}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}||^{2}_{W^{3,q_{-}}}||v_{d}||_{H^{1+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}}^{2}+||\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}||_{W^{3,q_{-}}}^{2}||v_{d}||^{2}_{W^{2,q_{-}}}+||\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}||_{H^{2+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}^{2}||v_{d}||_{W^{2,q_{-}}}.

Using the relation between
\bar{\eta}
and
\eta
, we have:

||Rv_{d}||^{2}_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\lesssim||\eta||_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}^{2}||v_{d}||_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}^{2}.

Similarly, we derive the second inequality as follows

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}v_{d}-D_{t}v_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}v_{d}-D_{t}v_{d}\|_{L^{2}H^{0}(\Sigma_{s})}^{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle||Rv_{d}||_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2}\lesssim||\nabla Rv_{d}||^{2}_{L^{2}L^{2}}+||R\nabla v_{d}||_{L^{2}L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle||\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}||_{L^{\infty}W^{2,\frac{4}{2-(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)}}}^{2}||v_{d}||_{L^{2}L^{\frac{4}{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}}}^{2}+||\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}||^{2}_{L^{\infty}W^{1,+\infty}}||v_{d}||^{2}_{L^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\eta\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}H^{2+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|v_{d}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}^{2}.

Finally, the third inequality can be derived by

\|\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}}\partial_{t}v_{d}-\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}}D_{t}v_{d}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{2}L^{2}}\lesssim T||Rv_{d}||_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}\lesssim T||\eta||^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|v_{d}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}.

Hence, we have proved the lemma.
∎

Remark 4.1
.

The result of Lemma 4.6 continues to hold when
R
is replaced by
R^{n}
.

5.
Local Well-Posedness of the Nonlinear System

In this section, we investigate the local well-posedness of the nonlinear (
1.7
) or equivalently (
1.17
). In contrast to Section
4
, we now assume that the forcing terms
F^{i}
indeed depend on the unknowns and thus an iteration/contraction-mapping argument is necessary. We will heavily rely on the estimates (
4.9
) obtained in Section
4
.

5.1.
Forcing Term Estimates

In this subsection, we derive estimates for the forcing terms
F^{i}
in (
1.17
). We first recall the definitions of
\mathscr{D}
and
\mathscr{E}
from (
4.6
) and (
4.7
), respectively, and then introduce two additional spaces.

(5.1)

\displaystyle\mathcal{X}=\Big\{(u,p,\eta)|\mathscr{E}(u,p,\eta)<\infty\Big\},\quad\|(u,p,\eta)\|_{\mathcal{X}}=\left[\mathscr{E}(u,p,\eta)\right]^{1/2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{Y}=\Big\{(u,p,\eta)|\mathscr{D}(u,p,\eta)<\infty\Big\},\quad\|(u,p,\eta)\|_{\mathcal{Y}}=\left[\mathscr{D}(u,p,\eta)\right]^{1/2}.

From the formulation of (
1.7
), the nonlinear interaction terms acting as forcing functions are given in Appendix
A
.
To close the energy estimates, we must control the forcing terms in the sense of Theorem
4.5
. We first estimate the forcing terms in the bulk.

Proposition 5.1
.

Let
F^{1},F^{4},F^{5}
be the forcing terms defined in Appendix
A
. Then,

\displaystyle\|F^{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}+\|F^{4}\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+\|F^{5}\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{1/2}\mathscr{D}^{1/2}.

Proof.

Step 1 – Estimate of
F^{1}

Using Hölder’s inequality, Sobolev embedding, the trace theory, and the bound on
\mathcal{A}
, we estimate terms including
\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}}
in
F^{1}
as follows

\displaystyle{}\begin{aligned} ||\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}S_{\mathcal{A}}(p,u)||_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim&||\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}||_{L^{\infty}W^{1,+\infty}}||\bar{\eta}||_{L^{\infty}W^{2,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}(||p||_{L^{\infty}W^{0,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}+||u||_{L^{\infty}W^{1,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}})\\
&+||\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}||_{L^{\infty}W^{1,\infty}}||\bar{\eta}||_{L^{\infty}W^{1,+\infty}}(||p||_{L^{\infty}W^{1,q_{-}}}+||u||_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}})\\
\lesssim&||\partial_{t}\eta||_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}||\eta||_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}(||p||_{L^{\infty}W^{1,q_{-}}}+||u||_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}).\end{aligned}

Similarly, we have

(5.2)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} ||\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}}\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u||_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim&||\partial_{t}\eta||_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}||u||_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}.\end{aligned}

We now estimate terms of the form
\partial_{t}^{i}u\nabla_{\partial_{t}^{j}\mathcal{A}}\partial_{t}^{k}u
, we use the fact that
|\partial_{t}^{i}\nabla^{j}\mathcal{A}|\lesssim\sum_{k=0}^{j}|\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{1}^{k}\bar{\eta}|
:

(5.3)

\displaystyle{}\begin{aligned} \|{\partial_{t}}u\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}}u\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim&||\partial_{t}u||_{L^{\infty}L^{\frac{4}{2-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}},\\
\|u\cdot\nabla_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim&||u||_{L^{\infty}L^{\frac{4}{2-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}||\partial_{t}\eta||_{L^{\infty}W^{1,+\infty}}\\
\lesssim&\|u\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}||\partial_{t}{\eta}||_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}},\\
\|u\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}}\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim&||u||_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}.\end{aligned}

For the remaining terms involved in
F^{1}
, we have the following computation:

(5.4)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}WK\partial_{2}u)=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}WK\partial_{2}u+\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\partial_{t}WK\partial_{2}u+\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}W\partial_{t}K\partial_{2}u+\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}WK\partial_{2}\partial_{t}u.

Using the fact that
|\partial_{t}^{i}\nabla^{j}K|\lesssim\sum_{k=0}^{j}|\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{1}^{k}\bar{\eta}|
, and that
|\partial_{t}^{i}\nabla^{j}W|\lesssim\sum_{k=0}^{j}|\partial_{t}^{i}\partial_{1}^{k}\bar{\eta}|
, we obtain the following estimates

(5.5)

\displaystyle{}\begin{aligned} \|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}WK\partial_{2}u\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{1-\alpha}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}^{2}{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}},\\
\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\partial_{t}WK\partial_{2}u\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{+\infty}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,+\infty}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}},\\
\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}W\partial_{t}K\partial_{2}u\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{+\infty}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,+\infty}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}},\\
\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}WK\partial_{2}\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{+\infty}}\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}.\end{aligned}

Combining estimates (
5.1
) to (
5.5
), we obtain the estimate for
F^{1}
.

Step 2 – Estimate of
F^{4}

We now bound each term involved in
F^{4}
. First, we bound the terms including the velocity field
u
as follows

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u\mathcal{N}\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}(\Sigma)}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}},

\displaystyle\|S_{\mathcal{A}}(u,p)\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}(\Sigma)}+\|p\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}(\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|p\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1,q_{-}{}}}).

Then we estimate each term involving the mean curvature. We have

\displaystyle\|2\left[g(\eta)-\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}\eta}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|)^{3/2}}\right)-\sigma\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta))\right]\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|2\left[g(\eta)-\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}\eta}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|)^{3/2}}\right)-\sigma\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta))\right]\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,q_{-}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(1+\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}},

where we used the trace theorem.

Therefore, combining all the computations in this step, we obtain the estimate for
\|F^{4}\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}
.

Step 3 – Estimate for
F^{5}

By the definition of
F^{5}
, we have:

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u\nu\cdot\tau\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}(\Sigma)}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}

which implies the estimate for
\|F^{5}\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}
.

Combining the estimates for
F^{1}
,
F^{4}
and
F^{5}
completes the proof.
∎

We now estimate the time derivatives of
F^{1},F^{4}
and
F^{5}
in
(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{*}
.

Theorem 5.2
.

It holds that

\|\partial_{t}(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{*}}\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{1/2}\mathscr{D}^{1/2}.

Proof.

We will mainly use duality as defined in (
2.10
).

Step 1 – Estimate for
\partial_{t}F^{1}
in
(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{*}

By definition of
F^{1}
, we first estimate the terms of the form
\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}^{i}\mathcal{A}}\nabla_{\partial_{t}^{j}\mathcal{A}}\partial_{t}^{k}u
included in
F^{1}
. It holds that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}S_{\mathcal{A}}(p,u))wJ\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}^{2}\mathcal{A}}S_{\mathcal{A}}(p,u)+\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}S_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}(p,u)+\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}S_{\mathcal{A}}(\partial_{t}p,\partial_{t}u))w\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\frac{2}{\alpha}}}(1+\|\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}})(\|p\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,q_{-}}}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}})\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,+\infty}}(\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}})(\|p\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,q_{-}}}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}})\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{-}}}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,+\infty}}(1+\|\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}})(\|\partial_{t}p\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}})\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{-}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}}\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}(u)wJ\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}(u)+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}}\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}^{2}\mathcal{A}}(u)+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}}\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}(\partial_{t}u))wJ\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,\infty}}(\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}})(\|u\|_{L^{+\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}})\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{-}}}}

\displaystyle+(1+\|\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,+\infty}})(\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}})(\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}})\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{+\infty}W^{1,+\infty}}(1+\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}})(\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}})\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{-}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.

We now estimate the nonlinear terms of the form
\partial_{t}^{i}u\cdot\nabla_{\partial_{t}^{j}\mathcal{A}}\partial_{t}^{k}u
included in
\partial_{t}F^{1}
. We have:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(u\cdot\nabla_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u)w=\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{t}u\cdot\nabla_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u)w+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(u\cdot\nabla_{\partial_{t}^{2}\mathcal{A}}u)w+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(u\cdot\nabla_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}\partial_{t}u)w

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\infty}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\frac{2}{\alpha}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{1-\alpha}}}

\displaystyle+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\infty}}\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}u\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}}u)w

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}^{2}u\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}\|{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}

\displaystyle\times\|u\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}(u\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}}\partial_{t}u)w

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}^{2}u\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}\|{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}

\displaystyle\times\|u\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.

Finally, we estimate the term
\partial_{t}^{2}(\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}WK\partial_{2}u)
involved in
F^{1}
. After decomposing
\partial_{t}^{2}(\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}WK\partial_{2}u)
, we first have the following computation

(5.6)
