\displaystyle{}\begin{aligned} \int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\eta}WK\partial_{2}u)w\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}}\|\partial_{2}u\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\|\omega\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{1+\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}^{3}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}},\\
\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\partial_{t}^{2}WK\partial_{2}u)w\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{2}u\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\frac{2}{\alpha}}}\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{1+\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}},\\
\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}W\partial_{t}^{2}K\partial_{2}u)w\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{2}u\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\frac{2}{\alpha}}}\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{1+\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}},\\
\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}WK\partial_{2}\partial_{t}^{2}u)w\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{2}\partial_{t}^{2}u\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{2}}\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.\end{aligned}

For other terms obtained from the decomposition of
\partial_{t}^{2}(\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}WK\partial_{2}u)
, we notice that they do not contain the highest-order temporal derivative terms. Therefore, they can be estimated via similar computation as in (
5.6
). We omit the details and only write the final result as follows

\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{2}(\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}WK\partial_{2}u)w\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.

From the discussion and computation above, we obtain the estimate for
F^{1}
in
(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{*}

\|\partial_{t}F^{1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{*}}\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Step 2 – Estimate for
\partial_{t}F^{4}
in
(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{*}

We first estimate the surface pressure terms included in
\partial_{t}F^{4}
.

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}(\mu\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u\mathcal{N})\cdot w=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(\mu\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}^{2}\mathcal{A}}u\mathcal{N})\cdot w+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(\mu\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}\partial_{t}u\mathcal{N})\cdot w+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(\mu\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u\partial_{t}\mathcal{N})\cdot w

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\frac{1}{\alpha}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)}\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\infty}}\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\times\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{-}}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\infty}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)}\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,+\infty}}\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{-}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}(\mu\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}}u\partial_{t}\mathcal{N})\cdot w\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.

We then estimate the terms including the capillary operator after decomposing
\partial_{t}F^{4}
. We have:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}(2\left[g(\eta)-\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}\eta}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|)^{3/2}}\right)-\sigma\partial_{1}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)\right]\partial_{t}\mathcal{N})\cdot w

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{2,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,+\infty}}\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{-}}}}+\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\frac{1}{\alpha}}}\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{-}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{-}}}}+\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{-}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.

Therefore, combining all the computations for
\partial_{t}F^{4}
above, we obtain

\|\partial_{t}F^{4}\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{*}}\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Step 3 – Estimate for
\partial_{t}F^{5}
in
(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{*}

We have the following estimate:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}(\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u\nu\cdot\tau)w=\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}^{2}\mathcal{A}}u\nu\cdot\tau)w+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}\partial_{t}u\nu\cdot\tau)w

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\frac{1}{\alpha}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)}\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{1,+\infty}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)}\|w\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{-}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.

Therefore, we obtain

\|\partial_{t}F^{5}\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{*}}\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Finally, combining Step 1 to Step 3, we obtain the following result.

\|\partial_{t}(F^{1}-F^{4}-F^{5})\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{*}}\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}},

which finishes the proof.
∎

We now estimate
F^{7}
via the following theorem.

Theorem 5.3
.

F^{7}
is defined in Appendix
A
. It holds that

\|[\partial_{t}F^{7}]_{\ell}\|_{L_{t}^{2}}\lesssim(1+T)\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Proof.

We have

\|[\kappa\partial_{t}(\hat{\mathscr{W}}^{\prime}(u\cdot\mathcal{N})\partial_{t}u\cdot\mathcal{N})]\|_{L_{t}^{2}}\lesssim\|[\partial_{t}u\cdot\mathcal{N}]_{\ell}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}}\|[\partial_{t}u\cdot\mathcal{N}]_{\ell}\|_{L_{t}^{2}}+\|[u\cdot\mathcal{N}]_{\ell}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}}\|[\partial_{t}^{2}u\cdot\mathcal{N}]\|_{L_{t}^{2}}\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

∎

Next, we establish the
q_{+}
boundedness for the nonlinear forcing terms by the following theorem.

Theorem 5.4
.

F^{1},F^{4},F^{5},F^{7}
are defined in Appendix
A
. They satisfy the following estimate

\|\int_{0}^{t}F^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{q_{+}}}+\|\int_{0}^{t}F^{4}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}+\|\int_{0}^{t}F^{5}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}+\|\int_{0}^{t}[F^{7}]_{\ell}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}}\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Proof.

Step 1 – Estimate for
F^{1}

By the definition of
F^{1}
and the temporal integration by parts, we have

\displaystyle\|\int_{0}^{t}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}S_{\mathcal{A}}(p,u)\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{q_{+}}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1,\infty}}(1+\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})(\|p\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1,q_{+}}}+\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}(1+\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})(\|p\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1,q_{+}}}+\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\|\int_{0}^{t}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}}S_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}(p,u)\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{q_{+}}}\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|\nabla^{2}\bar{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{+}}}})(\|\partial_{t}p\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}+\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{1,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}})

\displaystyle+T(\|\partial_{t}\nabla\bar{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{\infty}})(\|p\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1,q_{+}}}+\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T\|\partial_{t}{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}(\|p\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1,q_{+}}}+\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}})

\displaystyle+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}(\|\partial_{t}p\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+T)\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

For the remaining terms involved in
\int_{0}^{t}F^{1}
, they can be bounded as follows

\displaystyle\|u\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}}u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{q_{+}}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1,q_{+}}}\lesssim\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}WK\partial_{2}u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{q_{+}}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}}\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Step 2 – Estimates for
F^{4}
and
F^{5}

Noticing that
\int_{0}^{t}F^{5}
has a similar form compared to
\int_{0}^{t}F^{4}
, we only give the detailed estimate of
\int_{0}^{t}F^{4}
here. We have

\displaystyle\|\int_{0}^{t}S_{\mathcal{A}}(p,u)\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}(1+\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})(\|p\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1,q_{+}}}+\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}})

\displaystyle\lesssim T\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\|\int_{0}^{t}S_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}(p,u)\mathcal{N}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}(1+\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})(\|p\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1,q_{+}}}+\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}})

\displaystyle\lesssim T\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle\|\int_{0}^{t}(\mathcal{K}(\eta)-\partial_{1}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta))\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}(\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})\lesssim T\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Step 3 – Estimate for
F^{7}

By the definition of
F^{7}
, we have

\|\int_{0}^{t}F^{7}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}}=\|\hat{\mathcal{W}}(u\cdot\mathcal{N})(\pm\ell)\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}}\lesssim\|[u\cdot\mathcal{N}]_{\pi}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}}\lesssim\mathscr{E}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathscr{D}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Combining Step 1 – Step 3, the theorem is proved.
∎

5.2.
Initial Data for the Nonlinear System

We assume that the initial data

(5.7)

\displaystyle(u_{0},p_{0},\eta_{0},\partial_{t}u(0),\partial_{t}p(0),\partial_{t}\eta(0),\partial_{t}^{2}u(0),\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0))

for (
1.7
) are in the space
X
defined via

\displaystyle X:=W^{2,q_{+}}(\Omega)\times W^{1,q_{+}}(\Omega)\times W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma)\times H^{1+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}(\Omega)\times H^{\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}(\Omega)\times H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}(\Sigma)\times L^{2}(\Omega)\times H^{1}(\Sigma),

and satisfy the compatibility conditions (
1.12
)
as well as zero average conditions (
1.13
),
where
X
is a Banach space, with the square norm

\displaystyle\|(u_{0},p_{0},\eta_{0},\partial_{t}u(0),\partial_{t}p(0),\partial_{t}\eta(0),\partial_{t}^{2}u(0),\partial_{t}^{2}p(0),\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0),\partial_{t}^{3}u(0),\partial_{t}^{3}\eta(0))\|_{X^{\varepsilon}}^{2}=\mathcal{E}(0),

where
\mathcal{E}(0)
is defined via (
1.10
).
From Theorem
B.1
, we have known that our initial data set is non-empty, provided that
\|\partial_{t}^{2}u(0)\|_{H^{0}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)\|_{W^{2-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2}
is sufficiently small.

5.3.
Existence and Uniqueness of Solution

We now consider the local well-posedness of the nonlinear problem (
1.7
). Our strategy is to work in a complete metric space that imposes high-regularity bounds while being equipped with a metric involving only low-regularity norms, and then to show that a fixed point in this space yields a solution to (
1.7
).

We now define the desired metric space.

Definition 5.1
.

Suppose that
T>0
. For
\delta\in(0,\infty)
we define the space

\displaystyle S(T,\delta)

\displaystyle=\Big\{(u,p,\eta):\Omega\to\mathbb{R}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}\Big|(u,p,\eta)\in\mathcal{X}\cap\mathcal{Y},\ \text{with}\ \mathfrak{K}(u,p,\eta)^{1/2}\leq\delta

\displaystyle\text{and}\ (u,p,\eta)\ \text{achieve the initial data in Appendix \ref{sec:initial}}\Big\}.

We endow this space with the metric

(5.8)

\displaystyle d((u,p,\eta),(v,q,\xi))

\displaystyle=\|u-v\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u-v\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}u-\partial_{t}v\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{1}(\|\partial_{t}^{i}u-\partial_{t}^{i}v\|_{L^{\infty}H^{0}})+\|p-q\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}+\|p-q\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{0}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{1}\|\partial_{t}^{i}\eta-\partial_{t}^{i}\xi\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\eta-\xi\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3/2-\alpha}}+\|\eta-\xi\|_{L^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-}.q_{-}}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\eta-\partial_{t}\xi\|_{L^{2}H^{3/2-\alpha}}+\sum_{i=0}^{1}\|[\partial_{t}^{i}\eta-\partial_{t}^{i}\xi]_{\ell}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T])}+\|[\partial_{t}^{2}\eta-\partial_{t}^{2}\xi]_{\ell}\|_{L^{2}([0,T])},

for any
(u,p,\eta),(v,q,\xi)\in S(T,\delta)
, where the temporal norm is evaluated on the set
[0,T]
.

Remark 5.1
.

In the metric defined above, we only require the
q_{-}
elliptic regularity. This is enough for the energy estimate. We do not require
q_{+}
since the integral structure prevents us from deriving a
q_{+}
estimate for the difference functions.

For
\mathcal{X}
and
\mathcal{Y}
defined in (
5.1
), the subset satisfying
\mathfrak{K}(u,p,\eta)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\delta
is weak* compact in
\mathcal{X}
and weak compact in
\mathcal{Y}
. Thus it is easy to see that the space
S(T,\delta)
is complete for each fixed
\delta
.

Now we employ the metric space
S(T,\delta)
and a contraction mapping argument to construct the solutions
(u,p,\eta)
. The procedure is completed by several steps:

•

Firstly, we choose an appropriate
\delta
to determine the working space
S(T,\delta)
.

•

Secondly, we define the iterative operator from
S(T,\delta)
to
S(T,\delta)
. In order to show that the operator is a contraction map in the following steps, we give the PDEs of differences.

•

Thirdly, we use the energy method to control first time derivative of velocity and surface function in the energy space.

•

In the fourth step, we improve the regularity of first time derivative of surface function so that the first time derivative of velocity and surface function are controlled under the metric of
S(T,\delta)
.

•

In the fifth step, we give elliptic estimate for velocity and surface function in low regularity under the metric of
S(T,\delta)
.

•

Finally, we combine the results of above steps to give the contraction.

Theorem 5.5
.

There exists a universal constant
\delta>0
which is sufficiently small such that if the initial data for the nonlinear system are given as in Section
5.2
satisfying

\|(u_{0},p_{0},\eta_{0},\partial_{t}u(0),\partial_{t}p(0),\partial_{t}\eta(0),\partial_{t}^{2}u(0),\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)\|_{X}^{2}\leq\delta
,
then there exists a unique solution
(u,p,\eta)
to (
1.7
), belonging to the metric space
S(T,\delta)
, where
T>0
is sufficiently small. In particular
(u,p,\eta)\in\mathcal{X}\cap\mathcal{Y}
, where
\mathcal{X}
and
\mathcal{Y}
are defined in (
5.1
).

Proof.

Step 1 – Solving the Linear Problem

Suppose that
\mathfrak{K}(u,p,\eta)\leq\delta^{2}
is sufficiently small. For every
(u,p,\eta)\in S(T,\delta)
given, let
(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})
instead of
(u,p,\xi)
be the unique solution to the linear problem (
1.16
).
Then we apply the Theorem
4.5
to the system (
1.16
), with the Propositions
5.1
–
5.4
under the assumption on
\mathfrak{K}(u,p,\eta)
to obtain that

\mathfrak{K}(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})\leq C_{0}\Big[1+T\Big]e^{T\delta}\left(C_{1}\mathcal{E}(0)+C_{2}\Big(1+{T}\Big)\delta^{4}\right),

for some universal constants
C_{0}
,
C_{1}
and
C_{2}
.

We first choose
T
such that
T\leq 1
. Then we choose
\delta\in(0,1)
such that
4C_{0}C_{2}e^{\delta}\delta^{2}\leq\frac{1}{2}
. Finally, we restrict the initial data
\mathcal{E}(0)
so that
4C_{0}C_{1}e\mathcal{E}(0)\leq\frac{1}{2}\delta^{2}
. Consequently, we obtain
\mathfrak{K}(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})\leq\delta^{2}
, which implies that
(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})\in S(T,\delta)
.

Step 2 – PDEs for the Differences

Define the operator
A:S(T,\delta)\rightarrow S(T,\delta)
via

(5.9)

A(u,p,\eta)=(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})\ \text{for each}\ (u,p,\eta)\in S(T,\delta).

In order to show that the operator
A
is contract on
S(T,\delta)
, we choose two elements
(u^{i},p^{i},\eta^{i})\in S(T,\delta)
,
and define
A(u^{i},p^{i},\eta^{i})=(\tilde{u}^{i},\tilde{p}^{i},\tilde{\eta}^{i})
as above,
i=1,2
. For simplicity, we will abuse notation and denote
u=u^{1}-u^{2}
,
p=p^{1}-p^{2}
,
\eta=\eta^{1}-\eta^{2}
and the same for
\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}
. From the difference of equation for
(\tilde{u}^{i},\tilde{p}^{i},\tilde{\eta}^{i})
,
i=1,2
, we know that
the solutions are regular enough to be differentiated in time to result in the following equation

(5.10)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}\tilde{u})+\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\partial_{t}\tilde{p},\partial_{t}\tilde{u})=-\mu\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(S_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}({u}^{2},{p}^{2}))+R^{1,1}\ &\text{in}&\ \Omega,\\
&\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}=R^{2,1}\ &\text{in}&\ \Omega,\\
&S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\partial_{t}\tilde{p},\partial_{t}\tilde{u})\mathcal{N}^{1}=\mu\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}\tilde{u}^{2}\mathcal{N}^{1}+g(\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})\mathcal{N}^{1}-\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\right)\mathcal{N}^{1}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-\sigma\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})\mathcal{N}^{1}(t)\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-\sigma\partial_{1}((\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})\mathcal{N}^{1}(t)+R^{4,1}\ &\text{on}&\ \Sigma,\\
&(S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\partial_{t}\tilde{p},\partial_{t}\tilde{u})\nu-\beta\partial_{t}\tilde{u})\cdot\tau=\mu\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}{u}^{2}\nu\cdot\tau+R^{5,1}\ &\text{on}&\ \Sigma_{s},\\
&\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\nu=0\ &\text{on}&\ \Sigma_{s},\\
&\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})=\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}+R^{6,1}+u_{1}^{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}+u_{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\ &\text{on}&\ \Sigma,\\
&\kappa(\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{1}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{2})(\pm\ell,t)=\mp\sigma\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}(\pm\ell,t)\mp\sigma\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\mp\sigma\partial_{1}((\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})-R^{7,1}\end{aligned}\right.

with zero initial data, where
R^{1},R^{4},R^{7}
,
R^{1,1}
,
R^{2,1}
,
R^{4,1}
,
R^{5,1}
,
R^{6,1}
,
R^{7,1}
are defined by

(5.11)

\displaystyle R^{1,1}

\displaystyle=\partial_{t}R^{1}+\mu\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}(S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}({u},{p}))+\mu\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}(S_{(\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2})}({u}^{2},{p}^{2}))

\displaystyle+\mu\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(S_{(\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1})}({u},{p}))+\mu\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}S_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}S(u^{2},p^{2})

\displaystyle+\mu\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{2}}(p,u)+\mu\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}(\partial_{t}\tilde{p}^{2},\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2})+\mu\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\partial_{t}\tilde{p}^{2},\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}),

\displaystyle R^{2,1}

\displaystyle=-(\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}{u}^{2})-(\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2})-\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}u,

\displaystyle R^{4,1}

\displaystyle=\partial_{t}R^{4}+\mu S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}({u},{p})\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}+\mu S_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}({u},{p})\mathcal{N}^{1}+g({\eta})\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}-\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}{\eta}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\right)\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}

\displaystyle-\sigma\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{1})(\mathcal{N}^{1}-\mathcal{N}^{2})-\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{t}(\mathcal{N}^{1}-\mathcal{N}^{2})

\displaystyle-(\partial_{1}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})-\partial_{1}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2}))\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{2}+\mu S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}(\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2},\partial_{t}\tilde{p}^{2})\mathcal{N}^{2}+\mu S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}({u}^{2},{p}^{2})\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{2}

\displaystyle+g({\eta}^{2})(\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{2})-\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}{\eta}^{2}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\right)(\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{2})

\displaystyle+g(\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})(\mathcal{N}^{1}-\mathcal{N}^{2})-\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}{\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}}^{2}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\right)(\mathcal{N}^{1}-\mathcal{N}^{2}),

\displaystyle R^{5,1}

\displaystyle=-S_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}({p},{u})+\mu S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}(\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2},\partial_{t}\tilde{p}^{2}),

\displaystyle\quad R^{6,1}

\displaystyle=\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\cdot(\mathcal{N}^{1}-\mathcal{N}^{2}),

\displaystyle\quad R^{7,1}

\displaystyle=\partial_{t}R^{7},

where

(5.12)

\displaystyle R^{1}=

\displaystyle u\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}u^{1}+u^{2}\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}u^{1}+u^{2}\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{2}}u+\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}W^{1}K^{1}\partial_{2}u^{1}+\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}(W^{1}-W^{2})K^{1}\partial_{2}u^{1}

\displaystyle+\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}W^{2}(K^{1}-K^{2})\partial_{2}u^{1}+\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}W^{2}K^{2}\partial_{2}u^{1},

\displaystyle R^{4}=

\displaystyle S_{\mathcal{A}^{2}}(\tilde{p}^{2},\tilde{u}^{2})(\mathcal{N}^{1}-\mathcal{N}^{2}),

\displaystyle R^{7}=

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{W}}(\partial_{t}\eta^{1})-\hat{\mathcal{W}}(\partial_{t}\eta^{2}).

Step 3 – Introducing the Test Function

In this step, we introduce the test function.
We begin by introducing the following notations about the metrics.

d(u,p,\eta):=d((u^{1},p^{1},\eta^{1}),(u^{2},p^{2},\eta^{2})),\quad d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}):=d((\tilde{u}^{1},\tilde{p}^{1},\tilde{\eta}^{1}),(\tilde{u}^{2},\tilde{p}^{2},\tilde{\eta}^{2})).

We then introduce
w\in H_{0}^{1}(\Omega)
that solves the following equation

(5.13)

\displaystyle{}\begin{aligned} J^{1}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}w=J^{1}R^{2,1}-\left<J^{1}R^{2,1}\right>_{\Omega},~~~~\operatorname{where}~\left<J^{1}R^{2,1}\right>_{\Omega}=\frac{1}{|\Omega|}\int_{\Omega}J^{1}R^{2,1}.\end{aligned}

We refer to
[
10
, Section 10]
for the details of solvability of (
5.13
) and the corresponding estimate for
w
.

Step 4 – Energy Estimates for
(\partial_{t}\tilde{u},\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})

In this step, we aim to establish the energy estimates for
(\partial_{t}\tilde{u},\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})
. Multiplying the first equation of (
5.10
) by
J^{1}(\partial_{t}\tilde{u}-w)
, integrating over
\Omega
and using integration by parts, we obtain the following equation

(5.14)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|^{2}}{2}J^{1}+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\frac{g}{2}|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}|^{2}+\frac{\sigma}{2}\frac{|\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}|^{2}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}-\sigma\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})(\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})^{2}-\int_{\Omega}J^{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}w\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\mu}{2}\int_{\Omega}|\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|^{2}J^{1}+\beta\int_{\Sigma_{s}}|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|^{2}J^{1}+[\partial_{t}{u}^{1}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{t}u^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{2}]_{\ell}^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|^{2}}{2}\partial_{t}J^{1}-\frac{\mu}{2}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}{u}^{2}\right):\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\partial_{t}\tilde{u}-w)J^{1}+\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\partial_{t}(J^{1}w)+\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\tilde{p}\langle R^{2,1}J^{1}\rangle

\displaystyle-\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}R^{4,1}\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}+\partial_{1}R^{6,1}(\sigma\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{\frac{3}{2}})^{\frac{3}{2}}}-\sigma(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}))

\displaystyle+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(R^{6,1})(g\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{\eta})+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(\partial_{1}R^{6,1})\sigma(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})

\displaystyle+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{1}(u_{1}^{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}+u_{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})((g\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}+\sigma\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{\frac{3}{2}})^{\frac{3}{2}}}-\sigma(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})))

\displaystyle+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{1}(u^{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}+u\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})(\sigma((\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}))

\displaystyle+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\sigma((\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})+\int_{\Omega}R^{1,1}\cdot(\partial_{t}\tilde{u}J^{1}-wJ^{1})

\displaystyle+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}R^{5,1}\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}-[\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1},R^{7,1}]_{\ell}-[\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{u}^{1}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{2},\partial_{t}u^{2}\cdot(\mathcal{N}^{2}-\mathcal{N}^{1})]_{\ell}.

We now estimate each term on the right-hand side of (
5.14
). For the first line of the RHS of (
5.14
), using Hölder’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|^{2}}{2}\partial_{t}J^{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1,\infty}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{t}{\eta}^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})d(u,\eta,p),

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\mu}{2}(\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}u^{2}):\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\partial_{t}\tilde{u})J^{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|u^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})d(u,\eta,p).

Terms related to
R^{1}

For terms in the bulk, by definition in (
5.11
), we first handle terms involving
\partial_{t}R^{1}
as follows

(5.15)

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}R^{1}\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}J^{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\Omega}((|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}|+|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}|)|\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}|+|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}^{2}||\nabla\bar{\eta}|)|\partial_{2}u^{1}||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|+\int_{\Omega}((|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}|+|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}|)|\nabla\bar{\eta}|)|\partial_{2}\partial_{t}u^{1}||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|

\displaystyle+\int_{\Omega}((|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}|+|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}|)|\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}|+(|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}^{1}|+|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}^{2}|)|\nabla\bar{\eta}^{1}|)|\partial_{2}u||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|+\int_{\Omega}((|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}|+|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}|)|\nabla\bar{\eta}^{1}|)|\partial_{2}\partial_{t}u||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|

\displaystyle+\int_{\Omega}((|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}|)|\nabla\bar{\eta}^{2}|)|\partial_{2}u^{2}||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|+\int_{\Omega}((|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}|)|\nabla\bar{\eta}^{2}|)|\partial_{2}\partial_{t}u^{2}||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|+((|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}|)|\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}|)|\partial_{2}u^{2}||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|

\displaystyle+\int_{\Omega}(|\partial_{t}u|+|u||\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}|)|\nabla u^{1}||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|+\int_{\Omega}(|\partial_{t}u|)|\nabla\partial_{t}u^{1}||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|+\int_{\Omega}(|u^{1}||\nabla\bar{\eta}|)|\nabla\partial_{t}u^{1}||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|

\displaystyle+\int_{\Omega}(|\partial_{t}u^{2}|+|u^{2}|\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}||)|\nabla u||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|+(u^{2})|\nabla\partial_{t}u||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|+\int_{\Omega}(|\partial_{t}u^{1}||\nabla\bar{\eta}|+|u^{1}||\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}|)|\nabla u||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|,

where we have used
\|\mathcal{A}^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|J^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|K^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 1
,
i=1,2
.
Then by Hölder’s inequality, Sobolev embedding and trace theory, after integrating over
[0,T]
, the second line in (
5.15
) is bounded as follows

(5.16)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2/\alpha}}((\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\nabla\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L^{2}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2/(1-\alpha)}}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\partial_{t}\nabla\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L^{q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2/\varepsilon_{-}}}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}}(\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\nabla\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L^{q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{2}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}})\|u^{2}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}\|\eta\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{2}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}})\|\partial_{t}u^{2}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}\|\eta\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}(\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{1}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{2}\|_{H^{1}})\|u^{2}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{1/2}(\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}H^{3/2-\alpha}}+\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.

The third line in (
5.15
), after an integration over
[0,T]
, can be estimated similarly by

(5.17)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{2/\alpha}}((\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{2}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2/(1-\alpha)}}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{4/(\alpha-\varepsilon_{-})}}(\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\nabla\partial_{t}{u}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2/(1+\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)}}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}}(\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\partial_{t}\nabla{u}\|_{L^{q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{\frac{2}{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{2}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}})\|u\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}\|\eta^{1}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{2}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}})\|u\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}\|\eta^{1}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{2}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}})\|\partial_{t}u\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{1/2}(\|u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}H^{1}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.

For the fourth line in (
5.15
), we can use similar computation to show that it is bounded by:

(5.18)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\bar{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}((\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}})\|\nabla{u}^{2}\|_{L^{2}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2/(1-\alpha)}}+\|\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\nabla{u}^{2}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\quad+\|\nabla\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|\partial_{t}\nabla{u}^{2}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta\|_{1/2-\alpha}(\|\eta^{2}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}})\|u^{2}\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}+\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{2}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}(\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}})\|u^{2}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}\\
&\quad+\int_{0}^{T}\|\eta^{1}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}(\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}})\|\partial_{t}u^{2}\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}\\
&\lesssim\delta^{1/2}(\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}},\end{aligned}

where we have used the Sobolev embedding
H^{\varepsilon_{-}/2}\hookrightarrow L^{4/(2-\varepsilon_{-})}
and
H^{1}\hookrightarrow L^{4/(2\varepsilon_{+}+\varepsilon_{-}-2\alpha)}
for bounded domain
\Omega
.
The fifth line in (
5.15
), after an integration over
[0,T]
(without loss of generality, we may assume
T<1
), is estimated by

(5.19)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}(\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{\frac{4}{\varepsilon_{-}}}}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{W^{1,\infty}})\|u^{1}\|_{W^{1,\frac{4}{2-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{H^{1}}+\int_{0}^{T}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{t}u^{1}\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}(\|u^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\bar{\eta}\|_{W^{1,\infty}})\|\partial_{t}u^{1}\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}+\|u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.

After an integration over
[0,T]
, the last line in (
5.15
) can be similarly bounded by

(5.20)

\displaystyle\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}u\|_{L^{2}H^{1}})\|\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.

We omit the details here.
Combining (
5.15
)–(
5.20
), we obtain the following estimate

(5.21)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}R^{1}\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}J^{1}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}d(u,p,\eta)d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}).

Other terms in
R^{1,1}

We now estimate other terms involved in expression of
R^{1,1}
such that

(5.22)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\mathbb{D}_{(\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2})}{u}^{2})\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}J^{1}\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}|\left(|\nabla^{2}\bar{\eta}||\nabla{u}^{2}|+|\nabla\bar{\eta}||\nabla^{2}{u}^{2}|\right)|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\nabla^{2}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}}\|\nabla{u}^{2}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{1/\varepsilon_{-}}}+\|\nabla\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}{u}^{2}\|_{L^{q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2/\varepsilon_{-}}})

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}\|\eta\|_{W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}\|{u}^{2}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}\|{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}\lesssim\delta\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}.

Then using the similar estimate, we obtain the following estimate

(5.23)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\mathbb{D}_{(\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2})}\partial_{t}{u}^{2})\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}J^{1}\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\left(|\nabla^{2}\bar{\eta}||\partial_{t}\nabla{u}^{2}|+|\nabla\bar{\eta}||\partial_{t}\nabla^{2}{u}^{2}|\right)|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}(\|\nabla^{2}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}}\|\partial_{t}\nabla{u}^{2}\|_{L^{4/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{1/\varepsilon_{-}}}+\|\nabla\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{t}\nabla^{2}{u}^{2}\|_{L^{q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2/\varepsilon_{-}}})

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\|\eta\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}{u}^{2}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}+\int_{0}^{T}\|\eta\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}u^{2}\|_{H^{1+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}+\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta(\|\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}+\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.

Next, we have the following estimate

(5.24)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(\mu\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}{u})\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}J^{1}\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\left(|\partial_{t}\nabla^{2}\bar{\eta}^{1}||\nabla{u}|+|\partial_{t}\nabla\bar{\eta}^{1}||\nabla^{2}{u}|\right)|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\big[\|\partial_{t}\nabla^{2}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}}\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{4/(2-\varepsilon_{-})}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{4/(3\varepsilon_{-})}}+\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{W^{1,+\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}{u}\|_{L^{q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2/\varepsilon_{-}}}\big]

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}\|{u}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}+\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|u\|_{H^{1+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{1/2}(\|{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}.

Similarly, it holds that

(5.25)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(\mu\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}{u})\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}J^{1}\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{t}\nabla\bar{\eta}^{1}|\left(|\nabla^{2}\bar{\eta}^{1}||\nabla{u}|+|\nabla^{2}{u}|\right)|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{t}\nabla\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big[\|\nabla^{2}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}}\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{1/\varepsilon_{-}}}+\|\nabla^{2}{u}\|_{L^{q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2/\varepsilon_{-}}}\big]\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}\big[(1+\|\eta^{1}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}})\|{u}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}\big]\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}\\
&\lesssim\delta^{1/2}(\|{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}.\end{aligned}

Finally, we have the following estimates

(5.26)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(\mu\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}(\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{2}}{u}^{2})-\nabla_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}{p}^{2})\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}J^{1}\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}|\left(|\nabla^{2}\bar{\eta}^{2}||\nabla{u}^{2}|+|\nabla^{2}{u}^{2}|+|\nabla{p}^{2}|\right)|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}}\big[\|\nabla^{2}\bar{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}}\|\nabla{u}^{2}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{1/\varepsilon_{-}-\frac{\alpha}{2}}}+(\|\nabla^{2}{u}^{2}\|_{L^{q_{-}}}+\|\nabla{p}^{2}\|_{L^{q_{-}}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2/\varepsilon_{-}}}\big]

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\big[(1+\|\eta^{2}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}})\|{u}^{2}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|{p}^{2}\|_{W^{1,q_{-}}}\big]\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\big[(1+\|\eta^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}})\|{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|{p}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1,q_{-}}}\big]\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{1/2}\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}},

and

(5.27)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(\mu\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}(\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}{u}^{2}))\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}J^{1}\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla\bar{\eta}|\left(|\partial_{t}\nabla^{2}\bar{\eta}^{2}||\nabla{u}^{2}|+|\partial_{t}\nabla\bar{\eta}^{2}||\nabla^{2}{u}^{2}|\right)|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{+\infty}}\big[\|\partial_{t}\nabla^{2}\bar{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}}\|\nabla{u}^{2}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{1/\varepsilon_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}\nabla\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\nabla^{2}{u}^{2}\|_{L^{q_{-}}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2/\varepsilon_{-}}}\big]

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\|\eta\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\big[(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{2}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}})\|{u}^{2}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}\big]\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\big[(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}})\|{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\big]\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{1/2}\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}},

and

(5.28)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(\mu\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}(\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{2}}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2})+\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}\partial_{t}\tilde{p}^{2})\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}J^{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla\bar{\eta}|\left(|\nabla^{2}\bar{\eta}^{2}||\partial_{t}\nabla\tilde{u}^{2}|+|\nabla\bar{\eta}^{2}||\partial_{t}\nabla^{2}\tilde{u}^{2}|+|\nabla\partial_{t}\tilde{p}^{2}|\right)|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big[\|\nabla^{2}\bar{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\nabla\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{1/\varepsilon_{-}}}+(1+\|\nabla\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}})(\|\partial_{t}\nabla^{2}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L^{q_{-}}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\nabla\tilde{p}^{2}\|_{L^{q_{-}}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2/\varepsilon_{-}}}\big]

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\|\eta\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\big[(1+\|\eta^{2}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}})(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}\tilde{p}^{2}\|_{W^{1,q_{-}}})\big]\|\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{u}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\big[(1+\|\eta^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}})(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}\tilde{p}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}})\big]\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{1/2}\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}.

Hence, combining the estimate (
5.15
) and estimates (
5.22
) to (
5.28
), we obtain the following result

(5.29)

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}R^{1,1}\cdot\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{u}J^{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{1/2}d(u,p,\eta)d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}).

Terms related to
R^{4,1}
and
R^{5,1}

We now estimate integrals involving
R^{4,1}
. We first estimate the terms included in
\partial_{t}R^{4}
(In the following estimate, we use
\tilde{u}
to denote the trace of
\tilde{u}
). By definition, we have

(5.30)

\displaystyle|\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}R^{4}\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\left[(|\partial_{t}{p}^{2}|+|\nabla\partial_{t}{u}^{2}|+|\nabla\partial_{t}{\eta}^{2}||\nabla{u}^{2}|)|\partial_{1}\eta|\right]|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(|{p}^{2}|+|\nabla{u}^{2}|)|\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|.

The first and second lines on the right-hand side of (
5.30
), using the Hölder and Sobolev inequalities, are estimated by

(5.31)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\bigg[(\|\partial_{t}{p}^{2}\|_{L^{1/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}(\Sigma)}+\|\nabla\partial_{t}{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla{u}^{2}\|_{L^{1/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}(\Sigma)}+\|\nabla\partial_{t}{u}^{2}\|_{L^{1/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}(\Sigma)})\bigg]\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{1/(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)}(\Sigma)}\times\|\partial_{1}\eta\|_{L^{1/\alpha}}

\displaystyle+(\|{p}^{2}\|_{L^{1/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}(\Sigma)}+\|\nabla{u}^{2}\|_{L^{1/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}(\Sigma)})\|\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{1/\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{1/(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\bigg[(\|\partial_{t}{p}^{2}\|_{W^{1,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{2}\|_{H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}\|{u}^{2}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}{u}^{2}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}})\bigg]\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}\|\eta\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}

\displaystyle+(\|{p}^{2}\|_{W^{1,q_{-}}}+\|{u}^{2}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}})\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{1/2}(\|\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}H^{3/2-\alpha}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.

Therefore, we obtain

(5.32)

\displaystyle|\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}R^{4}\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|\lesssim\delta^{1/2}d(u,p,\eta)d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}).

Similarly, we estimate other terms involved in
R^{4,1}
, we have

(5.33)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(u,p)\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\lesssim&\int_{0}^{T}(\|u\|_{W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)}+\|p\|_{L^{\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)})\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{W^{1,+\infty}(\Sigma)}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|p\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}},\\
\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}S_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}(u,p)\mathcal{N}^{1}\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\lesssim&\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|u\|_{W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)}+\|p\|_{L^{\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)})\|\eta^{1}\|_{W^{1,+\infty}(\Sigma)}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|p\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,q_{-}}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}},\end{aligned}

and similarly we have

(5.34)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}{u}^{2}\mathcal{N}^{2}\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\lesssim&\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{W^{1,\frac{1}{\alpha}}}\|{u}^{2}\|_{W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{-}}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}},\\
\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\mathcal{N}^{2}\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\lesssim&\int_{0}^{T}\|\eta\|_{W^{1,\frac{1}{\alpha}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}},\\
\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}{u}^{2}\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{2}\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\lesssim&\int_{0}^{T}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{2}\|_{W^{1,+\infty}}\|\eta\|_{W^{1,\infty}}\|{u}^{2}\|_{W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{-}}}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}\eta^{2}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}{}}}\|{u}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}\\
\lesssim&\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}},\end{aligned}

and

(5.35)

\displaystyle|\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\bigg[g({\eta})\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}-\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}{\eta}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|)^{3/2}}\right)\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta))\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}\bigg]\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(|{\eta}|+|\partial_{1}{\eta}|+|\partial_{1}^{2}{\eta}|)|\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\eta^{1}||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}(\|{\eta}\|_{W^{2,1/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}})\|\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{1/\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{1/(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}.

Moreover, using the fact that
\|\partial_{1}\eta^{i}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 1
and the definition of
\mathcal{R}_{z}
, we obtain the following estimates

(5.36)

\displaystyle|\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{1}\bigg[-\sigma\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2}))\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{2}\bigg]\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(|\partial_{1}^{2}\eta|)|\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\eta^{2}||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}(\|\partial_{1}^{2}\eta\|_{W^{1,1/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{2}\|_{W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}})\|\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\eta^{2}\|_{L^{1/\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{1/(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}(\|\eta\|_{W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}})\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{3/2+}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}(\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}},

and

(5.37)

\displaystyle|\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\bigg[\mathcal{K}(\eta^{1})-\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\bigg]\partial_{t}(\mathcal{N}^{1}-\mathcal{N}^{2})\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(|\partial_{1}^{2}\eta^{1}|)|\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\eta||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}(\|\eta^{1}\|_{W^{2,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}})\|\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\eta^{2}\|_{L^{1/\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{1/(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}(\|\eta^{1}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}})\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}(\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}},

and

(5.38)

\displaystyle{}\begin{aligned} &|\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\bigg[\mathcal{K}(\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{1})-\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{1})\bigg](\mathcal{N}^{1}-\mathcal{N}^{2})\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(|\partial_{1}^{2}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{1}|+|\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{1}|)|\partial_{1}\eta||\partial_{t}\tilde{u}|\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}(\|\tilde{\eta}^{1}\|_{W^{2,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}+\|\tilde{\eta}^{1}\|_{W^{1,+\infty}})\|\partial_{1}\eta\|_{L^{1/\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{1/(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)}}\\
&\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}(\|\tilde{\eta}^{1}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}})\|\eta\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{1}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}(\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}},\end{aligned}

Therefore, by estimate (
5.32
), and estimates (
5.36
) to (
5.38
), we derive the following estimate for
R^{4,1}

(5.39)

\displaystyle{}\begin{aligned} \|R^{4,1}\|_{L_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{\ast}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(u,\eta,p),\end{aligned}

and

(5.40)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}R^{4,1}\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}d(u,p,\eta)d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}).

We use similar methods to deal with the terms involving
R^{5,1}
since the terms in
R^{5,1}
are all included in
R^{4,1}
. We have

(5.41)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} |\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}R^{5,1}\cdot\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}d(u,p,\eta)d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})\end{aligned}

Terms related to
R^{6,1}

We now estimate the integrals involving
R^{6,1}
. First, using spatial integration by parts, we have

(5.42)

\displaystyle|\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}g(\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})R^{6,1}-\sigma\left(\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}(\tilde{\eta})}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\right)\partial_{1}R^{6,1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}|+|\partial_{1}\partial_{t}(\tilde{\eta})|)(|\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\tilde{u}_{1}^{2}||\partial_{1}\eta|+|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}_{1}^{2}||\partial_{1}^{2}\eta|+|\partial_{1}u_{1}||\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}|+|u_{1}||\partial_{1}^{2}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}|^{2}+|\partial_{1}u_{1}^{2}||\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}|)

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\frac{1}{\alpha}}})(\|{u}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{1/(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)}(\Sigma)}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+\|{u}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{2,1/(1-\varepsilon_{-})}}+\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1,+\infty}}+\|\partial_{1}{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}(\Sigma)}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,\frac{1}{\alpha}}})

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{1/2}(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}})(\|{u}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}+\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}+\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{3/2-\alpha}})

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})(d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})+d({u},{\eta},{p})).

Moreover, the computation of (
5.42
) implies that:

\|R^{6,1}\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})+d({u},{\eta},{p})).

For the following term including
R^{6,1}
, since

\displaystyle\|\partial_{1}((\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}+\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p}),

it holds that

(5.43)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{1}((\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}^{3}\tilde{\eta}^{2})dsR^{6,1}dxdt

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{1}((\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})\mathcal{N}^{1}(t)\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}}\|R^{6,1}\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})+d(u,\eta,p))^{2}.

Then we obtain the following bound:

(5.44)

\displaystyle\text{Terms with}~~R^{6,1}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})(d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})+d({u},{\eta},{p})).

Other Boundary Terms

We now estimate all of the remaining boundary terms. From the computation of terms including
R^{6,1}
, we have the following result

\displaystyle\|(g\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})-\sigma(\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}})\|_{L^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}}+\|\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}}

\displaystyle+\|(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(u,p,\eta)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})).

Therefore, to bound the following integral

(5.45)

\displaystyle{}\begin{aligned} &\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{1}(u_{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})\left(g\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}+\sigma\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}-\sigma(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})\right)\mathcal{N}^{1}(t)\\
&\quad+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{1}(u_{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})\sigma\left(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}\right)\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\mathcal{N}^{1}(t),\end{aligned}

it suffices to show the following estimate:

\displaystyle\|\partial_{1}(u_{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{1}u_{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}}+\|u_{1}\partial_{1}^{2}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}+\|u_{1}\partial_{1}^{2}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}+\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}},

which implies that

(5.46)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\partial_{1}u_{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{1}{1-\alpha}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}})\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(u,\eta,p).\end{aligned}

Therefore , we have

\eqref{eq:complex_1}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d^{2}(u,\eta,p).

Then we estimate the following integral

(5.47)

\displaystyle{}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(u_{1}^{1}\partial_{1}^{2}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})\left(g\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}+\sigma\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}-\sigma\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\right).

For simplicity, we only show the details of estimating the most difficult part

\left|\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(u_{1}^{1}\partial_{1}^{2}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})\sigma\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\right|.

in (
5.47
).
Using spatial integration by parts (noting that the boundary terms vanish since
u_{1}^{1}=0
), we have

\displaystyle|\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(u_{1}^{1}\partial_{1}^{2}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})((\sigma(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}))|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}|\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{1}u_{1}^{1}\sigma\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})(\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})^{2}|+\frac{1}{2}|\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}u_{1}^{1}\sigma\partial_{1}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})(\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})^{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|u^{1}\|_{W^{1,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}]}}(\Sigma)}\|\eta^{1}\|_{W^{1,+\infty}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}^{2}+\|u^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Sigma)}\|\eta^{1}\|_{W^{2,\frac{1}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|u^{1}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\eta^{1}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}^{2}+\|u^{1}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\eta^{1}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}^{2}.

Therefore, we have

\int_{0}^{T}|\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(u_{1}^{1}\partial_{1}^{2}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})((\sigma(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}))|\lesssim\delta d^{2}(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p}).

Next, we estimate the following term:

(5.48)

\displaystyle{}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}u^{1}\partial_{1}^{2}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}(\sigma((\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})).

By spatial integration by parts, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}u^{1}\partial_{1}^{2}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\sigma(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|u^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W}\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}

\displaystyle+\|u^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}

(5.49)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta d(u,\eta,p)d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p}).

Terms related to pressure

We now estimate the terms involving pressure. Using temporal integration by parts, it holds that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\tilde{p}\langle R^{2,1}J^{1}\rangle=\tilde{p}\langle R^{2,1}J^{1}\rangle(t)-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\Omega}\tilde{p}\partial_{t}\langle R^{2,1}J^{1}\rangle.

This can be controlled by

\displaystyle|\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\tilde{p}\langle R^{2,1}J^{1}\rangle|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}(\int_{\Omega}\tilde{p})\int_{\Omega}(|\partial_{t}^{2}\nabla\bar{\eta}||\nabla u^{1}|+|\partial_{t}\nabla\bar{\eta}||\partial_{t}\nabla u^{1}|+|\partial_{t}\nabla\bar{\eta}||\partial_{t}\nabla\tilde{u}^{2}|

\displaystyle+|\nabla\bar{\eta}||\partial_{t}^{2}\nabla\tilde{u}^{2}|+|\partial_{t}^{2}\nabla\bar{\eta}^{1}||\nabla u|+|\partial_{t}\nabla\bar{\eta}^{1}||\partial_{t}\nabla u|)

\displaystyle+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\int_{\Omega}\tilde{p}\int_{\Omega}|(\partial_{t}\nabla\bar{\eta}||\nabla u^{2}|+|\nabla\bar{\eta}||\partial_{t}\nabla\tilde{u}^{2}|+|\partial_{t}\nabla\bar{\eta}^{1}||\nabla u|)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\|\tilde{p}\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{2}}+\|\tilde{p}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{2}})\bigg(\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}}\|u^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}u^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}

\displaystyle+\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}\bigg)

\displaystyle+\|\tilde{p}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{2}}(\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|u^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})+d({u},{\eta},{p}))^{2}.

Terms related to
w

We next establish the estimates for terms involving
w
. From the result in
[
10
, Section 10]
, we have

\displaystyle\|w\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W_{0}^{1,\frac{4}{3-2\varepsilon_{-}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|R^{2,1}J^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{\frac{4}{3-2\varepsilon_{+}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\tilde{u}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\frac{4}{2-\varepsilon_{-}}}}+\|\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{4}(\Omega)}\|\nabla\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{+}}}}+\|\nabla\bar{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{\frac{4}{2-\varepsilon_{-}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\|\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(u,\eta,p)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})).

Using the fact that
1-\varepsilon_{+}\geq\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}>\varepsilon_{+}-\varepsilon_{-}-2\alpha
, the estimate above yields

\displaystyle|\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}wJ^{1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{0}}\|w\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{0}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{0}}\|w\|_{L^{\infty}W_{0}^{1,\frac{4}{3-2\varepsilon_{+}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})(d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})+d({u},{\eta},{p})).

Similarly, we have the following estimate

\displaystyle\|w\|_{L^{2}W_{0}^{1,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|R^{2,1}J^{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}+\|\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}}\|\nabla\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}L^{\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{+}}}}+\|\nabla\bar{\eta}\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{\frac{4}{2-\varepsilon_{-}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}}{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(u,\eta,p)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})).

This estimate, together with Cauchy’s inequality, implies that

\displaystyle|\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}R^{1,1}\cdot wJ^{1}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta\bigg(\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}^{2}+\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}^{2}+\|u\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}^{2}+\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\tilde{p}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(u,\eta,p)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p}))^{2},

and similarly

\displaystyle|\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}\tilde{u}^{2}:\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}w|\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|w\|_{L_{t}^{2}W_{0}^{1,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\|\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(u,\eta,p)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p}))^{2}.

Moreover, the estimate for
\partial_{t}w
is given by

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}w\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}(J^{1}R^{2,1})\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}})\|R^{2,1}\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}+\|\partial_{t}R^{2,1}\|_{L^{q_{-}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta d(u,\eta,p)+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{1,\frac{2}{1+\alpha}}}\|\nabla u^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{+}}}}+\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\nabla\partial_{t}u^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{1,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{1,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}+\|\bar{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{1,\frac{2}{\alpha}}}\|\nabla\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{2}}+\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{2+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|\nabla\partial_{t}u\|_{L_{t}^{2}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{1\frac{2}{\alpha}}}\|\nabla u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta d(u,\eta,p)+\|\partial_{t}^{2}{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}}\|u^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\nabla\partial_{t}u^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\bar{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|\nabla{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}{\eta}^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}},

which yields that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\partial_{t}(J^{1}w)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\partial_{t}J^{1}w+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}J^{1}\partial_{t}w\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{2}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha}{2}}}\|w\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})(d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})+d(u,\eta,p)).

Terms related to contact points

We first notice that
\partial_{t}u^{1}_{1}=\partial_{t}u^{2}_{1}=0
at the contact points, so that
\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{\eta}(\pm\ell)=\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\mathcal{N}(\pm\ell)
.
We then note that

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}R^{7}|

\displaystyle\lesssim|\hat{\mathscr{W}}^{\prime}(u^{1}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1})||\partial_{t}u^{1}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{t}u^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{2}|+|\hat{\mathscr{W}}^{\prime}(u^{1}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1})-\hat{\mathscr{W}}^{\prime}(u^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{2})||\partial_{t}u^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim(|u^{1}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}|)(|\partial_{t}u^{1}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{t}u^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{2}|)+(|\partial_{t}u^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{2}|)(\|u\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|u^{2}\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\eta\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q-}}),

which implies that:

\|\partial_{t}R^{7}\|_{L_{t}^{2}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|\partial_{t}^{2}u^{1}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{t}^{2}u^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}}+\|\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+\|u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}})\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(u,\eta,p)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})).

Hence, using trace theory, we have the following estimate

(5.50)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}[\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1},R^{7,1}]_{\ell}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}[\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{1}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{2},R^{7,1}]_{\ell}+\int_{0}^{T}[\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{2},R^{7,1}]_{\ell}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(u,p,\eta)d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(u,p,\eta)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(u,p,\eta)d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}).

Finally, for the last term, we bound it by:

\int_{0}^{T}[\partial_{t}u^{1}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{t}u^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N},\partial_{t}u^{2}\cdot(\mathcal{N}^{2}-\mathcal{N}^{1})]_{\ell}\lesssim d(u,p,\eta)\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(u,p,\eta)^{2}.

After integrating (
5.14
) over
[0,T]
, we use the Cauchy’s inequality and Propositions (
2.4
), Gronwall’s Lemma along with (
5.29
), (
5.32
), (
5.40
), (
5.42
), (
5.50
) to deduce the bound

(5.51)

\displaystyle d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})

\displaystyle\leq e^{C_{1}T\delta^{1/2}}C_{2}\delta^{1/2}(d(u,p,\eta)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}))^{2},

where the universal constants
C_{1}
and
C_{2}
are determined by the estimates of (
5.29
), (
5.32
), (
5.40
), (
5.42
), (
5.50
).

The lower-order energy estimates for
(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})
follow directly from integrating the higher-order terms
\partial_{t}\tilde{u}
and
\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}
, so we omit the details here.

Step 5 – Enhanced Estimate for
\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}

In order to close the estimates for
(\partial_{t}\tilde{u},\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})
, we have to estimate
\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{3/2-\alpha}}
contained in (
5.51
). This part is similar to
[
10
, Proof of Theorem 9.3]
, but the details are omitted there. So we give the full proof here. The weak formulation for (
5.10
) might be given as

(5.52)

\displaystyle\left<\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{u},\psi\right>_{\ast}+((\partial_{t}\tilde{u},\psi))+(\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta},\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1})_{1,\Sigma_{0}}+[\partial_{t}{u}^{1}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{2},\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}]_{\ell}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\mu}{2}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}{u}^{2}\right):\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}}\psi J^{1}+\int_{\Omega}R^{1,1}\cdot\psi J^{1}
