\displaystyle-\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}R^{4,1}\cdot\psi-[\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1},R^{7,1}]_{\ell}-\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}R^{5,1}\cdot\psi-\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\partial_{1}(\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1})

for any
\psi\in W_{\sigma}(t)
. We now choose
\psi=M^{1}\nabla\phi
, where
\phi
solves the equations

\displaystyle-\Delta\phi=0\ \text{in}\ \Omega,\quad\partial_{\nu}\phi=(D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}-\tilde{a}_{0}(t)\zeta_{0})/|\mathcal{N}_{0}|\ \text{on}\ \Sigma,\quad\partial_{\nu}\phi=0\ \text{on}\ \Sigma_{s},

where the definition of the operator
D_{j}^{s}
can be found in
[
10
, Section 8]
and
\tilde{a}_{0}(t)
is defined to be a function of
t
such that

(5.53)

\displaystyle\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}-\tilde{a}_{0}(t)\zeta_{0})dx=0.

Using the sixth equation of (
5.10
), we have:

(5.54)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}(t)=\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}ds+\int_{0}^{t}R^{6,1}ds+\int_{0}^{t}u_{1}^{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}+\int_{0}^{t}u_{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2},

which implies that

(5.55)

\displaystyle\tilde{a}_{0}(t)=\frac{\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\big(\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}ds+\int_{0}^{t}R^{6,1}ds+\int_{0}^{t}u_{1}^{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}+\int_{0}^{t}u_{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\big)}{\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\zeta_{0}dx},

and

(5.56)

\displaystyle\tilde{a}_{0}^{\prime}(t)=\frac{\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\big(\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}ds+R^{6,1}+u_{1}^{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}+u_{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\big)}{\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\zeta_{0}dx}.

Using the definition of
R^{6,1}
and integration by part, we have the following estimate

\displaystyle|\tilde{a}_{0}(t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{T}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}+\sqrt{T}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+T\|u^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+T\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}

(5.57)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{T}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta}),

and similarly

\displaystyle|\tilde{a}^{\prime}_{0}(t)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}(t)\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+T\|u\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}+T\|u^{2}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{1}}

(5.58)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}(t)\|_{H^{1}}+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta}).

We take
s=1-2\alpha
so that
\frac{1}{2}+s+\alpha=\frac{3}{2}-\alpha
. Note that
M^{1}=K^{1}\nabla\Phi^{1}
for
\Phi^{1}
the straightening map in terms of
\eta^{1}
, so for the first term in (
5.52
), we have

(5.59)

\left<\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{u},\psi\right>_{\ast}=\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\nabla\Phi^{1}\nabla\phi-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{1}\nabla\phi-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\nabla\Phi^{1}\nabla\partial_{t}\phi.

Under the assumptions for
S(T,\delta)
, we have the bounds

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\nabla\Phi^{1}\nabla\phi

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{0}}(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}+|\tilde{a}_{0}(t)|_{L_{t}^{\infty}})

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{0}}(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}+\sqrt{T}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta})),

and

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{T}-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{1}\nabla\phi-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\nabla\Phi^{1}\nabla\partial_{t}\phi\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{T}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{0}}(\|\phi\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\phi\|_{L^{2}H^{1}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{T}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{0}}\big(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}}+\|a(t)\|_{L_{t}^{2}}+\|a(t)^{\prime}\|_{L_{t}^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{T}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{0}}\big(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}}\big)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{T}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{0}}\big(\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}+\sqrt{T}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta})\big).

For the second term in (
5.52
), we estimate

(5.60)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}:\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}\psi\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}\|\phi\|_{L^{2}H^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}(\|D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{K}}^{1/2}}+|\tilde{a}_{0}(t)|_{L_{t}^{2}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}(\|D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{K}}^{1/2}}+\sqrt{T}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta})),

where the definition of the space
\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{K}}^{1/2}
can also be found in
[
10
, Section 8]
.

The third term in (
5.52
), after integration over
[0,T]
in time, can be rewritten as

(5.61)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta},\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1})_{1,\Sigma_{0}}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta},D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})_{1,\Sigma_{0}}-\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta},\tilde{a}_{0}(t)D_{j}^{s}\rho_{0})_{1,\Sigma_{0}}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{T}(D_{j}^{s/2}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta},D_{j}^{s/2}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})_{1,\Sigma}-\int_{0}^{T}(\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta},\tilde{a}_{0}(t)D_{j}^{s}\rho_{0})_{1,\Sigma_{0}}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\|D_{j}^{s/2}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{K}}^{1}}^{2}-\|a_{0}(t)\|_{L_{t}^{2}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\|D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{K}}^{1/2+\alpha}}^{2}-\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}(\sqrt{T}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta})),

since
M^{1}\nabla\phi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}=\nabla\phi\cdot\mathcal{N}_{0}=D_{t}^{j}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}-\tilde{a}_{0}(t)D_{j}^{s}\rho_{0}
.
The fourth term in (
5.52
), after integration over
[0,T]
in time, can be estimated by

(5.62)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}|[\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{1}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{2},\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}]_{\ell}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}[\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{1}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{2}]_{\ell}(\|D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{K}}^{1/2+\alpha}}+|\tilde{a}_{0}(t)|)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|[\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{1}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{2}]_{\ell}\|_{L_{t}^{2}}\|D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{K}}^{1/2+\alpha}}

\displaystyle+\|[\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{1}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}-\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{2}]_{\ell}\|_{L_{t}^{2}}(\sqrt{T}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta})).

Then the symmetric gradient form will result in the estimate

(5.63)

\displaystyle|\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\left(\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}{u}^{2}\right):\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}}\psi J^{1}|\lesssim\delta^{1/2}\|\phi\|_{L^{2}H^{2}}(\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}H^{3/2-\alpha}}+|\tilde{a}_{0}(t)|)

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{1/2}\|D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{K}}^{1/2}}(d(u,\eta,p)+\sqrt{T}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta})).

The terms involving
R^{1,j}
, for
j=1,4,5
in (
5.52
) are estimated, similar to (
5.29
) and (
5.40
). From the computation in Step 4, we have

(5.64)

\displaystyle\|R^{1,1}-R^{4,1}-R^{5,1}\|_{L^{2}(\mathcal{H}^{1})^{*}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(u,\eta,p)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p})).

Therefore:

(5.65)

\displaystyle|\int_{0}^{T}(\int_{\Omega}R^{1,1}\cdot\psi J^{1}-\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}R^{4,1}\cdot\psi-\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}R^{5,1}\cdot\psi)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(u,\eta,p)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p}))\|\psi\|_{H^{1}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(u,\eta,p)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p}))(\|D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{K}}^{1/2}}+|\tilde{a}_{0}(t)|)

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(u,\eta,p)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p}))(\|D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}\mathcal{H}_{\mathcal{K}}^{1/2}}+\sqrt{T}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta})).

Similarly, we still use
\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}=M^{1}\nabla\phi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}=D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}-\tilde{a}_{0}(t)D_{j}^{s}\rho_{0}
on
\pm\ell
and the computation for
R^{7,1}
to bound the following term

(5.66)

\displaystyle|\int_{0}^{T}[\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1},R^{7,1}]_{\ell}|

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|[D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}]_{\ell}+|\tilde{a}_{0}(t)|)\|_{L_{t}^{2}}\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(u,\eta,p)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p}))

\displaystyle\lesssim\|D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1-s/2}}\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(u,\eta,p)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p}))

\displaystyle+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(u,\eta,p)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p}))(\sqrt{T}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta})).

Finally, we estimate the last term in (
5.52
). We have

(5.67)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\int_{0}^{T}(\sigma(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}),\partial_{1}(\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}(t)))_{L^{2}}\\
\lesssim&(\|\partial_{1}D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{-\frac{s}{2}}}+\|\tilde{a}_{0}(t)\|_{L_{t}^{2}})\|\int_{0}^{t}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{s}{2}}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}H^{1+\frac{s}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}+}}+\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{1+\frac{s}{2}}}(\sqrt{T}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta}))\\
\lesssim&\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}H^{1+\frac{s}{2}}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{1+\frac{s}{2}}}(\sqrt{T}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta}))\\
\lesssim&\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{1+\frac{s}{2}}}^{2}+\sqrt{T}d^{2}(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})d({u},{p},{\eta})+\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})^{2}.\end{aligned}

Setting
T\lesssim\delta
, the combination of (
5.52
)–(
5.67
) along with the Cauchy’s inequality, after
j\to\infty
, implies that

(5.68)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{3/2-\alpha}}^{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(u,p,\eta)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}))^{2}.

Now it remains to use the kinematic boundary condition to show the estimate for
\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{\eta}
. We have:

(5.69)

\displaystyle\|(\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{\eta})\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}}=

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}}+\|R^{6,1}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}}+\|u^{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}}+\|u\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle J_{1}+J_{2}+J_{3}+J_{4}.

For the term
J_{1}
, by trace theorem, it is bounded by

J_{1}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}}.

For
J_{2}
, by definition of
R^{6,1}
, we have

J_{2}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}.

For
J_{3}
, we have

J_{3}\lesssim\|u^{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}}\lesssim\|u^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}.

Similarly, for the term
J_{4}
, we have:

J_{4}\lesssim\|u\|_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{1}{2}+}(\Sigma)}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\lesssim\|u\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}.

Therefore, combining estimates for
J_{1}
to
J_{4}
, we obtain

\|\partial_{t}^{2}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(u,p,\eta)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})).

By integrating
\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}
, we obtain the following estimate for
\tilde{\eta}

(5.70)

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(d(u,p,\eta)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}))

Step 6 – Pressure Estimates and Elliptic Estimates for
(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})

We first derive the system for the zero-order terms. Integrating the system (
5.10
) from
0
to
t
for any
t\in(0,T)
, we obtain

(5.71)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &(\partial_{t}\tilde{u})+\int_{0}^{t}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\partial_{t}\tilde{p},\partial_{t}\tilde{u})=\tilde{R}^{1,1}\ &\text{in}&\ \Omega,\\
&\int_{0}^{t}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}=\tilde{R}^{2,1}\ &\text{in}&\ \Omega,\\
&\int_{0}^{t}S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\partial_{t}\tilde{p},\partial_{t}\tilde{u})\mathcal{N}^{1}=\int_{0}^{t}g(\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})\mathcal{N}^{1}-\int_{0}^{t}\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|)^{3/2}}\right)\mathcal{N}^{1}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})\mathcal{N}^{1}(t)\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{1}((\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})\mathcal{N}^{1}(t)+\tilde{R}^{4,1}\ &\text{on}&\ \Sigma,\\
&\int_{0}^{t}(S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\partial_{t}\tilde{p},\partial_{t}\tilde{u})\nu-\beta\partial_{t}\tilde{u})\cdot\tau=\tilde{R}^{5,1}\ &\text{on}&\ \Sigma_{s},\\
&\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\nu=0\ &\text{on}&\ \Sigma_{s},\\
&(\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})=\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}+\tilde{R}^{6,1}\ &\text{on}&\ \Sigma,\\
&\kappa(\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})(\pm\ell,t)=\mp\sigma\frac{\partial_{1}\tilde{\eta}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}(\pm\ell,t)\mp\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}(\pm\ell,t)\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\mp\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{1}((\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})(\pm\ell,t)-\tilde{R}^{7,1}(\pm\ell,t)\end{aligned}\right.

where:

(5.72)

\displaystyle\tilde{R}^{1,1}

\displaystyle=R^{1}+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(p,u)+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}(p^{2},u^{2})+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{2}}(p^{2},u^{2}),

\displaystyle\tilde{R}^{2,1}

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{t}(\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}{u}^{2})-\int_{0}^{t}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}{u}-\int_{0}^{t}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}\tilde{u}^{2},

\displaystyle\tilde{R}^{4,1}

\displaystyle=R^{4}+\int_{0}^{t}\mu S_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}({u},{p})\mathcal{N}^{1}+\int_{0}^{t}\mu S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(u,p)\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}+\int_{0}^{t}\mu S_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}(u^{2},p^{2})\mathcal{N}^{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\mu S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}({u}^{2},{p}^{2})\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}+\int_{0}^{t}g{\eta}\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}-\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}{\eta}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|)^{3/2}}\right)\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}(\mathcal{K}(\eta^{1})-\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{t}(\mathcal{N}^{1}-\mathcal{N}^{2})-(\mathcal{K}(\eta)-\partial_{1}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})+\partial_{1}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2}))\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{2}

\displaystyle-\sigma\int_{0}^{t}(\mathcal{K}(\tilde{\eta}^{1})-\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{1}))(\mathcal{N}^{1}-\mathcal{N}^{2})+\int_{0}^{t}\mu S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}(\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2},\partial_{t}\tilde{p}^{2})\mathcal{N}^{2}

\displaystyle\tilde{R}^{5,1}

\displaystyle=-\int_{0}^{t}S_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}({p},{u})+\mu\int_{0}^{t}S_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}({u}^{2},p^{2})+\int_{0}^{t}\mu S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}(\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2},\partial_{t}\tilde{p}^{2}),

\displaystyle\quad\tilde{R}^{6,1}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\cdot(\mathcal{N}^{1}-\mathcal{N}^{2})+\int_{0}^{t}(u_{1}^{1}-u_{1}^{2})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}+\int_{0}^{t}(u_{1}^{2})(\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}),

\displaystyle\quad\tilde{R}^{7,1}=

\displaystyle R^{7}.

To apply elliptic theory to (
5.71
), we begin by estimating the elliptic norms of each equation independently.

Momentum equation

Using integration by part, we can reformulate the first equation of (
5.71
) to:

(5.73)

\displaystyle\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\tilde{p},\tilde{u})=-\partial_{t}\tilde{u}+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\tilde{p},\tilde{u})+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}\tilde{u}\Big)+\tilde{R}^{1,1}.

We estimate the elliptic nor for each term on the right hand side of equation (
5.73
). We have

(5.74)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}H^{1}},

and

\displaystyle{}\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\tilde{p},\tilde{u})\right\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim

\displaystyle T\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,\infty}}\|\bar{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}(\|\tilde{p}\|_{L^{2}L^{\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}+\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T\|\partial_{t}{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}(\|\tilde{p}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}+\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}})

(5.75)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T\delta d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}),

and similarly

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0}^{t}(\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}\tilde{u})\right\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+\|{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})(\|\partial_{t}{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,\infty}}\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}}\|\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{1,\frac{2}{1-\varepsilon_{-}}}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|\partial_{t}{\eta}^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}})

(5.76)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{2}}\delta d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}).

Then it remains to estimate the terms included in
R^{1,1}
. We first discuss the terms in
R^{1}
. By definition of
R^{1}
, we have (For simplicity, we omitted details here; the estimate is similar to the computation done in the proof of Theorem
5.4
):

(5.77)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|R^{1}\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim&T(1+\|\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+\|\eta^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})(\|u^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}})\|u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\\
&+T(1+\|\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\\
&+T(1+\|\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|u^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\\
&+T(1+\|\eta^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}})\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|u^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\eta^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\\
\lesssim&T\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(u,p,\eta).\end{aligned}

And then for other terms included in
\tilde{R}^{1,1}
, similar to estimates (
5.75
) and (
5.76
), we have the following estimates

(5.78)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(p,u)\right\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}Td(u,p,\eta),\end{aligned}

and

(5.79)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}S_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}(p,u)\right\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{\frac{1}{2}}d(u,p,\eta).\end{aligned}

Then for the remaining terms, using temporal integration by part we have

(5.80)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} &\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}(p^{2},u^{2})\right\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}(\|u^{2}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|p^{2}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}})\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(u,p,\eta),\\
&\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{2}}(p^{2},u^{2})\right\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim T\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}}\|\eta^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}(\|u^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|p^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{1,q_{+}}})\lesssim T\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(u,p,\eta),\\
&\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}u^{2}\right\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}u^{2}\right\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}+\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}\partial_{t}u^{2}\right\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\\
&\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}(\|u^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}})\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|u^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}})\|\eta\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{\frac{1}{2}}d(u,p,\eta),\\
&\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}u^{2}\right\|_{L^{2}L^{q_{-}}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{2}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}(\|u^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}})\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{\frac{1}{2}}d(u,p,\eta),\end{aligned}

which finishes the estimate of the momentum equations.

Divergence equation

We can rewrite the divergence equation as follows

(5.81)

\displaystyle{}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}\tilde{u}=-\int_{0}^{t}\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}\tilde{u}+\int_{0}^{t}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}+\tilde{R}^{2,1}.

We then estimate the elliptic norms of each term on the right hand side of (
5.81
). We have:

(5.82)

\displaystyle\|\int_{0}^{t}\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}\lesssim T\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}Td(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}),

and

(5.83)

\displaystyle\|\int_{0}^{t}\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}-\mathcal{A}^{2}}\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}\lesssim T\|\eta\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}^{2}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}Td(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}).

Then for the terms included in
\tilde{R}^{2,1}
, using integration by part, we have

(5.84)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \|\int_{0}^{t}\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}{u}^{2}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}\lesssim&T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}{u}^{2}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta}),\\
\|\int_{0}^{t}\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}{u}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}\lesssim&T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|{u}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta}),\end{aligned}

which finishes the estimate for divergence equation.

Fixed boundary condition

The equation on the fixed boundary can be rewritten as:

(5.85)

\displaystyle{}S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\tilde{u},\tilde{p})\nu\cdot\tau=\beta\tilde{u}\cdot\tau-\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\tilde{u})+\tilde{R}^{5,1}.

Then we estimate each term on the right hand side of the equation(
5.85
). We have

(5.86)

\displaystyle\|\tilde{u}\cdot\tau\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}(\Sigma)}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{\infty}H^{1}},

and

(5.87)

\displaystyle\|\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\tilde{u})\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}),

where we used the following relation:

(5.88)

\displaystyle\|fg\|_{W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim\|f\|_{W^{1,q_{-}}}\|g\|_{W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}.

Then we show the estimate for terms included
\tilde{R}^{5,1}
. It holds that

(5.89)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \left\|\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}({u})\right\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim&T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|{u}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta}),\\
\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}}({u}^{2})\right\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim&T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}{u}^{2}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta}),\end{aligned}

which finishes the estimate for the fixed boundary equation.

Kinematic boundary condition

The kinematic boundary condition can be rewritten as follows

(5.90)

\displaystyle{}\tilde{u}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}=-\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}+\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}+\tilde{R}^{6,1}+\int_{0}^{t}u^{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}+\int_{0}^{t}u\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}.

We now estimate each term on the right hand side of (
5.90
), we have the following computation:

(5.91)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{2-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}},

and

(5.92)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\tilde{u}\cdot\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}\right\|_{L^{2}W^{2-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}T^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}),

where we used the trace theorem and the fact that
W^{2-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}
is an algebra.

Similarly, using temporal integration by part, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0}^{t}u^{1}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\right\|_{L^{2}W^{2-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|u^{1}\partial_{1}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{2-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}u^{1}\partial_{1}\tilde{\eta}\right\|_{L^{2}W^{2-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+\|\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}u^{1}\partial_{1}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{2-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}

(5.93)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(\|u^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}u^{1}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}})\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim(1+T^{\frac{1}{2}})\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}),

and

(5.94)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \left\|\int_{0}^{t}u\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\right\|_{L^{2}W^{2-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}&\lesssim(\|u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}+\|u\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}})(\|\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})\\
&\lesssim(1+T^{\frac{1}{2}})\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta}).\end{aligned}

The estimate for
\tilde{R}^{6,1}
is similar to (
5.93
) and (
5.94
), we omit the details here and only write down the result

(5.95)

\displaystyle\|\tilde{R}^{6,1}\|_{L^{2}W^{2-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+T^{\frac{1}{2}})d(u,p,\eta).

Contact point

The contact point condition can be rewritten as:

(5.96)

\displaystyle{}\mp\sigma\frac{\partial_{1}\tilde{\eta}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}(\pm\ell,t)=

\displaystyle-\kappa(\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})(\pm\ell,t)\mp\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}

\displaystyle\mp\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{1}((\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})-\tilde{R}^{7,1}.

We now estimate terms on the right hand side of (
5.96
). Using temporal integration by part, it holds that

(5.97)

\displaystyle\sigma\int_{0}^{t}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+\|\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}),

and

(5.98)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{1}((\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})\lesssim

\displaystyle T^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}})\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d({u},{p},{\eta}).

For the integrated term in
\tilde{R}^{7,1}
, we use an estimate similar to the one above. We omit the details. Then it remains to show the estimate for
\partial_{t}R^{7}
. We have the following:

(5.99)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(\hat{\mathcal{W}}(\partial_{t}\eta^{1})-\hat{\mathcal{W}}(\partial_{t}\eta^{2}))\lesssim\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}[\partial_{t}\eta]_{\ell},

which finishes the estimate for the contact point condition.

Capillary equation

Finally, we show the estimate for the capillary equation. We rewrite the equation as

(5.100)

\displaystyle{}\begin{aligned} S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\tilde{p},\tilde{u})\mathcal{N}^{1}=&\int_{0}^{t}S_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\tilde{u},\tilde{p})\mathcal{N}^{1}+\int_{0}^{t}S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\tilde{p},\tilde{u})\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}-g(\tilde{\eta})\mathcal{N}^{1}-\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}\tilde{\eta}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|)^{3/2}}\right)\mathcal{N}^{1}\\
&-\int_{0}^{t}g(\tilde{\eta})\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}-\int_{0}^{t}\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}\tilde{\eta}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|)^{3/2}}\right)\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}-\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})\mathcal{N}^{1}(t)\\
&-\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{1}((\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})\mathcal{N}^{1}(t)+\tilde{R}^{4,1}.\end{aligned}

For the right hand side of (
5.100
), all of the terms including the integrated terms can be estimated similarly to those of the other equations except for the mean curvature-type terms. We have

(5.101)

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \left\|\int_{0}^{t}S_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\tilde{p},\tilde{u})\mathcal{N}^{1}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}(\|\tilde{p}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}+\|\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}})\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p}),\\
\left\|\int_{0}^{t}S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}}(\tilde{p},\tilde{u})\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}\right\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}(\|\tilde{p}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}+\|\tilde{u}\|_{L_{t}^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}})\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p}),\end{aligned}

and

(5.102)

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0}^{t}(g\tilde{\eta}-\partial_{1}(\frac{\partial_{1}\tilde{\eta}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}))\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}\right\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim T\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{2,q_{-}}}\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim T\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}).

Using temporal integration by part, we have

(5.103)

\displaystyle\left\|\sigma\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta})\mathcal{N}^{1}(t)\right\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\tilde{\eta})\mathcal{N}^{1}(t)\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{zz}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\partial_{1}\tilde{\eta})\mathcal{N}^{1}(t)\right\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1})\partial_{1}\tilde{\eta})\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}(t)\right\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\eta^{1}\|_{L^{\infty}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\right)\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}),

and similarly

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{1}((\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2})-\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2})\mathcal{N}^{1}(t)\right\|_{L^{2}W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}

(5.104)

\displaystyle\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}^{2}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\|\eta\|_{L^{2}W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}}\lesssim T^{\frac{1}{2}}\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}d(u,\eta,p).

For terms in
\tilde{R}^{4,1}
, they can be estimated similarly to the terms above, but we omit the details here. The idea is that each time a temporal derivative hits a difference term, we use integration by parts to shift the time derivative to the non-difference term.

Using elliptic theory for system (
5.71
) and estimates (
5.73
) to (
5.104
), we deduce that

(5.105)

\displaystyle\|\tilde{u}\|_{L^{2}W^{2,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\tilde{p}\|_{L^{2}W^{1,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2}\lesssim(\delta+T+T^{\frac{1}{2}})(d(u,\eta,p)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{\eta},\tilde{p}))^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\tilde{\eta}\|_{L^{2}H^{3/2-\alpha}}^{2}.

Step 7 – Fixed Point

Estimates (
5.51
), (
5.68
), and (
5.105
) yield the bound:

(5.106)

\displaystyle d^{2}(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})\leq{C}\exp(\delta^{\frac{1}{2}})(\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}+T)(d(u,p,\eta)+d(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta}))^{2},

where
C
is a universal constant that may change from line to line, and we have used the fact that
Ce^{\delta^{\frac{1}{2}}}(\delta+T)<\frac{1}{2}
. We then restrict
\delta
and
T
to be sufficiently small so that all terms with a tilde superscript on the right-hand side of (
5.106
) can be absorbed into the left-hand side of inequality (
5.106
). Moreover, we may further restrict
\delta
to deduce the following inequality:

d^{2}(\tilde{u},\tilde{p},\tilde{\eta})\leq\frac{1}{2}d^{2}(u,p,\eta).

Consequently, the map
A
defined in (
5.9
) is a contraction. Thus, Banach’s fixed point theorem implies that the nonlinear system (
1.17
) with
(\partial_{t}^{j}u,\partial_{t}^{j}p,\partial_{t}^{j}\eta)
, has a solution
(u,p,\eta)\in S(T,\delta)
for a small
T
determined above.

The uniqueness for (
1.17
) follows directly from the uniqueness of the solution to the corresponding linear system.
∎

6.
Uniform Bounds and Global Well-Posedness of the Nonlinear System

In this section, we study the global well-posedness of the nonlinear system (
1.7
) and complete the proof of the main theorem. In light of the local well-posedness results in Theorem
5.5
, it suffices to derive the global-in-time a priori estimate.

We denote
\mathcal{A}
,
\mathcal{N}
,
J
and
K
as defined in terms of
\eta
. Note that
(\partial_{t}^{2}u,\partial_{t}^{2}\eta)
and
(\partial_{t}^{j}u,\partial_{t}^{j}p,\partial_{t}^{j}\eta)
,
j=0,1
satisfy the equations

(6.1)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}^{j+1}u+\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}}S_{\mathcal{A}}(\partial_{t}^{j}p,\partial_{t}^{j}u)=F^{1,j}\quad&\text{in}&\ \Omega,\\
&\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}}\partial_{t}^{j}u=F^{2,j}\quad&\text{in}&\ \Omega,\\
&S_{\mathcal{A}}(\partial_{t}^{j}p,\partial_{t}^{j}u)\mathcal{N}=\left[g\partial_{t}^{j}\eta-\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}^{j}\eta}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}+F^{3,j}\right)\right]\mathcal{N}+F^{4,j}\quad&\text{on}&\ \Sigma,\\
&\partial_{t}^{j+1}\eta-\partial_{t}^{j}u\cdot\mathcal{N}=F^{6,j}\quad&\text{on}&\ \Sigma,\\
&(S_{\mathcal{A}}(\partial_{t}^{j}p,\partial_{t}^{j}u)\cdot\nu-\beta\partial_{t}^{j}u)\cdot\tau=F^{5,j}\quad&\text{on}&\ \Sigma_{s},\\
&\partial_{t}^{j}u\cdot\nu=0\quad&\text{on}&\ \Sigma_{s},\\
&\kappa\partial_{t}^{j+1}\eta=\mp\sigma\left(\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}^{j}\eta}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}+F^{3,j}\right)-\kappa F^{7,j}\quad&\text{on}&\ \pm\ell,\end{aligned}\right.

where the forcing terms are given in the Appendix
A
.
We follow the argument of
[
10
, Section 10.2]
to derive the uniform boundedness of solutions to (
6.1
).

Proof of Theorem
1.1
.

We give only a sketch of the proof.

Step 1 – Energy-Dissipation Estimates for (
6.1
)

Using standard arguments, we obtain
the energy-dissipation equality for
(\partial_{t}^{j}u,\partial_{t}^{j}\eta,\partial_{t}^{j}p)
with
j=0,1
in (
6.1
), and
(\partial_{t}^{2}u,\partial_{t}^{2}\eta)

(6.2)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}|u|^{2}J+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}g|\eta|^{2}+\sigma\frac{|\partial_{1}\eta|^{2}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\right)+\frac{\mu}{2}\int_{\Omega}|\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}}u|^{2}J+\beta\int_{\Sigma_{s}}|u\cdot\tau|^{2}J+[\partial_{t}\eta]_{\ell}^{2}

\displaystyle=-\sigma(F^{3,0},\partial_{1}(\partial_{t}\eta))_{L^{2}(-\ell,\ell)}-[\partial_{t}\eta,F^{7,0}]_{\ell},

(6.3)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{t}u|^{2}J+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}g|\partial_{t}\eta|^{2}+\sigma\frac{|\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta|^{2}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\right)+\frac{\mu}{2}\int_{\Omega}|\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}}\partial_{t}u|^{2}J+\beta\int_{\Sigma_{s}}|\partial_{t}u\cdot\tau|^{2}J+[\partial_{t}^{2}\eta]_{\ell}^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{F}^{1}(\partial_{t}u,\partial_{t}p,\partial_{t}\eta)+(\partial_{t}p,F^{2,1})_{\mathcal{H}^{0}(\Omega)},

(6.4)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{t}^{2}u|^{2}J+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}g|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta|^{2}+\sigma\frac{|\partial_{1}\partial_{t}^{2}\eta|^{2}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{2}u\omega J\right)+\frac{\mu}{2}\int_{\Omega}|\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}}\partial_{t}^{2}u|^{2}J+\beta\int_{\Sigma_{s}}|\partial_{t}^{2}u\cdot\tau|^{2}J+[\partial_{t}^{2}\eta]_{\ell}^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{F}^{2}(\partial_{t}^{2}u,\partial_{t}^{2}p,\partial_{t}^{2}\eta)+\int_{\Omega}F^{1,2}\omega J+\int_{\Omega}\langle JF^{2,2}\rangle\partial_{t}^{2}p+\frac{\mu}{2}\int_{\Omega}\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}}\partial_{t}^{2}u:\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}}\omega J,

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{1}(\partial_{t}u,\partial_{t}p,\partial_{t}\eta)

\displaystyle=\int_{\Omega}(F^{1,1}\cdot\partial_{t}u)J-\int_{\Sigma_{s}}F^{5,1}(\partial_{t}u\cdot\tau)J+[\partial_{t}^{2}\eta,F^{6,1}-F^{7,1}]_{\ell}

\displaystyle-\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\sigma F^{3,2}\partial_{1}(\partial_{t}u\cdot\mathcal{N})+F^{4,1}\cdot\partial_{t}u-\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}g\partial_{t}\eta F^{6,1}+\sigma\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\partial_{1}F^{6,1},

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{2}(\partial_{t}^{2}u,\partial_{t}^{2}p,\partial_{t}^{2}\eta)

\displaystyle=\int_{\Omega}(F^{1,2}\cdot\partial_{t}^{2}u)J-\int_{\Sigma_{s}}F^{5,2}(\partial_{t}^{2}u\cdot\tau)J+[\partial_{t}^{3}\eta,F^{6,2}-F^{7,2}]_{\ell}

\displaystyle-\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\sigma F^{3,2}\partial_{1}(\partial_{t}^{2}u\cdot\mathcal{N})+F^{4,2}\cdot\partial_{t}^{2}u-\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}g\partial_{t}^{2}\eta F^{6,2}+\sigma\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}^{2}\eta}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\partial_{1}F^{6,2}.

Here the construction of
\omega
is in
[
10
, Proposition 10.1]
.

Using estimate for the forcing terms in
[
10
]
and Section 5, the nonlinear interaction bounds for (
6.2
), (
6.3
) and (
6.4
) yield the energy-dissipation estimate for
(u,\eta)
:

(6.5)

\displaystyle\|u(t)\|_{H^{0}}^{2}+\|\eta(t)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\int_{s}^{t}(\|u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|u\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s})}^{2}+[\partial_{t}\eta]_{\ell}^{2})\lesssim\|u(s)\|_{H^{0}}^{2}+\|\eta(s)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\int_{s}^{t}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}}\mathcal{D},

and the energy-dissipation estimate for
(\partial_{t}u,\partial_{t}\eta)
:

(6.6)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}u(t)\|_{H^{0}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\eta(t)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\int_{s}^{t}(\|\partial_{t}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s})}^{2}+[\partial_{t}^{2}\eta]_{\ell}^{2})\lesssim\|\partial_{t}u(s)\|_{H^{0}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\eta(s)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\int_{s}^{t}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}}\mathcal{D},

as well as the energy-dissipation estimate for
(\partial_{t}^{2}u,\partial_{t}^{2}\eta)
:

(6.7)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{2}u(t)\|_{H^{0}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(t)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}-(\mathcal{E}(t))^{3/2}+\int_{s}^{t}(\|\partial_{t}^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}u\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s})}^{2}+[\partial_{t}^{3}\eta]_{\ell}^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}^{2}u(s)\|_{H^{0}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(s)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+(\mathcal{E}(s))^{3/2}+\int_{s}^{t}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}}\mathcal{D}

for all
0\leq s\leq t\leq T
. We set

\mathcal{E}_{\shortparallel}:=\sum_{j=0}^{2}\|\partial_{t}^{j}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{j}\eta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}
,
and

\mathcal{D}_{\shortparallel}:=\sum_{j=0}^{3}\|\partial_{t}^{j}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{j}u\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s})}^{2}+[\partial_{t}^{j+1}\eta]_{\ell}^{2}.

Then, summing inequalities (
6.5
)–(
6.7
) yields the estimate

(6.8)

\mathcal{E}_{\shortparallel}(t)-(\mathcal{E}(t))^{3/2}+\int_{s}^{t}\left(\mathcal{D}_{\shortparallel}\right)\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{\shortparallel}(s)+(\mathcal{E}(s))^{3/2}+\int_{s}^{t}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}}\mathcal{D}

for all
0\leq s\leq t\leq T
.

Step 2 – Enhanced Dissipation on the Surface

First, from the kinematic boundary condition, it holds that

\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}\eta dx=\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}u\cdot\mathcal{N}dx=\int_{\Omega}J\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}}u=0.

Similarly, we have

\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}^{j}\eta dx=\partial_{t}^{j-1}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}\eta=0

for
j=0,1,2
. Using these results, we obtain

\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\eta(t,x)dx=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}\eta(s,x)dsdx=0.

Using the zero mean value properties for
\partial_{t}^{j}\eta
, we apply
the functional calculus in
[
10
, Section 8]
for the gravity-capillary operator

\mathcal{K}:\partial_{t}^{j}\eta\mapsto g(\partial_{t}^{j}\eta)-\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}(\partial_{t}^{j}\eta)}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\right)

to construct test functions
\psi_{i}=M\nabla\phi_{i}
, where
\phi_{i}
solves the equation

-\Delta\phi_{i}=0~\operatorname{in}~\Omega,~~~\partial_{\nu}\phi_{i}=(D_{j}^{s}\partial_{t}^{i}{\eta}\zeta_{0})/|\mathcal{N}_{0}|~\operatorname{on}~\Sigma,~~~\partial_{\nu}\phi_{i}=0~\operatorname{on}~\Sigma_{s},

for
i=\{0,1,2\}
. Plugging this test function
\phi_{i}
into the
i
-th order weak formulation and using computations similar to those in
[
10
]
, step 6 in the proof of Theorem
4.5
and step 7 in the proof of Theorem
5.5
, we obtain that

(6.9)

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{j}\eta\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}^{j}u(s)\|_{H^{0}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{j}\eta(s)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{j}u(t)\|_{H^{0}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{j}\eta(t)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{s}^{t}\left[(\sum_{k=0}^{2}\|\partial_{t}^{k}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{k}u\|_{L^{2}(\Sigma_{s})}^{2}+[\partial_{t}^{k+1}\eta]_{\ell}^{2})+\mathcal{E}\mathcal{D}\right],

for all
0\leq s\leq t\leq T
and each
j\in\{0,1,2,3\}
.
The inequalities (
6.8
) and (
6.9
) give the following result

(6.10)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\shortparallel}(t)-(\mathcal{E}(t))^{3/2}+\int_{s}^{t}\left(\mathcal{D}_{\shortparallel}+\sum_{j=0}^{2}\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{\shortparallel}(s)+\sum_{j=0}^{2}(\|\partial_{t}^{j}u(s)\|_{H^{0}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{j}\eta(s)\|_{H^{1}}^{2})+\sum_{j=0}^{1}\|\partial_{t}^{j}p(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+(\mathcal{E}(s))^{3/2}+\int_{s}^{t}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}}\mathcal{D}.

Step 3 – Enhanced Dissipation Estimates

The elliptic theory for (
6.1
), with
j=0,1,2
, yields

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}(\|u\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|p-\bar{p}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\eta\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2})\lesssim\int_{s}^{t}\|\partial_{t}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}^{2}+\sqrt{\mathcal{E}}\mathcal{D},

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}(\|\partial_{t}u\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}p-\overline{\partial_{t}{p}}\|_{W^{1,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{s}^{t}\|\partial_{t}^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}^{2}+(\sqrt{\mathcal{E}})\mathcal{D},

with
\overline{\partial_{t}^{j}p}=\frac{\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{j}p}{|\Omega|}
for all
0\leq s\leq t\leq T
.

We now consider the dissipation at the contact points. From the transport equation

\partial_{t}^{j+1}\eta-\partial_{t}^{j}u\cdot\mathcal{N}=F^{6,j}
,
and the vanishing of
F^{6,j}
at
\pm\ell
, we immediately obtain

\sum_{j=0}^{2}[\partial_{t}^{j}u\cdot\mathcal{N}]_{\ell}^{2}=\sum_{j=0}^{2}[\partial_{t}^{j+1}\eta]_{\ell}^{2}
.

The enhanced surface dissipation result then yields

\|\partial_{t}^{3}\eta\|_{H^{1/2-\alpha}}^{2}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\mathcal{E}\mathcal{D}.

Consequently, by the result in (
6.10
), we derive the following estimate

(6.11)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\shortparallel}(t)-(\mathcal{E}(t))^{3/2}+\int_{s}^{t}\left(\mathcal{D}_{\shortparallel}+\sum_{j=1}^{2}\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\int_{s}^{t}\sum_{j=0}^{2}[\partial_{t}^{j+1}\eta]_{\ell}^{2}+\int_{s}^{t}\sum_{j=0}^{1}(\|\partial_{t}^{j}u\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{j}p-\overline{\partial_{t}^{j}p}\|_{W^{1,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{j}\eta\|_{W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{\shortparallel}(s)+\sum_{j=0}^{2}(\|\partial_{t}^{j}u(s)\|_{H^{0}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{j}\eta(s)\|_{H^{1}}^{2})+\sum_{j=0}^{1}\|\partial_{t}^{j}p(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+(\mathcal{E}(s))^{3/2}+\int_{s}^{t}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}}\mathcal{D}.

Step 4 –The Estimate for
\overline{\partial_{t}^{j}p}

Using the same computation as in the proof of Theorem
3.5
, we obtain

\|\overline{p}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{2}}\lesssim\|u\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}u\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{2}L^{2}}+\|[\partial_{t}^{2}\eta]_{\ell}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{2}}+\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}}\mathcal{D}+\mathcal{E}(0),

\|\overline{\partial_{t}p}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{2}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L_{t}^{2}H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}u\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{2}L^{2}}+\|[\partial_{t}^{2}\eta]_{\ell}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{2}}+\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}}\mathcal{D}+\mathcal{E}(0).

Hence, combining Steps 3 and 4, and applying interpolation theory, we obtain that for any
t>0
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\shortparallel}(t)-(\mathcal{E}(t))^{3/2}+\int_{s}^{t}\left(\mathcal{D}_{\shortparallel}+\sum_{j=0}^{2}\|\partial_{t}^{j}\eta\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\int_{s}^{t}\sum_{j=0}^{2}[\partial_{t}^{j+1}\eta]_{\ell}^{2}+\int_{s}^{t}\sum_{j=0}^{1}(\|\partial_{t}^{j}u\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{j}p\|_{W^{1,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{j}\eta\|_{W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{\shortparallel}(s)+\sum_{j=0}^{2}(\|\partial_{t}^{j}u(s)\|_{H^{0}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{j}\eta(s)\|_{H^{1}}^{2})+\sum_{j=0}^{1}\|\partial_{t}^{j}p(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+(\mathcal{E}(s))^{3/2}+\int_{s}^{t}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}}\mathcal{D}.

Step 5 – Enhanced Energy Estimates

Interpolation allows us to enhance the energy estimates:

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}u(t)\|_{H^{1+\varepsilon_{-}/2}}^{2}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}u(s)\|_{H^{1+\varepsilon_{-}/2}}^{2}+\int_{s}^{t}\mathcal{D},

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\eta(t)\|_{H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}^{2}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(s)\|_{H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}}^{2}+\int_{s}^{t}\mathcal{D}.

The elliptic theory for (
6.1
) with
j=0,1
produces the bound:

\|u\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|p\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\eta\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}^{2}+\mathcal{E}.

Theorem
3.5
reveals the estimate

\|\partial_{t}p\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}u\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta\|_{H^{1}}.

Thus, with the estimate in (
6.11
), we have

(6.12)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\shortparallel}(t)+\|u(t)\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|p(t)\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\eta(t)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}

\displaystyle-(\mathcal{E}(t))^{3/2}+\|\partial_{t}p(t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{s}^{t}\left(\mathcal{D}_{\shortparallel}+\sum_{j=0}^{2}\|\partial_{t}^{j}\eta\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}^{2}\right)+\int_{s}^{t}\sum_{j=0}^{2}[\partial_{t}^{j+1}\eta]_{\ell}^{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{s}^{t}(\|u\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|p\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\eta\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2})+\int_{s}^{t}(\|\partial_{t}u\|_{W^{2,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}p\|_{W^{1,q_{-}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\eta\|_{W^{3-1/q_{-},q_{-}}}^{2})

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{E}(s)+(\mathcal{E}(s))^{3/2}+\int_{s}^{t}\sqrt{\mathcal{E}}\mathcal{D}.

Step 6 – Synthesis

For any
T>0
, we take
s=0
and restrict
\delta
to be smaller if necessary, so that if

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\mathcal{E}(t)\leq\delta\ll 1,

then we can absorb the superlinear terms of
\mathcal{E}
in (
6.12
) to obtain the estimate

\sup_{0\leq t\leq t^{\prime}}\mathcal{E}(t)+\int_{0}^{t^{\prime}}\mathcal{D}(s)\,\mathrm{d}s\lesssim\mathcal{E}(0),

for each
t^{\prime}\leq T
, so that

(6.13)

\mathcal{E}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{D}(s)\,\mathrm{d}s\lesssim\mathcal{E}(0)

for all
t\leq T
.

Since
\mathcal{E}\lesssim\mathcal{D}
,
\mathcal{E}
is integrable over
(0,T)
. Gronwall’s lemma guarantees that there exists a universal constant
\lambda>0
such that

e^{\lambda t}\mathcal{E}(t)\lesssim\mathcal{E}(0)
,
for all
t<T
. Then the inequality (
6.13
) also implies

\int_{0}^{T}\mathcal{D}\lesssim\mathcal{E}(0).

∎

We note that this theorem is conditional. To ensure it is non-vacuous, we must prove that the set of initial data satisfying the limiting conditions is nonempty. Thanks to Theorem
B.1
, the inclusion of the term
\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)\in W^{2-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma)
in addition to the initial energy
\mathcal{E}(0)
guarantees that such initial data exist and satisfy the conditions in Appendix
B
. Consequently, it is sufficient to complete the global well-posedness in Theorem
1.1
.

Appendix A
Forcing Terms in the Linear System

Define

(A.1)

\displaystyle{}X:=\{(u,p)|(u,p)\in W^{2,q_{-}}\times W^{1,q_{-}}\},

and

(A.2)

\displaystyle{}Y:=\{(F^{1},F^{2},F^{4},F^{4_{+}},F^{5})|(F^{1},F^{2},F^{4},F^{4_{+}},F^{5})\in L^{q_{-}}\times W^{1,q_{-}}\times W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}\times W^{2-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}\times W^{1-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}\}.

We now present the nonlinear interaction terms for (
1.17
). From (
1.7
), we explicitly set

\displaystyle F^{1}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}S_{\mathcal{A}}(p,u)+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}}\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u+\partial_{t}u\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}}u+u\cdot\nabla_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u+u\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}}\partial_{t}u+\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}WK\partial_{2}u),

\displaystyle F^{2}

\displaystyle=\operatorname{div}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u,

\displaystyle F^{4}

\displaystyle=\mu\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u\mathcal{N}+\mu S_{\mathcal{A}}(p,u)\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}+\left[g(\eta)-\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}\eta}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\right)-\sigma\partial_{1}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)\right]\partial_{t}\mathcal{N},

\displaystyle F^{5}

\displaystyle=\mu\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u\nu\cdot\tau,

\displaystyle F^{7}

\displaystyle=\kappa\hat{\mathscr{W}}^{\prime}(u\cdot\mathcal{N})\partial_{t}u\cdot\mathcal{N}(\pm\ell).

Appendix B
Initial Data for the Nonlinear System

Our aim is to use the Galerkin method to establish the existence and uniqueness of solutions to (
1.7
). Therefore, it is crucial to ensure that the initial data of the sequence of approximate solutions converge to the initial data of PDE (
1.7
).
Now we state our theorem for the construction of initial conditions for nonlinear system (
1.7
).

Theorem B.1
.

Suppose that the initial data
\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)\in\mathring{H}^{1}(\Sigma)\cap W^{2-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma)
and
D_{t}^{2}u(0)\in H^{0}(\Omega)
are given and satisfy
\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}D_{t}^{2}u(0)=0
. Then we can construct
(u_{0},p_{0},\eta_{0},\partial_{t}u(0),\partial_{t}p(0),\partial_{t}\eta(0))

solving the system (
1.7
) at
t=0
such that

(B.1)

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}(0)+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)\|_{W^{2-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}\leq\delta

for a universal constant
\delta>0
sufficiently small.

Remark B.1
.

It is important to note that the initial data in Theorem
B.1
implies the finiteness of
\mathcal{E}(0)
, where
\mathcal{E}
is defined in (
4.7
). So, Theorem
B.1
guarantees that our initial data for nonlinear system (
1.7
) are well defined.

B.1.
Initial Data for the Nonlinear System

In this section, we construct the initial data for the nonlinear system (
6.1
). Evaluating the system (
6.1
) at
t=0
, we define the initial forcing terms

(B.2)

\displaystyle F^{1,j}(0)\in L^{q_{-}}(\Omega),\ F^{2,j}(0)\in W^{1,q_{-}}(\Omega)\ ,F^{3,j}\in W^{2-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}(\Sigma),

\displaystyle F^{4,j}(0)\in W^{1-1/q_{-},q_{-}}(\Sigma),F^{6,j}\in W^{2-\frac{1}{q_{-}},q_{-}}(\Sigma),F^{5,j}(0)\in W^{1-1/q_{-},q_{-}}(\Sigma_{s}),

are in terms of
(u_{0},p_{0},\eta_{0},\partial_{t}u(0),\partial_{t}p(0),\partial_{t}\eta(0),\partial_{t}^{2}u(0),\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0))
, and
\mathcal{A}_{0}
,
\mathcal{N}(0)
,
J(0)=1/K(0)
are in terms of
\eta_{0}
, and
R(0)
are in terms of
(\eta_{0},\partial_{t}\eta(0))
. We replace
(u,p,\eta)
with
(v,q,\xi)
on the left hand side of the system.

We now use elliptic theory to construct
(D_{t}u(0),\partial_{t}p(0),\partial_{t}\xi(0))
. First, we define the initial forcing terms
F^{1,1}(0)
,
F^{3,1}(0)
,
F^{4,1}(0)
and
F^{5,1}(0)
as

(B.3)

\displaystyle F^{1,1}:=\partial_{t}F^{1,0}+\mathfrak{G}^{1}(u,p),\quad F^{3,1}(0)=\partial_{t}F^{3,0}(0),

\displaystyle F^{4,1}:=\partial_{t}F^{4,0}+\mathfrak{G}^{4}(u,p,\xi),\quad F^{5,1}:=\partial_{t}F^{5,0}+\mathfrak{G}^{5}(u),

where

(B.4)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{G}^{1}(u,p)

\displaystyle=-\partial_{t}Ru-R^{2}u+\nabla_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}p+\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}}\left(\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}}(Ru)+\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u)+\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}}u\right),

\displaystyle\mathfrak{G}^{4}(u,p,\xi)

\displaystyle=\mu\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}}(Ru)\mathcal{N}-(pI-\mu\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}}u)\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}+\mu\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u\mathcal{N}+\mathcal{K}(\xi)\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}-\sigma\partial_{1}F^{3}\partial_{t}\mathcal{N},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{G}^{5}(u)

\displaystyle=(\mu\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}}(Ru)\nu+\mu\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}}u\nu+\beta Ru)\cdot\tau.

and

(B.5)

{}F^{1,0}=\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}WK\partial_{2}u-u\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}}u,\quad F^{3,0}=\sigma\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta),\quad F^{4,0}=0,\quad F^{5,0}=0,\quad F^{7,0}=\kappa\hat{\mathscr{W}}(\partial_{t}\eta).

Provided
u
,
p
and
\xi
are sufficiently regular, we define

(B.6)

F^{1,2}:=\mathfrak{G}^{1}(D_{t}u,\partial_{t}p)+D_{t}F^{1,1},\ F^{4,2}:=\mathfrak{G}^{4}(D_{t}u,\partial_{t}p,\partial_{t}\xi)+\partial_{t}F^{4,1},\ F^{5,2}:=\mathfrak{G}^{5}(D_{t}u)+\partial_{t}F^{5,1}.

Then we have the following lemma.

Lemma B.2
.

The following estimates hold:

(B.7)

\displaystyle\|F^{1,1}(0)\|_{L^{q_{+}}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}F^{1,0}(0)\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\|F^{1,0}(0)\|_{L^{q_{+}}}

\displaystyle+\big(\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)\|_{W^{2-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\big)\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\big(\|p_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}\big),

(B.8)

\displaystyle\|F^{4,1}(0)\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\big(\|p_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|\xi_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\xi(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{1}F^{3,0}(0)\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma)}\big),

and

(B.9)

\displaystyle\|F^{5,1}(0)\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma_{s})}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}.

These (
B.7
)– (
B.9
) imply

\displaystyle F^{1,1}(0)\in L^{q_{+}}(\Omega),\quad\partial_{1}F^{3,1}(0)=\partial_{1}\partial_{t}F^{3,0}(0)\in W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma),

\displaystyle F^{4,1}(0)\in W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma),\quad F^{5,1}(0)\in W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma_{s}).

Proof.

By the assumption that
F^{1,0}(0)
,
\partial_{t}F^{1,0}(0)\in L^{q_{+}}
, and
D_{t}F^{1,0}(0)=\partial_{t}F^{1,0}(0)+R^{1}(0)F^{1,0}(0)
, we directly deduce that

(B.10)

\displaystyle\|D_{t}F^{1,0}(0)\|_{L^{q_{+}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}F^{1,0}(0)\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|R(0)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|F^{1,0}(0)\|_{L^{q_{+}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}F^{1,0}(0)\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\|F^{1,0}(0)\|_{L^{q_{+}}}.

We now estimate
\mathfrak{G}^{1}(u_{0},p_{0})
term by term. By
\|\mathcal{A}_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\lesssim 1
, we have

(B.11)

\displaystyle\|(R(0)+\partial_{t}J(0)K(0))\Delta_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|R(0)+\partial_{t}J(0)K(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}(\|\nabla^{2}u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|\nabla\mathcal{A}_{0}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla u_{0}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{+})}})

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}(1+\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}})\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}.

Similarly, we have

(B.12)

\|(\partial_{t}J(0)K(0)+R(0)+R^{T}(0))\nabla_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}p_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\|p_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}},

and

(B.13)

\displaystyle\|\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}\left(\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}(R(0)u_{0})+\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}(0)}u_{0}-R(0)\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}u_{0}\right)\|_{L^{q_{+}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla\mathcal{A}_{0}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla(R(0)u_{0})\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{+})}}+\|\nabla^{2}(R(0)u_{0})\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\nabla^{2}u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}(0)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla u_{0}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{+})}}+\|\nabla R(0)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla u_{0}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{+})}}+\|R(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\nabla^{2}u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}

\displaystyle+\|R(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\nabla\mathcal{A}_{0}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla u_{0}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{+})}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla\mathcal{A}_{0}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}(\|\nabla R(0)\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{+})}}\|u_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|R(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\nabla u_{0}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{+})}})

\displaystyle+\|\nabla^{2}R(0)\|_{L^{q_{+}}(\Omega)}\|u_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|R(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\nabla u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|\nabla R(0)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla u_{0}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{+})}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\nabla^{2}u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|\nabla\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}(0)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla u_{0}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{+})}}+\|\nabla R(0)\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla u_{0}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{+})}}

\displaystyle+\|R(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\nabla^{2}u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|R(0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\|\nabla\mathcal{A}_{0}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega)}\|\nabla u_{0}\|_{L^{2/(1-\varepsilon_{+})}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}(1+\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}})\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}.

Then we estimate

(B.14)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}R(0)u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|\nabla\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}(0)\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|\nabla\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}(0)\|_{L^{q_{+}}})\|u_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)\|_{W^{2-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2})\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}.

The combination of (
B.11
)–(
B.14
) gives the bound of
\mathfrak{G}^{1}(v_{0},q_{0})
, which together with (
B.10
) imply (
B.7
).

We now consider the bounds for
\mathfrak{G}^{4}(u_{0},p_{0},\xi_{0})
. We first employ Sobolev embedding theory and usual trace theory to deduce that

(B.15)

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}(R(0)u_{0})\mathcal{N}(0)\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}(R(0)u_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\nabla R(0)u_{0})\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|R(0)\nabla u_{0})\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|\nabla^{2}R(0)u_{0})\|_{L^{q_{+}}}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla R(0)\nabla u_{0})\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|R(0)\nabla^{2}u_{0})\|_{L^{q_{+}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}.

The similar argument allows us to deduce

(B.16)

\displaystyle\|(p_{0}I-\mu\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}u_{0})\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}(0)\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|p_{0}I-\mu\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}u_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}(\Omega)}\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}(0)\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\|p_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}+\||\mathcal{A}_{0}|\nabla u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\||\nabla\mathcal{A}_{0}|\nabla u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\||\mathcal{A}_{0}|\nabla^{2}u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}})\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}(\|p_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}),

and

(B.17)

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}(0)}u_{0}\mathcal{N}(0)\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma)}\lesssim\|\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}(0)}u_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\||\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}(0)|\nabla u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\||\nabla\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}(0)|\nabla u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\||\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}_{0}|\nabla^{2}u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}})\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}.

For the capillary term, we have

(B.18)

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}(\xi_{0})\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}(0)\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathcal{K}(\xi_{0})\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma)}\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}(0)\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}(\|\xi_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}\xi(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}).

The assumption for
\partial_{1}F^{3}(0)
shows that

(B.19)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{1}F^{3,0}(0)\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{1}F^{3,0}(0)\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma)}\|\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}(0)\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\|\partial_{1}F^{3,0}(0)\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma)}.

The estimates (
B.15
)–(
B.19
) along with the assumption
\partial_{t}F^{4}(0)\in W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma)
imply (
B.8
).

Finally, we estimate
\mathfrak{G}^{5}(u_{0})
. We first bound that

(B.20)

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}(R(0)u_{0})\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma_{s})}+\|\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}(0)}u_{0}\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma_{s})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}(R(0)u_{0})\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}(\Omega)}+\|\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}(0)}u_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}(\Omega)}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}.

We then bound the remainder terms:

(B.21)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}J(0)K(0)\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}(0)}u_{0}\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma_{s})}+\|R(0)u_{0}\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma_{s})}+\|\partial_{t}J(0)K(0)u_{0}\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma_{s})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}J(0)K(0)\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}(\Sigma_{s})}\|\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}(0)}u_{0}\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma_{s})}+\||R(0)|+|\partial_{t}J(0)K(0)|\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}(\Sigma_{s})}\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}.

Inequalities (
B.20
) and (
B.21
) yield the bound (
B.9
).
∎

The result for the nonlinear system is given in Theorem
B.1
.
The proof of Theorem
B.1
relies on the Banach fixed-point theorem. We first assume that
(u_{0},p_{0},\eta_{0})
,
(D_{t}u(0),\partial_{t}p(0),\partial_{t}\eta(0))
, and
(D_{t}^{2}u(0),\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0))
are given so that

\mathcal{E}(u,p,\eta)(0)+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)\|_{W^{2-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}\leq\delta
. We then show that the forcing terms are well-defined in Lemma
B.3
. Then we denote the unknowns
(v_{0},q_{0},\xi_{0})
in (
6.1
) with
j=0
at time
t=0
instead of
(u_{0},p_{0},\xi_{0})
, and the unknowns
(\partial_{t}v(0),\partial_{t}q(0),\partial_{t}\xi(0))
in (
6.1
) with
j=1
at time
t=0
instead of
(\partial_{t}u(0),\partial_{t}p(0),\partial_{t}\xi(0))
, and the unknowns
(\partial_{t}^{2}v(0),\partial_{t}^{2}q(0),\partial_{t}^{2}\xi(0))
in (
6.1
) with
j=2
at time
t=0
instead of
(\partial_{t}^{2}u(0),\partial_{t}^{2}p(0),\partial_{t}^{2}\xi(0))
, and obtain the bound
\mathcal{E}(v,q,\xi)(0)+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\xi(0)\|_{W^{2-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}\leq\delta
for nonlinear initial data, subject to the assumption that
D_{t}^{2}v(0)=D_{t}^{2}u(0)
and
\partial_{t}^{2}\xi(0)=\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)
. Finally, we use a contraction mapping argument to prove the existence of fixed points.

Lemma B.3
.

It holds that

(B.22)

\displaystyle\|F^{1,0}(0)\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{1}F^{1,0}(0)\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}+[F^{7,0}(0)]_{\ell}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}(\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2})+\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}

and

(B.23)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}F^{1,0}(0)\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{1}\partial_{t}F^{3,0}(0)\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}+[\partial_{t}F^{7,0}(0)]_{\ell}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}(\|D_{t}u(0)\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}})

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}(\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}).

Proof.

From the definition of
F^{1}(0)
, we directly use Hölder’s inequality to show that

(B.24)

\displaystyle\|F^{1,0}(0)\|_{L^{q_{+}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}(0)\partial_{2}u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|u_{0}\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}\alpha)/2}}\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2},

where in the last inequality, we have used the fact
\|\mathcal{A}_{0}\|\lesssim 1
, and the Sobolev embedding theory and the usual trace theory.
By definition of
\mathcal{R}
, we have
\partial_{1}F^{3,0}(0)=\partial_{y}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta_{0})\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}+\partial_{z}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta_{0})\partial_{1}\eta_{0}
. By the boundedness of
\partial_{y}\mathcal{R}
,
\partial_{z}\mathcal{R}
and
\partial_{z}^{2}\mathcal{R}
, we have

(B.25)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{1}F^{3,0}(0)\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\lesssim\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}.

By property
|\hat{\mathscr{W}}(z)|\lesssim|z|^{2}
, we have

(B.26)

[F^{7,0}(0)]_{\ell}\lesssim[\partial_{t}\eta(0)]_{\ell}\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}.

The combination of (
B.24
), (
B.25
) and (
B.26
) implies (
B.22
).

We estimate
\partial_{t}F^{1,0}(0)
term by term. First, by the Hölder’s inequality, and the Sobolev embedding theory and the usual trace theory, we have that

(B.27)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}(0)K(0)\partial_{2}u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}^{2}\bar{\eta}(0)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{2}u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}},

where we have used
\|K(0)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 1
. Similarly,

(B.28)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}(0)\partial_{t}K(0)W\partial_{2}u_{0}+\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}(0)K(0)W\partial_{2}D_{t}u(0)\|_{L^{q_{+}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}(\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|D_{t}u(0)\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}),

and

(B.29)

\|\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}(0)K(0)W\partial_{2}(R(0)u_{0})\|_{L^{q_{+}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\|R(0)u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}.

Then the lower order terms are bounded by

(B.30)

\displaystyle\|D_{t}u(0)\nabla_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|u_{0}\nabla_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}(0)}u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}\lesssim(\|D_{t}u(0)\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}})\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}.

and

(B.31)

\displaystyle\|u_{0}\nabla_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}D_{t}u(0)\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|(R(0)u_{0})\nabla_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}u_{0}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|u_{0}\nabla_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}(R(0)u_{0})\|_{L^{q_{+}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}(\|D_{t}u(0)\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}).

We expand

\displaystyle\partial_{1}\partial_{t}F^{3,0}(0)

\displaystyle=\partial_{yz}^{2}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta_{0})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta(0)\partial_{1}^{2}\zeta_{0}+\partial_{zz}^{2}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta_{0})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta(0)\partial_{1}^{2}\eta_{0}+\partial_{z}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta_{0})\partial_{1}^{2}\partial_{t}\eta(0),

and then use the fact that
W^{1,q_{+}}(\Sigma)
is a Banach algebra to estimate

(B.32)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{1}\partial_{t}F^{3,0}(0)\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{yz}^{2}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta_{0})\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}\|\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{zz}^{2}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta_{0})\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}\|\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta(0)\partial_{1}^{2}\eta_{0}\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{z}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta_{0})\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}\|\partial_{1}^{2}\partial_{t}\eta_{0}\|_{W^{1-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\|\eta+\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}},

where we have used the facts
|\partial_{yz}^{2}\mathcal{R}(y,z)|\lesssim|z|
and
|\partial_{zz}^{2}\mathcal{R}(y,z)|\lesssim 1
.

The definition of
\hat{\mathscr{W}}
gives the bound
|\hat{\mathscr{W}}^{\prime}(z)|\lesssim|z|
for
|z|\lesssim 1
. Then

(B.33)

[\partial_{t}F^{7,0}(0)]_{\ell}\lesssim\max_{\pm\ell}|\partial_{t}\eta(0)||\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)|\lesssim\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}.

So all the above estimates from (
B.27
)–(
B.33
) show the estimate (
B.23
).
∎

To establish the contraction, we denote velocities by
v^{i}
,
u^{i}
, pressures by
q^{i}
,
p^{i}
, and surface functions by
\eta^{i}
,
\xi^{i}
for
i=1,2
. We suppose that for
j=1,2
,
(u_{0}^{j},p_{0}^{j},\eta_{0}^{j},D_{t}u^{j}(0),\partial_{t}p^{j}(0),\partial_{t}\eta^{j}(0))

as well as
(D_{t}^{2}v^{1}(0),\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{1}(0))=(D_{t}^{2}v^{2}(0),\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{2}(0))
are two initial data for the nonlinear system (
1.7
) with
\xi
replaced by
\eta
. We then consider the following systems with
j=0,1

(B.34)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &(D_{t}^{j+1}v^{i})(0)+\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{i}_{0}}(\partial_{t}^{j}q^{i})(0)-\mu\Delta_{\mathcal{A}^{i}_{0}}(D_{t}^{j}v^{i}(0)+2^{j}R^{i}(0)D_{t}^{j}v^{i}(0)=\mathfrak{f}^{1,i}_{j}(0)\quad&\text{in}&\quad\Omega,\\
&\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{i}_{0}}(D_{t}^{j}v^{i})(0)=0\quad&\text{in}&\quad\Omega,\\
&S_{\mathcal{A}^{i}_{0}}(\partial_{t}^{j}q^{i}(0),D_{t}^{j}v^{i}(0))\mathcal{N}^{i}(0)=\bigg[g(\partial_{t}^{j}\xi^{i}(0))-\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}^{j}\xi^{i}(0)}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\right)-\partial_{1}\mathfrak{f}^{3,i}_{j}(0)\bigg]\mathcal{N}(0)+\mathfrak{f}^{4,i}_{j}(0)\quad&\text{on}&\quad\Sigma,\\
&D_{t}^{j}v^{i}(0)\cdot\mathcal{N}^{i}(0)=\partial_{t}^{j+1}\xi^{i}(0)\quad&\text{on}&\quad\Sigma,\\
&(S_{\mathcal{A}^{i}_{0}}(\partial_{t}^{j}q^{i}(0),D_{t}^{j}v^{i}(0))\nu-\beta D_{t}^{j}v^{i}(0))\cdot\tau=\mathfrak{f}^{5,i}_{j}(0)\quad&\text{on}&\quad\Sigma_{s},\\
&D_{t}^{j}v^{i}(0)\cdot\nu=0\quad&\text{on}&\quad\Sigma_{s},\\
&\mp\sigma\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}^{j}\xi^{i}(0)}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}(\pm\ell)=\kappa(D_{t}^{j}v^{i}(0)\cdot\mathcal{N}^{i}(0))(\pm\ell)\pm\mathfrak{f}^{3,i}_{j}(\pm\ell,0)-\mathfrak{f}^{7,i}_{j}(\pm\ell,0),\end{aligned}\right.

where
\mathfrak{f}^{k,i}_{0}=F^{j,0}(u_{0}^{i},\eta_{0}^{i},\partial_{t}\eta^{i}(0))
for
i=1,2
,
k=1,3,4,5
,
\mathfrak{f}^{7,i}_{0}=F^{7,0}(\partial_{t}\eta^{i}(0))
and for
i=1,2
,

\mathfrak{f}^{k,i}_{1}=\partial_{t}F^{k,0}(u_{0}^{i},\eta_{0}^{i},D_{t}u^{i}(0),\partial_{t}\eta^{i}(0))+\mathfrak{G}^{k}(v^{i}_{0},q^{i}_{0},\xi^{i}_{0}),

k=1,4,5
,
\mathfrak{f}^{3,i}_{1}=\partial_{t}F^{3,0}(\eta_{0}^{i},\partial_{t}\eta^{i}(0))
and
\mathfrak{f}^{7,i}_{1}=\partial_{t}F^{7,0}(\partial_{t}\eta^{i}(0))
. We define
\mathcal{A}^{i}
,
\mathcal{N}^{i}
, etc, in terms of
\eta^{i}
,
i=1,2
.
Set

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathfrak{E}}_{0}(u_{0}^{i},p_{0}^{i},\eta_{0}^{i},D_{t}u^{i}(0),\partial_{t}p^{i}(0),\partial_{t}\xi^{i}(0))=\|D_{t}u^{i}(0)\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}p^{i}(0)\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\xi^{i}(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|u_{0}^{i}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|p_{0}^{i}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\xi_{0}^{i}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}.

Lemma
B.3
directly shows that

Lemma B.4
.

The solutions
(v^{i}_{0},q^{i}_{0},\xi^{i}_{0})
and
(D_{t}v^{i}(0),\partial_{t}q^{i}(0),\partial_{t}\xi^{i}(0))
solving (
B.34
) are bounded by

\displaystyle\|v_{0}^{i}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|q_{0}^{i}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\xi_{0}^{i}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}+\|D_{t}v(0)\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}q(0)\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\xi(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|D_{t}^{2}u(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}+\widetilde{\mathfrak{E}}_{0}(u_{0}^{i},p_{0}^{i},\eta_{0}^{i},D_{t}u^{i}(0),\partial_{t}p^{i}(0),\partial_{t}\eta^{i}(0)).

Therefore, by assuming
\|D_{t}^{2}u(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}\lesssim\delta_{0}
, we have

(B.35)

\widetilde{\mathfrak{E}}_{0}(v_{0}^{i},q_{0}^{i},\xi_{0}^{i},D_{t}v^{i}(0),\partial_{t}q^{i}(0),\partial_{t}\xi^{i}(0))\lesssim\delta

for a universal constant
\delta
sufficiently small,
i=1,2
.

For simplicity, we might abuse the notation to denote

(B.36)

u_{0}=u_{0}^{1}-u_{0}^{2},\ p_{0}=p_{0}^{1}-p_{0}^{2},\ \xi_{0}=\xi_{0}^{1}-\xi_{0}^{2},\ v_{0}=v_{0}^{1}-v_{0}^{2},\ q_{0}=q_{0}^{1}-q_{0}^{2},\ \eta_{0}=\eta_{0}^{1}-\eta_{0}^{2},

and

(B.37)

\displaystyle D_{t}u(0)=D_{t}u^{1}(0)-D_{t}u^{2}(0),\ \partial_{t}p(0)=\partial_{t}p^{1}(0)-\partial_{t}p^{2}(0),\ \partial_{t}\xi(0)=\partial_{t}\xi^{1}(0)-\partial_{t}\xi^{2}(0),

\displaystyle D_{t}v(0)=D_{t}v^{1}(0)-D_{t}v^{2}(0),\ \partial_{t}q=\partial_{t}q(0)^{1}-\partial_{t}q(0)^{2},\ \partial_{t}\eta(0)=\partial_{t}\eta(0)^{1}-\partial_{t}\eta(0)^{2}.

Lemma B.5
.

The triple
(D_{t}v(0),\partial_{t}q(0),\partial_{t}\xi(0))
in (
B.37
) obeys the estimate

(B.38)

\displaystyle\|D_{t}v(0)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}q(0)\|_{H^{0}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\xi(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}\leq C\delta\big(\|D_{t}u(0)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}\|v_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|q_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\xi_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}\big)

where
C
