is a universal constant that may change from line to line.

Proof.

By
(D_{t}^{2}v^{1}(0),\partial_{t}^{2}\xi^{1}(0))=(D_{t}^{2}v^{2}(0),\partial_{t}^{2}\xi^{2}(0))
, the difference of (
B.37
) satisfies the equations

(B.39)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\nabla_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}}\partial_{t}^{j}q-\Delta_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}}D_{t}^{j}v+2^{j}R(0)^{1}D_{t}^{j}v=\mathfrak{g}^{1}_{j}&\text{in}&\ \Omega,\\
&\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}}D_{t}^{j}v=\mathfrak{g}^{2}_{j}&\text{in}&\ \Omega,\\
&S_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}}(\partial_{t}^{j}q,D_{t}^{j}v)\mathcal{N}^{1}(0)=\bigg[g\partial_{t}^{j}\xi(0)-\sigma\partial_{1}\bigg(\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}^{j}\xi(0)}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\bigg)-\partial_{1}\mathfrak{g}^{3}_{j}\bigg]\mathcal{N}^{1}(0)+\mathfrak{g}^{4}_{j}&\text{on}&\ \Sigma,\\
&D_{t}^{j}u\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}(0)=\mathfrak{g}^{6}_{j}\quad&\text{on}&\ \Sigma,\\
&(S_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}}(\partial_{t}^{j}q,D_{t}^{j}v)\nu-\beta D_{t}^{j}v)\cdot\tau=\mathfrak{g}^{5}_{j}&\text{on}&\ \Sigma_{s},\\
&D_{t}^{j}v\cdot\nu=0&\text{on}&\ \Sigma_{s},\\
&\mp\sigma\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}^{j}\xi(0)}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}(\pm\ell)=\kappa D_{t}^{j}v\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}(0)(\pm\ell)\pm\mathfrak{g}^{3}_{j}(\pm\ell,0)-\mathfrak{g}^{7}_{j}(\pm\ell,0)\end{aligned}\right.

with
j=1
, where the forcing terms
\mathfrak{g}^{k}_{1}
,
k=1,\ldots,7
, are presented in Appendix
B.2
.

We multiply the first equation of (
B.39
) by
J^{1}(0)D_{t}v(0)
, then integrate over
\Omega
and integrate by parts to obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\mu}{2}\int_{\Omega}|\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}^{1}_{0}}D_{t}v(0)|^{2}J^{1}(0)+\beta\int_{\Sigma_{s}}|D_{t}v(0)|^{2}J^{1}(0)=\int_{\Omega}\mathfrak{g}^{1}\cdot D_{t}v(0)J^{1}(0)+\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}q(0)\mathfrak{g}^{2}J^{1}(0)-(\partial_{t}\xi(0),\mathfrak{g}^{6})_{1,\Sigma}

\displaystyle-\int_{\Sigma}\mathfrak{g}^{3}\partial_{1}(\mathfrak{g}^{6})-\int_{\Sigma}\mathfrak{g}^{4}\cdot(D_{t}v(0))-\int_{\Sigma_{s}}\mathfrak{g}^{5}(D_{t}v(0))\cdot\tau J^{1}(0),

where we have used the fact
\mathfrak{g}^{6}(\pm\ell)=0
on the contact points. By the assumption (
B.35
) and integration by parts, we obtain the bounds

(B.40)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Omega}\mathfrak{g}^{1}\cdot(D_{t}v(0))J^{1}(0)-\int_{\Sigma}\mathfrak{g}^{4}\cdot(D_{t}v(0))-\int_{\Sigma_{s}}\mathfrak{g}^{5}(D_{t}v(0))\cdot\tau J^{1}(0)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{1/2}(\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}+\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}+\|D_{t}u(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}

\displaystyle+\|v_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|q_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}+\|\xi_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}})\|D_{t}v(0)\|_{H^{1}}.

Similarly, we have

(B.41)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}q(0)\mathfrak{g}^{2}J^{1}(0)-(\partial_{t}\xi(0),\mathfrak{g}^{6})_{1,\Sigma}-\int_{\Sigma}\mathfrak{g}^{3}\partial_{1}(\mathfrak{g}^{6})\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{1/2}(\|\partial_{t}q(0)\|_{H^{0}}+\|\partial_{t}\xi(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}+\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{3/2}})\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}.

By Cauchy’s inequality, we have the bounds

(B.42)

\displaystyle\|D_{t}v(0)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\delta(\|D_{t}u(0)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2})

\displaystyle+C\delta(\|v_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|q_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\xi_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}q(0)\|_{H^{0}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\xi(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}).

It is observed from (
B.41
) and (
B.42
), we have to estimate the difference
\partial_{t}q(0)
and
\partial_{t}\xi(0)
. We now estimate the difference
\partial_{t}q(0)
. We first define
w=M^{1}(0)\nabla\psi
, where
\psi\in H^{2}(\Omega)
solves

-\Delta\psi=\partial_{t}q(0)\quad\text{in}\ \Omega,\quad\psi=0\quad\text{on}\ \Sigma,\quad\partial_{\nu}\psi=0\quad\text{on}\ \Sigma_{s},

which, by the elliptic theory on the
\Omega
with convex corners, enjoys the estimate

\|\psi\|_{H^{2}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}q(0)\|_{H^{0}}
.
Then we have

(B.43)

\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}_{0}}w=\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{1}_{0}}M^{1}(0)\nabla\psi=K^{1}(0)[\partial_{t}q(0)],\quad\text{satisfying}\quad\|w\|_{H^{1}}\lesssim\|\partial_{t}q(0)\|_{H^{0}}.

We now multiply the first equation of (
B.39
) by
J^{1}(0)w
, and then integrate over
\Omega
and integrate by parts, together with (
B.43
), to deduce that

(B.44)

\displaystyle-\|\partial_{t}q(0)\|_{H^{0}}^{2}+\frac{\mu}{2}\int_{\Omega}(\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}}(D_{t}v(0)):\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}}w)J^{1}(0)=\int_{\Omega}\mathfrak{g}^{1}\cdot J^{1}(0)w-\int_{\Sigma_{s}}\mathfrak{g}^{5}(w\cdot\tau)J^{1}(0)

\displaystyle-\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\left[g\partial_{t}\xi(0)-\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi(0)}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}+\mathfrak{g}^{3}\right)\right]w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}(0)+\mathfrak{g}^{4}\cdot w.

Then by (
B.43
), we estimate

(B.45)

\displaystyle|\int_{\Omega}(\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}}(D_{t}v(0)):\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}}w)J^{1}(0)|\lesssim\|D_{t}v(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|w\|_{H^{1}}\lesssim\|D_{t}v(0)\|_{H^{1}}\|\partial_{t}q(0)\|_{H^{0}}.

The same argument as (
B.40
) implies that

(B.46)

\displaystyle|\int_{\Omega}\mathfrak{g}^{1}\cdot J^{1}(0)w-\int_{\Sigma_{s}}\mathfrak{g}^{5}(w\cdot\tau)J^{1}(0)-\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\mathfrak{g}^{4}\cdot w|

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{1/2}(\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}+\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}+\|D_{t}u(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}

\displaystyle+\|v_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|q_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}+\|\xi_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}})\|w\|_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta^{1/2}(\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}+\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}+\|D_{t}u(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}

\displaystyle+\|v_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|q_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}+\|\xi_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}})\|\partial_{t}q(0)\|_{H^{0}}.

We directly estimate

(B.47)

\displaystyle|\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\left[g\partial_{t}\xi(0)-\sigma\partial_{1}\left(\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi(0)}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}+\mathfrak{g}^{3}\right)\right]w\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}(0)|\lesssim(\|\partial_{t}\xi(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}+\|\mathfrak{g}^{3}\|_{H^{1/2}})\|w\|_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim[\|\partial_{t}\xi(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}+\delta^{1/2}(\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}+\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}})]\|\partial_{t}q(0)\|_{H^{0}}.

So (
B.44
), together with (
B.45
)–(
B.47
) implies that

(B.48)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}q(0)\|_{H^{0}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|D_{t}v(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\|\partial_{t}\xi(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}+\delta^{1/2}(\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}+\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}

\displaystyle+\|D_{t}u(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|v_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|q_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}+\|\xi_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}).

We now estimate the difference
\partial_{t}\xi(0)
. The weak formulation for (
B.39
) might be given as

(B.49)

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}\xi(0),\varphi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}(0))_{1,\Sigma}+\frac{\mu}{2}\int_{\Omega}(\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}}(D_{t}u(0)):\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}}w)J^{1}(0)

\displaystyle=\int_{\Omega}\mathfrak{g}^{1}\cdot J^{1}(0)\varphi-\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\mathfrak{g}^{3}\partial_{1}(\varphi\cdot\mathcal{N}^{1}(0))+\mathfrak{g}^{4}\cdot\varphi-\int_{\Sigma_{s}}\mathfrak{g}^{5}(\varphi\cdot\tau)J^{1}(0)

for each
\varphi\in\mathcal{W}_{\sigma}(\Omega)
. Then the dissipation theory for the surface function developed in
[
9
]
can be directly applied to (
B.49
) to obtain the estimate

(B.50)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\xi(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|D_{t}v(0)\|_{H^{1}}+\delta^{1/2}(\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}+\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}+\|D_{t}u(0)\|_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|v_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|q_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}+\|\xi_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}).

In order to obtain the estimate (
B.38
), we plug (
B.48
) and (
B.50
) into (
B.42
) to obtain

(B.51)

\displaystyle\|D_{t}v(0)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\delta(\|D_{t}u(0)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2})+C\delta(\|v_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|q_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\xi_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}).

for
\delta
sufficiently small. Then we plunge (
B.51
) into (
B.50
) to bound
(\partial_{t}\xi^{1}(0)-\partial_{t}\xi^{2}(0))
by the right-hand side of inequality in (
B.38
). Finally, we plunge (
B.51
) and the resulting estimate for
(\partial_{t}\xi^{1}(0)-\partial_{t}\xi^{2}(0))
into (
B.48
) to bound the
\partial_{t}q^{1}(0)-\partial_{t}q^{2}(0)
to obtain the estimate (
B.38
).
∎

In order to get the contraction result, we have to estimate the difference of (
B.36
).

Lemma B.6
.

The triple
(v_{0},q_{0},\xi_{0})
in (
B.36
) obeys the estimate

\displaystyle\|v_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|q_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\xi_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}\lesssim\|D_{t}v(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta(\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}).

Proof.

The differences defined in (
B.36
) satisfy equations (
B.39
) for j=0, where the forcing terms
\mathfrak{g}_{0}^{j}
are given below. To apply the elliptic theory in
[
10
, Theorem 4.9]
, we need to guarantee that the assumptions in the elliptic estimates are meaningful. We employ the Hölder’s inequality, Sobolev embedding theory and usual trace theory to deduce the following estimates.

(B.52)

\displaystyle\|(R(0)^{1}-R(0)^{2})v_{0}^{2}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{3/2+(\varepsilon_{-}-\alpha)/2}},

(B.53)

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}-\mathcal{A}_{0}^{2}}q_{0}^{2}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}+\|\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}-\mathcal{A}_{0}^{2}}\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}}v_{0}^{2}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}},

(B.54)

\|\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{2}}\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}-\mathcal{A}_{0}^{2}}u_{0}^{2}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}},

(B.55)

\|\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}-\mathcal{A}_{0}^{2}}v_{0}^{2}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}},

(B.56)

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{f}^{1}\|_{L^{q_{+}}}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}(\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}),

(B.57)

\|v_{0}^{2}\cdot[\mathcal{N}^{1}(0)-\mathcal{N}^{2}(0)]\|_{W^{2-1/q_{+},q_{+}}(\Sigma)}\lesssim\delta^{1/2}\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}.

Then by the elliptic theory and the above estimates (
B.52
)–(
B.57
), with
\delta
sufficiently small,

\displaystyle\|v_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|q_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\xi_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}\lesssim\|D_{t}v(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta(\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\eta(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\|u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}).

∎

The Lemmas
B.5
and
B.6
give the initial data for
\varepsilon
- nonlinear system (
1.7
).

Proof of Theorem
B.1
.

The results in Lemma
B.5
and Lemma
B.6
imply the estimates

\displaystyle\|v_{0}^{1}-v_{0}^{2}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|q_{0}^{1}-q_{0}^{2}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\xi_{0}^{1}-\xi_{0}^{2}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}+\|D_{t}v^{1}(0)-D_{t}v^{2}(0)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}q^{1}(0)-\partial_{t}q^{2}(0)\|_{H^{0}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\xi^{1}(0)-\partial_{t}\xi^{2}(0)\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}(\|D_{t}u^{1}(0)-D_{t}u^{2}(0)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|u_{0}^{1}-u_{0}^{2}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\eta_{0}^{1}-\eta_{0}^{2}\|_{W^{3-1/q_{+},q_{+}}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\eta^{1}(0)-\partial_{t}\eta^{2}(0)\|_{H^{3/2-\alpha}}^{2})

for
\delta
sufficiently small.

Then by the Banach’s fixed point theory, given
D_{t}^{2}u(0)
and
\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)
satisfying the conditions in assumptions in Theorem
B.1
, there exist one unique
(u_{0},p_{0},\eta_{0},D_{t}u(0),\partial_{t}p(0),\partial_{t}\eta(0))
satisfying the full initial conditions.
∎

Denote

\displaystyle\mathscr{F}_{j}(\psi)=\int_{\Omega}F^{1,j}(0)\cdot\psi J(0)-\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}F^{3,j}(0)\partial_{1}(\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}(0))+F^{4,j}(0)\cdot\psi-\int_{\Sigma_{s}}F^{5,j}(0)(\psi\cdot\tau)J(0)

\displaystyle-[F^{7,j}(0),\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}(0)]_{\ell}

for
j=0,1
.
The weak solution to (
1.7
) at
t=0
for j=0 satisfies

(B.58)

\displaystyle(D_{t}u(0),\psi)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}+((u_{0},\psi))+[u_{0}\cdot\mathcal{N},\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}]_{\ell}-(p_{0},\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}\psi)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}+(Ru_{0},\psi)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}=\mathscr{F}_{1}(\psi)-(\xi(0),\psi\cdot\mathcal{N})_{1,\Sigma}

for each
\psi\in\mathcal{W}(0)
.

So, the pressureless weak solutions to (
1.7
) at
t=0
with
j=0
are supposed to satisfy the equation

(B.59)

\displaystyle(D_{t}u(0),\psi)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}+((u_{0},\psi))+[u_{0}\cdot\mathcal{N},\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}]_{\ell}+(Ru_{0},\psi)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}=\mathscr{F}_{1}(\psi)-(\xi(0),\psi\cdot\mathcal{N})_{1,\Sigma}

for each
\psi\in\mathcal{W}_{\sigma}(0)
.
The pressureless weak formulation at
t=0
with
j=1
is expressed as

(B.60)

\displaystyle(D_{t}^{2}u(0),\psi)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}+((D_{t}u(0),\psi))+[D_{t}u(0)\cdot\mathcal{N}(0),\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}(0)]_{\ell}-(\partial_{t}p(0),\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}_{0}}\psi)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}

\displaystyle+2(RD_{t}u(0),\psi)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}=\mathscr{F}_{2}(\psi)-(\partial_{t}\xi(0),\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}(0))_{1,\Sigma}

for each
\psi\in\mathcal{W}(0)
.
So, the pressureless weak solutions to (
1.7
) at
t=0
with
j=1
satisfy

(B.61)

\displaystyle(D_{t}^{2}u(0),\psi)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}+((D_{t}u(0),\psi))+[D_{t}u(0)\cdot\mathcal{N}(0),\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}(0)]_{\ell}

\displaystyle+2(RD_{t}u(0),\psi)_{\mathcal{H}^{0}}=\mathscr{F}_{2}(\psi)-(u_{0}\cdot\mathcal{N}(0),\psi\cdot\mathcal{N}(0))_{1,\Sigma}

for each
\psi\in\mathcal{W}_{\sigma}(0)
.

B.2.
Initial Forcing Terms for Contraction

We give the forcing terms in the system of (

). For
k=0
,

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}^{1}_{0}

\displaystyle=-(R(0)^{1}-R(0)^{2})v_{0}^{2}-\nabla_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}-\mathcal{A}_{0}^{2}}q_{0}^{2}+\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}-\mathcal{A}_{0}^{2}}\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}}v_{0}^{2}+\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{2}}\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}-\mathcal{A}_{0}^{2}}v_{0}^{2}+\partial_{t}F^{1}(\eta^{1}_{0},u^{1}_{0})-\partial_{t}F^{1}(\eta^{2}_{0},u^{2}_{0}),

\displaystyle+\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}(0)(\partial_{t}K^{1}(0)-\partial_{t}K^{2}(0))W\partial_{2}v^{1}_{0}+\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}(0)\partial_{t}K^{2}(0)W\partial_{2}v_{0}+\partial_{t}\bar{\eta}^{2}(0)K^{2}(0)W\partial_{2}D_{t}v(0)

\displaystyle-D_{t}v(0)\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{1}_{0}}v^{1}_{0}-D_{t}v^{2}(0)\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{1}_{0}-\mathcal{A}^{2}_{0}}v^{1}_{0}-D_{t}v^{2}(0)\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{2}_{0}}v_{0}-R^{1}(0)v_{0}\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{1}_{0}}v^{1}_{0}-R^{2}(0)v^{2}_{0}\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{1}_{0}-\mathcal{A}^{2}_{0}}v^{1}_{0}

\displaystyle-R^{2}(0)v^{2}_{0}\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{2}_{0}}v_{0}-[R^{1}(0)-R^{2}(0)]v^{2}_{0}\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{1}_{0}}v^{1}_{0}-v_{0}\cdot\nabla_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}(0)}v^{1}_{0}-v^{2}_{0}\cdot\nabla_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}(0)-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}(0)}v^{1}_{0}

\displaystyle-v^{2}_{0}\cdot\nabla_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}(0)}v_{0}-v_{0}\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{1}(0)}D_{t}v^{1}(0)-v^{2}_{0}\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{1}(0)-\mathcal{A}^{2}(0)}D_{t}v^{1}(0)-u^{2}_{0}\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{2}(0)}D_{t}v(0)

\displaystyle-v_{0}\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{1}_{0}}(R^{1}(0)v^{1}_{0})-v^{2}_{0}\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{1}_{0}-\mathcal{A}^{2}_{0}}(R^{1}(0)v^{1}_{0})-u^{2}_{0}\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{2}_{0}}(R^{1}(0)v_{0})-v^{2}_{0}\cdot\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{1}_{0}}[R^{1}(0)-R^{2}(0)]v^{2}_{0}.

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}^{2}_{1}

\displaystyle=-\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}-\mathcal{A}_{0}^{2}}D_{t}u^{2}(0),

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}^{3}_{1}

\displaystyle=\partial_{z}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}(0))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta^{1}(0)-\partial_{z}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2}(0))\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta^{2}(0),

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}^{4}_{1}

\displaystyle+\mu\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}-\mathcal{A}_{0}^{2}}R^{2}(0)u^{2}_{0}\mathcal{N}^{2}(0)+\mu\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}}R^{2}(0)u^{2}_{0}(\mathcal{N}^{1}(0)-\mathcal{N}^{2}(0))+\mu\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}(0)}u_{0}\mathcal{N}^{1}(0)

\displaystyle+\mu\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}(0)-\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{2}(0)}u^{2}_{0}\mathcal{N}^{2}(0)+\mu\mathbb{D}_{\partial_{t}\mathcal{A}^{1}(0)}u^{2}_{0}(\mathcal{N}^{1}(0)-\mathcal{N}^{2}(0))-S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}_{0}}(p_{0},u_{0})\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}(0)

\displaystyle-S_{\mathcal{A}^{1}_{0}}(p^{2}_{0},u^{2}_{0})[\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}(0)-\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{2}(0)]+\mu\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}-\mathcal{A}_{0}^{2}}u^{2}_{0}\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{2}(0)

\displaystyle+\bigg[g\xi^{2}(0)-\sigma\partial_{1}\bigg(\frac{\partial_{1}\xi^{2}(0)}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\bigg)-\partial_{1}\mathcal{R}\bigg][\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}(0)-\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{2}(0)]

\displaystyle+\bigg[g\xi_{0}-\sigma\partial_{1}\bigg(\frac{\partial_{1}\xi_{0}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\bigg)-\partial_{1}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{1}_{0}+\partial_{1}\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{2}_{0})\bigg]\partial_{t}\mathcal{N}^{1}(0),

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}^{5}_{1}

\displaystyle=\mu\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}-\mathcal{A}_{0}^{2}}D_{t}u^{2}(0)\nu\cdot\tau+\mu\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}}R^{1}(0)u_{0}\nu\cdot\tau+\mu\mathbb{D}_{\mathcal{A}_{0}^{1}}[R^{1}(0)-R^{2}(0)]u^{2}_{0}\nu\cdot\tau

\displaystyle=-D_{t}u^{2}(0)\cdot[\mathcal{N}^{1}(0)-\mathcal{N}^{1}(0)],

\displaystyle\mathfrak{g}^{7}_{1}

\displaystyle=\kappa D_{t}u^{2}(0)\cdot[\mathcal{N}^{1}(0)-\mathcal{N}^{2}(0)](\pm\ell)+\kappa\partial_{t}\hat{\mathscr{W}}(\partial_{t}\eta^{1})(\pm\ell)-\kappa\partial_{t}\hat{\mathscr{W}}(\partial_{t}\eta^{2})(\pm\ell).

Appendix C
Construction of Smooth Sequences

In this section, we construct the approximating sequence in the linear estimate.
Recall that in equation (
1.28
), we only smooth the functions on the free surface.

We construct the extension map

E:H^{s}(-\ell,\ell)\to H^{s}(\mathbb{R})
,
explicitly as follows

(Eu):=\begin{cases}u(x)~~~~~&|x|\leq\ell,\\
\eta(x-\ell)(6u(2\ell-x)-8u(3\ell-2x)+6u(4\ell-3x))~~~~~~&x>\ell,\\
\eta(x+\ell)(6u(-2\ell-x)-8u(-3\ell-2x)+6u(-4\ell-3x))~~~~~&x<-\ell,\end{cases}

where the function
\eta\in C_{c}^{\infty}(-\frac{\ell}{8},\frac{\ell}{8})
satisfying

\eta(x)=1\ \text{for}\ |x|\leq\frac{\ell}{24}
,

\eta(x)=0~\operatorname{for}~|x|\geq\frac{\ell}{12}
,
and

\eta(x)\in[0,1]
for any
x
.
The definition of
E
above provides a bounded extension operator that is independent of time
t
for all
0\leq s\leq 3
.

Let
K(x)
be a smooth function defined by

K(x):=\begin{cases}C_{K}\exp(\frac{1}{x^{2}-1})\quad\quad\quad\quad&|x|<1,\\
0\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad&|x|\geq 1.\end{cases}

where
C_{K}
is a constant such that
\int_{\mathbb{R}}K(x)dx=1
.

Using the extension operator and mollifier introduced above, we define the following smooth approximating sequence for any
T>0
and each function
f(t,x)\in L_{t}^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\Sigma))

(C.1)

\displaystyle{}\begin{aligned} f^{\varepsilon}(t,x):=\frac{C_{K}}{\varepsilon}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}K\big(\frac{x-y}{\varepsilon}\big)Ef(t,y)dy.\end{aligned}

By the standard properties of mollification and the boundedness of the extension operator, if
f\in L^{p}(0,T;W^{i,q}(\Sigma))
for any
p,q\in[1,\infty]
and
i\in(1,\infty)
, we have the following convergence results:

(C.2)

\displaystyle{}\begin{aligned} \lim_{\varepsilon\rightarrow 0}f^{\varepsilon}(t,x)=f(t,x)~~\operatorname{in}~~L^{p}(0,T;W^{i,q}(\Sigma)).\end{aligned}

Moreover, the following regularity properties hold for the smooth modification function
f^{\varepsilon}

(C.3)

\displaystyle{}\begin{aligned} f^{\varepsilon}\in&L_{t}^{\infty}(0,T;C^{\infty}(\Sigma)),\end{aligned}

Appendix D
Initial Data for the Linear System

D.1.
Initial Data for System with Smooth Surface Function
\eta^{n}

For any prescribed
n
, we establish the initial data for
(v,\xi,q)
which is a solution to the following system

(D.1)

{}\begin{cases}\partial_{t}^{2}v+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(v,q)=F^{1}(u,p,\eta)~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}D_{t}v=0~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(\partial_{t}q,\partial_{t}v)\mathcal{N}^{n}=g\xi_{d}\mathcal{N}^{n}-\sigma\partial_{1}(\frac{1}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\partial_{1}\xi_{d})\mathcal{N}^{n}+\sigma\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)(\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi))\mathcal{N}^{n}\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+F^{4}(u,p,\eta)~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
(S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(\partial_{t}q,\partial_{t}v)\nu-\beta\partial_{t}v)\cdot\tau=F^{5}(u,\eta,p)~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
\partial_{t}v\cdot\nu=0~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
\partial_{t}^{2}\xi=\partial_{t}v\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}+u_{1}\cdot\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma\\
\sigma(\mp\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\pm\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi)(\pm\ell))(\pm\ell)=\kappa(v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n})(\pm\ell)-{F}^{7}.\end{cases}

For any prescribed
n
, let
\partial_{t}^{2}\xi(0)=\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0)
and
D_{t}^{2}v(0)=D_{t}^{2}u(0)
which are the initial functions constructed in Appendix
B
. We construct the initial conditions by solving the following system. Let
(v(0),\xi(0),q(0))
, and
(D_{t}v(0),\partial_{t}\xi(0),\partial_{t}q(0))
be the solution to the following systems

(D.2)

\displaystyle{}\begin{cases}D_{t}v_{0}+R^{n}(0)v_{0}+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(v_{0},q_{0})=F^{1,0}(u_{0},p_{0},\eta_{0})~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}v_{0}=0~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(q_{0},v_{0})\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)=g\xi_{0}\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)-\sigma\partial_{1}(\frac{1}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\partial_{1}\xi_{0})\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)+\sigma\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta_{0}))\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
(S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(q_{0},v_{0})\nu-\beta v_{0})\cdot\tau=0~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
v_{0}\cdot\nu=0~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
\partial_{t}\xi_{0}=v_{0}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
\sigma(\mp\frac{\partial_{1}\xi_{0}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\pm\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta_{0})\partial_{1}\xi_{0})(\pm\ell))(\pm\ell)=\kappa(v_{0}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}(0))(\pm\ell)-{F}^{7,0},\end{cases}

and

(D.3)

\displaystyle{}\begin{cases}D_{t}^{2}v_{0}+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(D_{t}v_{0}+R^{n}(0)v_{0},\partial_{t}q_{0})\\
=\partial_{t}F^{1,0}(u_{0},p_{0},\eta_{0})+\mathfrak{G}^{1}(u_{0},p_{0})-R^{n}(0)D_{t}v_{0}-\partial_{t}(R^{n}(0)v_{0})~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}D_{t}v_{0}=0~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(\partial_{t}q_{0},D_{t}v_{0}+R^{n}(0)v_{0})\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)=g\partial_{t}\xi_{0}\mathcal{N}^{n}-\sigma\partial_{1}(\frac{1}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{0})\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)\\
~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~+\sigma\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta_{0})(\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{0}))\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)+\mathfrak{G}^{4}(u_{0},p_{0},\eta_{0})~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
(S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(\partial_{t}q_{0},D_{t}v_{0}+R^{n}(0)v_{0})\nu-\beta\partial_{t}v_{0})\cdot\tau=\mathfrak{G}^{5}(u_{0},p_{0},\eta_{0})~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
D_{t}v_{0}\cdot\nu=0~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
\partial_{t}^{2}\xi_{0}=D_{t}v_{0}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)+R^{n}(0)v_{0}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)-u_{0}\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{0}-\frac{1}{2\ell}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(R^{n}(0)v_{0}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)-u_{0}\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{0})~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
\sigma(\mp\frac{\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{0}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\pm\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta_{0})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{0})ds)(\pm\ell)=\kappa(D_{t}v_{0}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}(0))(\pm\ell)-\partial_{t}{F}^{7,0}.\end{cases}

Remark: Because the initial data of the smooth sequence do not satisfy the original compatibility conditions, we add a new integral to the kinematic boundary condition so that the modified system remains compatible.
This integral converges to
0
when
n,k\rightarrow\infty

Following the arguments in Appendix
B
, given
\eta_{0},u_{0},p_{0},\eta_{0}^{n},\partial_{t}\eta_{0}^{n},\partial_{t}^{2}\eta_{0}^{n}
. the system above has a solution. We state the following theorem with respect to this result.

Theorem D.1
.

(u_{0},\eta_{0},p_{0})
are as constructed in Appendix
B
.
\eta_{0}^{n},\partial_{t}\eta_{0}^{n},\partial_{t}^{2}\eta_{0}^{n}
are the initial data of
\eta^{n},\partial_{t}\eta^{n},\partial_{t}^{2}\eta^{n}
as constructed in Appendix
C
. Then for any given
(D_{t}^{i}u_{0},\partial_{t}^{i}\eta_{0},\partial_{t}^{i}p_{0})
with
i=0,1,2
, and
\eta_{0}^{n},\partial_{t}\eta_{0}^{n},\partial_{t}^{2}\eta_{0}^{n}
. Equation (
D.2
) and equation (
D.3
) admits a solution
(v^{n}_{0},\xi_{0}^{n},q_{0}^{n},D_{t}v_{0}^{n},\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n},\partial_{t}q_{0}^{n})
. Moreover, when
n\rightarrow\infty
, it holds that

D_{t}^{i}v_{0}^{n}\rightarrow D_{t}^{i}u_{0}~~~\operatorname{in}~~~W^{2,q_{+}},\ \partial_{t}^{i}\xi_{0}^{n}\rightarrow\partial_{t}^{i}\eta_{0}~~~\operatorname{in}~~~W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}},\ \partial_{t}^{i}q_{0}^{n}\rightarrow\partial_{t}^{i}q_{0}~~~\operatorname{in}~~~W^{1,q_{+}}

for
i=0,1
.

Proof.

To prove existence, it suffices to verify the compatibility conditions. Applying the same construction as in Appendix
B
then yields existence.

Since
\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}^{i}\xi^{n}(0)=0
, integrating both sides of the kinematic boundary condition in (
D.2
), we obtain

\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}v^{n}_{0}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)=\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}\xi^{n}(0)=0

Moreover,
v_{0}^{n}\cdot\nu=0
on
\Sigma_{s}
implies that

\int_{\Sigma_{s}}v_{0}^{n}\cdot\nu=0
.
Therefore,

\int_{\Omega}\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}v^{n}_{0}=0.

This guarantees the compatibility for the zero-order equation.

We now show the compatibility condition for the first-order system (
D.3
). From the kinematic boundary condition in (
D.3
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}D_{t}v^{n}_{0}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)

\displaystyle=\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}^{2}\xi^{n}_{0}-\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(R^{n}(0)v^{n}_{0}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)+u_{0}\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi^{n}_{0})+\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\frac{1}{2\ell}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(R^{n}(0)v^{n}_{0}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)+u^{n}_{0}\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi^{n}_{0})

\displaystyle=\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}\partial_{t}^{2}\xi^{n}_{0}=0.

Since
D_{t}v^{n}_{0}\cdot\nu=0
on
\Sigma_{s}
, we have

\int_{\Omega}\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}D_{t}v^{n}_{0}=\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}D_{t}v_{0}^{n}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)+\int_{\Sigma_{s}}D_{t}v_{0}^{n}\cdot\nu=0,

which guarantees the compatibility for the first-order equation.

We now prove the convergence results. By the elliptic theory developed in
[
10
]
, we have

\displaystyle\|v^{n}_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|\xi^{n}_{0}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}+\|q^{n}_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}v^{n}_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}\xi^{n}_{0}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}q^{n}_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u_{0}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}+\|\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}+\|q_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}u_{0}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}q_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}^{2}\eta_{0}\|_{W^{2-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}u_{0}\|_{L^{2}}.

Subtracting (
D.2
), (
D.3
) by (
1.7
) and (
1.17
), respectively, we obtain the following system for
(v^{n}(0)-u(0),\xi^{n}(0)-\eta(0),q^{n}(0)-p(0),D_{t}v^{n}-D_{t}u(0),\partial_{t}\xi^{n}(0)-\partial_{t}\eta(0),\partial_{t}q^{n}(0)-\partial_{t}p(0))

(D.4)

\displaystyle{}\begin{cases}D_{t}(v_{0}^{n}-u_{0})+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}(0)}S_{\mathcal{A}(0)}(v_{0}^{n}-u_{0},q_{0}^{n}-p_{0})+R(0)(v_{0}^{n}-v_{0})\\
\qquad\qquad\qquad=\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}(0)}S_{\mathcal{A}(0)-\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(v_{0}^{n},q_{0}^{n})+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}(0)-\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(v_{0}^{n},q_{0}^{n})~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}(0)}(v_{0}^{n}-u_{0})=\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}(0)-\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}v_{0}^{n}~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
S_{\mathcal{A}(0)}(q_{0}^{n}-p_{0},v_{0}^{n}-u_{0})\mathcal{N}(0)=g(\xi^{n}(0)-\eta_{0})\mathcal{N}(0)-\sigma\partial_{1}(\frac{1}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\partial_{1}(\xi^{n}(0)-\eta_{0}))(\mathcal{N}(0))\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sigma\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta_{0}))(\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)-\mathcal{N}(0))+S_{\mathcal{A}(0)-\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(q_{0}^{n},v_{0}^{n})\mathcal{N}(0)\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(q_{0}^{n},v_{0}^{n})(\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)-\mathcal{N}(0))-\mathcal{K}(\xi_{0}^{n})(\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)-\mathcal{N}(0))~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
(S_{\mathcal{A}(0)}(q_{0}^{n}-p_{0},v_{0}^{n}-u_{0})\nu-\beta(v_{0}^{n}-u_{0}))\cdot\tau=S_{\mathcal{A}(0)-\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(q_{0}^{n},v_{0}^{n})~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
(v_{0}^{n}-u_{0})\cdot\nu=0~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n}-\partial_{t}\eta_{0}=(v_{0}^{n}-u_{0})\cdot\mathcal{N}(0)+v_{0}^{n}(\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)-\mathcal{N}(0))~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
\sigma(\mp\frac{\partial_{1}(\xi_{0}^{n}-\eta_{0})}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\pm\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta_{0})\partial_{1}(\xi_{0}^{n}-\eta_{0}))ds)(\pm\ell)\\
=\kappa((v_{0}^{n}-u_{0})\cdot\mathcal{N}(0))(\pm\ell)+\kappa v_{0}^{n}\cdot(\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)-\mathcal{N}(0)),\end{cases}

and

(D.5)

\displaystyle{}\begin{cases}\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}(0)}S_{\mathcal{A}(0)}(D_{t}(v_{0}^{n}-u_{0})+R(0)(v_{0}^{n}-u_{0}),\partial_{t}(q_{0}^{n}-p_{0}))=-R^{n}(0)(D_{t}v_{0}^{n}-D_{t}u_{0})\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-(R(0)-R^{n}(0))(D_{t}v_{0}^{n})-\partial_{t}(R^{n}(0)(v_{0}^{n}-u_{0}))-\partial_{t}((R(0)-R^{n}(0))v_{0}^{n})+R(0)(v_{0}^{n}),\partial_{t}(q_{0}^{n}))\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}(0)}S_{\mathcal{A}(0)-\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(D_{t}(v_{0}^{n})+R(0)(v_{0}^{n}),\partial_{t}(q_{0}^{n}))+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}(0)-\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(D_{t}(v_{0}^{n})~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}(0)}(D_{t}v_{0}^{n}-D_{t}u_{0})=\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}(0)-\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(D_{t}v_{0}^{n})~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
S_{\mathcal{A}(0)}(\partial_{t}q_{0}^{n}-\partial_{t}p_{0},(D_{t}v_{0}^{n}-D_{t}u_{0})+R(0)(v_{0}^{n}-u_{0}))\mathcal{N}(0)=g(\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n}-\partial_{t}\eta_{0})\mathcal{N}(0)\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-\sigma\partial_{1}(\frac{1}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}}\partial_{1}(\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n}-\partial_{t}\eta_{0}))\mathcal{N}(0)+\sigma\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta_{0})(\partial_{1}(\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n}-\partial_{t}\eta_{0})))\mathcal{N}(0)\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+S_{\mathcal{A}(0)}(\partial_{t}q_{0}^{n},D_{t}v_{0}^{n}+R(v_{0}^{n}))(\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)-\mathcal{N}(0))\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)-\mathcal{A}(0)}(\partial_{t}q_{0}^{n},D_{t}v_{0}^{n}+R(0)(v_{0}^{n}))(\mathcal{N}^{n}(0))+S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(0,(R^{n}(0)-R(0))(v_{0}^{n}))(\mathcal{N}^{n}(0))\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+(\mathcal{K}(\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n})+\sigma\partial_{1}(\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta)\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n}))(\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)-\mathcal{N}(0))~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
(S_{\mathcal{A}(0)}(\partial_{t}q_{0}^{n}-\partial_{t}p_{0},(D_{t}v_{0}^{n}-D_{t}u_{0})+R(0)(v_{0}^{n}-u_{0}))\nu-\beta(D_{t}v_{0}-D_{t}u_{0}))\cdot\tau\\
=S_{\mathcal{A}(0)-\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(\partial_{t}q_{0}^{n},D_{t}v_{0}^{n}+Rv_{0}^{n})\cdot\tau+S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(0,(R^{n}-R)v_{0}^{n})\cdot\tau~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
(D_{t}v_{0}^{n}-D_{t}u_{0})\cdot\nu=0~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
(D_{t}v_{0}^{n}-D_{t}u_{0})\cdot\mathcal{N}(0)+R(0)(v_{0}^{n}-u_{0})\cdot\mathcal{N}(0)\\
-u_{0}\partial_{1}(\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n}-\partial_{t}\eta_{0})-{\frac{1}{2\ell}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(R(0)(v_{0}^{n}-v_{0})\cdot\mathcal{N}(0)-u_{0}\partial_{1}(\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n}-\partial_{t}\eta_{0}))}\\
=D_{t}v_{0}^{n}\cdot(\mathcal{N}(0)-\mathcal{N}^{n}(0))+(R^{n}(0))v_{0}^{n}\cdot\mathcal{N}(0)+(R(0)-R^{n}(0))v_{0}^{n}\cdot(\mathcal{N}(0)-\mathcal{N}^{n}(0))\\
-\frac{1}{2\ell}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(R(0)-R^{n}(0))v_{0}^{n}\cdot\mathcal{N}(0)+{R}^{n}(0)v_{0}^{n}\cdot(\mathcal{N}(0)-\mathcal{N}^{n}(0))~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
\sigma(\mp\frac{\partial_{1}(\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n}-\partial_{t}\eta_{0})}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\pm\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta_{0})\partial_{1}(\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n}-\partial_{t}\eta_{0}))(\pm\ell))(\pm\ell)=\kappa((D_{t}v_{0}^{n}-D_{t}u_{0})\cdot\mathcal{N}(0))(\pm\ell).\end{cases}

∎

By the similar elliptic estimate used in Appendix
B
, we have the following estimate

\displaystyle\|v_{0}^{n}-u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|\xi_{0}^{n}-\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}+\|q_{0}^{n}-p_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}

\displaystyle+\|D_{t}v_{0}^{n}-D_{t}u_{0}\|_{W^{2,q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n}-\partial_{t}\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}q_{0}^{n}-\partial_{t}p_{0}\|_{W^{1,q_{+}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\eta_{0}^{n}-\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}+\|\partial_{t}\eta_{0}^{n}-\partial_{t}\eta_{0}\|_{W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}}}.

This directly implies the convergence results as
n\rightarrow\infty
.

D.2.
Initial Data for Smooth Given Data Problem with
\eta^{k},\eta^{n},v_{l}^{k},\xi_{l}^{k},u^{k}

In this subsection, we consider the initial data for the following system

(D.6)

\begin{cases}\partial_{t}v_{d}+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(v_{d},q_{d})+\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}\nabla_{\mathcal{A}}(R^{n}v_{l})+\partial_{t}(R^{n}v_{l})=F^{1}(u,p,\eta)~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}v_{d}=0~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(q_{d},v_{d})\mathcal{N}^{n}+\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(R^{n}v_{l})\mathcal{N}^{n}=g\xi_{d}\mathcal{N}^{n}-\sigma\partial_{1}(\frac{\partial_{1}\xi_{d0}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}})\mathcal{N}^{n}+\partial_{1}I_{1}^{n}\mathcal{N}^{n}\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sigma\partial_{1}(\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})(\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{l}^{k})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta^{k})\mathcal{N}^{n}+\sigma\partial_{1}(\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d})\mathcal{N}^{n}\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+F^{4}(u,p,\eta)~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
(S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(q_{d},v_{d})\nu+\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{n}}(R^{n}v_{l})\nu-\beta v_{d})\cdot\tau=F^{5}(u,\eta,p)~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
v_{d}\cdot\nu=0~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
\partial_{t}\xi_{d}=v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}+(R^{n}v_{l}^{k})\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}+\int_{0}^{t}(\partial_{t}u_{1}^{k}\cdot\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{l}^{k}+u_{1}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\xi_{d})+I_{2}^{n}~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
\sigma(\mp\frac{\partial_{1}\xi_{d}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\pm\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{d})(\pm\ell)\pm\int_{0}^{t}\mathcal{R}_{z}(\partial_{1}\zeta_{0},\partial_{1}\eta^{k})(\partial_{t}\partial_{1}\xi_{l}^{k}\partial_{1}\partial_{t}\eta^{k})\pm I_{1}^{n})\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad=\kappa(v_{d}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n})(\pm\ell)-{F}^{7},\end{cases}

where
v_{l}^{k},\eta^{k},\xi_{l}^{k},u^{k},\eta^{n}
are as defined in section
C
. For simplicity, we give the following definition

f^{k}(x):=\frac{C_{K}}{\frac{1}{k}}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}K(\frac{x-y}{\frac{1}{k}})f(y)dy.

We construct the initial data for
(v_{d},\xi_{d},q_{d})
via solving the following system

(D.7)

{}\begin{cases}D_{t}v_{d0}+R^{n}(0)v_{d0}+\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(v_{d0},q_{d0})+\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(R^{n}(0)v_{l0})+\partial_{t}(R^{n}(0)v_{l0})\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad~=F^{1}(u_{0},p_{0},\eta_{0})~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
\operatorname{div}_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}v_{d0}=0~~~&\operatorname{in}~~\Omega,\\
S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(q_{d0},v_{d0})\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)+\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(R^{n}(0)v_{l0})\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)=g\xi_{d0}\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)-\sigma\partial_{1}(\frac{\partial_{1}\xi_{d0}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{3/2}})\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)\\
\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~+\partial_{1}I_{1}^{n,k}\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)+F^{4}(u_{0},p_{0},\eta_{0})~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
(S_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(q_{d0},v_{d0})\nu+\nabla_{\mathcal{A}^{n}(0)}(R^{n}(0)v_{l0})\nu-\beta v_{d0})\cdot\tau=F^{5}(u_{0},\eta_{0},p_{0})~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
v_{d0}\cdot\nu=0~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma_{s},\\
\partial_{t}\xi_{d0}=v_{d0}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)+(R^{n}(0)v_{l0}^{k})\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}(0)+I_{2}^{n,k}-\frac{1}{2\ell}\int_{-\ell}^{\ell}(I^{n,k}_{2}+(R^{n}v_{l0}^{k})\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}(0))~~~&\operatorname{on}~~\Sigma,\\
\sigma(\mp\frac{\partial_{1}\xi_{d0}}{(1+|\partial_{1}\zeta_{0}|^{2})^{\frac{3}{2}}}\pm I_{1}^{n,k})=\kappa(v_{d0}\cdot\mathcal{N}^{n}(0))(\pm\ell)-{F}^{7}(u_{0},q_{0},\eta_{0}),\end{cases}

where

\displaystyle u^{k}(0)=(u_{0})^{k},~~~D_{t}v_{d0}=D_{t}^{2}u(0),~~~~~~\partial_{t}\xi_{d0}=\partial_{t}^{2}\eta(0),

\displaystyle v_{l0}^{k}=(v^{n}(0))^{k},~~~~v_{l0}=(v^{n}(0)),~~~\partial_{t}v_{l0}=(\partial_{t}v^{n}(0)),~~~\eta^{k}(0)=(\eta_{0})^{k}.

By an elliptic argument identical to that in Appendix
B
, there exists a unique solution
(v_{d0}^{n,k},\xi^{n,k}_{d0},q^{n,k}_{d0})
of system (
D.7
). Moreover, when
k\rightarrow+\infty
, we have

\displaystyle v_{d0}^{n,k}\rightarrow D_{t}v_{0}^{n}~~~\operatorname{in}~~~W^{2,q_{+}},\ \xi_{d0}^{n,k}\rightarrow\partial_{t}\xi_{0}^{n}~~~\operatorname{in}~~~W^{3-\frac{1}{q_{+}},q_{+}},\ q_{d0}^{n,k}\rightarrow\partial_{t}q_{0}^{n}~~~\operatorname{in}~~~W^{1,q_{+}}.
